[AFE_SRC_PARTS] Original Coef, CRT comp, primary parts, c2, Sb and Tb
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/HeightAndVanishing/DirichletPrimeToLPartMulLPowerPart.lean#L1-L237
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CharacterLevelBounds.ShaFamMono
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.ExistsO

noncomputable section

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual Formalism.HC ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.GaloisCohomology UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.Top

namespace UpperTheory.V.HV

structure Coef (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] where
  O : Subalgebra ℤ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l)
  dvr : IsDiscreteValuationRing O
  fin : Module.Finite ℤ_[l] O

attribute [instance] Coef.dvr Coef.fin

instance factPrimes (p : Nat.Primes) : Fact (p : ℕ).Prime := ⟨p.2⟩

section Parts

variable {Γ : Type} [Group Γ]

def lPart (l : ℕ) (x : Γ) : Γ := x ^ (ordCompl[l] (orderOf x)) ^ Nat.totient (ordProj[l] (orderOf x))

def lPrime (l : ℕ) (x : Γ) : Γ := x * (lPart l x)⁻¹

theorem lPrime_mul_lPart (l : ℕ) (x : Γ) : lPrime l x * lPart l x = x := inv_mul_cancel_right _ _

end Parts

theorem coprime_ordProj_ordCompl (r N : ℕ) : (ordProj[r] N).Coprime (ordCompl[r] N) := by
  by_cases hr : r.Prime
  · by_cases hN : N = 0
    · subst hN
      simp
    · exact Nat.Coprime.pow_left _ (Nat.coprime_ordCompl hr hN)
  · rw [Nat.factorization_eq_zero_of_not_prime N hr, pow_zero]
    exact Nat.coprime_one_left _

def idem (r N : ℕ) : ZMod N := ((ordCompl[r] N ^ Nat.totient (ordProj[r] N) : ℕ) : ZMod N)

theorem idem_mul_idem (r N : ℕ) : idem r N * idem r N = idem r N := by
  unfold idem
  rw [← Nat.cast_mul]
  have hcop : (ordCompl[r] N).Coprime (ordProj[r] N) := (coprime_ordProj_ordCompl r N).symm
  have hapos : 0 < ordProj[r] N := Nat.ordProj_pos N r
  have he1 : ordCompl[r] N ^ Nat.totient (ordProj[r] N) ≡ 1 [MOD ordProj[r] N] :=
    Nat.ModEq.pow_totient hcop
  have hdvd : ordCompl[r] N ∣ ordCompl[r] N ^ Nat.totient (ordProj[r] N) :=
    dvd_pow_self _ (Nat.totient_pos.mpr hapos).ne'
  have hA : ordCompl[r] N ^ Nat.totient (ordProj[r] N) * ordCompl[r] N ^ Nat.totient (ordProj[r] N) ≡
      ordCompl[r] N ^ Nat.totient (ordProj[r] N) [MOD ordProj[r] N] := by
    refine (he1.mul he1).trans ?_
    rw [one_mul]
    exact he1.symm
  have hC : ordCompl[r] N ^ Nat.totient (ordProj[r] N) * ordCompl[r] N ^ Nat.totient (ordProj[r] N) ≡
      ordCompl[r] N ^ Nat.totient (ordProj[r] N) [MOD ordCompl[r] N] :=
    (Nat.modEq_zero_iff_dvd.mpr (dvd_mul_of_dvd_left hdvd _)).trans
      (Nat.modEq_zero_iff_dvd.mpr hdvd).symm
  have key := (Nat.modEq_and_modEq_iff_modEq_mul hcop.symm).mp ⟨hA, hC⟩
  rw [Nat.ordProj_mul_ordCompl_eq_self N r] at key
  exact (ZMod.natCast_eq_natCast_iff _ _ _).mpr key

def projZ (r N : ℕ) : ZMod N →* ZMod N where
  toFun u := idem r N * u + (1 - idem r N)
  map_one' := by ring
  map_mul' u v := by linear_combination (-(u * v) + u + v - 1) * idem_mul_idem r N

def proj (r N : ℕ) : (ZMod N)ˣ →* (ZMod N)ˣ := Units.map (projZ r N)

section Data

variable {O : Type} [CommRing O] {N : ℕ}

def comp (r : ℕ) (χ : DirichletCharacter O N) : DirichletCharacter O N :=
  MulChar.ofUnitHom (χ.toUnitHom.comp (proj r N))

def kap (l : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : DirichletCharacter O N := lPart l ψ

def th0 (l : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : DirichletCharacter O N := lPrime l ψ

def psi2 (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : DirichletCharacter O N := comp 2 ψ

def th1 (l : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : DirichletCharacter O N := th0 l ψ * (comp 2 (th0 l ψ))⁻¹

def chiP (l : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : DirichletCharacter O N := th0 l ψ * (comp l (th0 l ψ))⁻¹

def kapL (l : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : DirichletCharacter O N := comp l (kap l ψ)

def kapB (l : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : DirichletCharacter O N := kap l ψ * (kapL l ψ)⁻¹

def c2 (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : ℕ := (psi2 ψ).conductor.factorization 2

open Classical in

def Sb (l : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : Finset ℕ := N.primeFactors.filter fun p => p ≠ l ∧ comp p (kap l ψ) ≠ 1

def Tb (l : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : Finset ℕ := insert l (Sb l ψ)

def f0 (l : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : ℕ := orderOf (l : ZMod (Nat.lcm 4 (orderOf (th1 l ψ))))

def n0 (l : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : ℕ := (l ^ f0 l ψ - 1).factorization 2

def PsiEven (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : Prop := ψ (-1) = (-1) ^ e

def EV (l : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : Prop := Even (orderOf (chiP l ψ))

theorem dirichletPrimeToLPart_mul_lPowerPart (l : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : th0 l ψ * kap l ψ = ψ := lPrime_mul_lPart l ψ

end Data

section Len

variable (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) {N : ℕ} [NeZero N]

def Vlen (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) : ℕ∞ := Chr.V' l N e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)

def Vn (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) : ℝ := Chr.nrm l c.O (Vlen Ω c e ψ)

def VLe (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (B : ℝ) : Prop := Vlen Ω c e ψ ≠ ⊤ ∧ Vn Ω c e ψ * Real.log l ≤ B

def kerFam {O : Type} [CommRing O] (l : ℕ) (θ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) : Set (Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) :=
  {D | (∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧ D = Dec (F := ℚ) A) ∨
    ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧ D = Ine (F := ℚ) A ⊓ θ.ker}

def VkerLen (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) : ℕ∞ :=
  Module.length c.O (LocSel (Chr.rho l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) ⊤ (kerFam Ω l (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)))

def hLen (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) : ℕ∞ :=
  ∑ᶠ (v : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ)) (_ : ((l * N : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) ∈ v.asIdeal),
    Chr.h0 l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) (Dec (F := ℚ) (placeOf Ω v))

def hn (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) : ℝ := Chr.nrm l c.O (hLen Ω c e ψ)

theorem Vn_nonneg (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) : 0 ≤ Vn Ω c e ψ := Chr.nrm_nonneg _ _

theorem VLe.mono {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} {B B' : ℝ} (h : VLe Ω c e ψ B) (hB : B ≤ B') : VLe Ω c e ψ B' :=
  ⟨h.1, h.2.trans hB⟩

def orb (H : Subgroup (ZMod N)ˣ) : Finset (DirichletCharacter c.O N) :=
  (Set.toFinite {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N | ψ.toUnitHom.ker = H}).toFinset

theorem mem_orb {H : Subgroup (ZMod N)ˣ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} : ψ ∈ orb c H ↔ ψ.toUnitHom.ker = H :=
  Set.Finite.mem_toFinset _

end Len

structure Host where
  chi : PChar
  ex : ℕ
  q1 : ℕ
  d : ℕ
  two_le : 2 ≤ ex
  pair : PrimePair chi.N q1 d

namespace Host

variable (h : Host)

abbrev q : ℕ := h.chi.N

abbrev o : ℕ := ordC h.chi

def N : ℕ := Nat.lcm (2 ^ (h.ex + 2)) h.q * (h.q1 * h.d)

theorem N_pos : 0 < h.N := by
  unfold N
  obtain ⟨hq1, -, hd, -, -⟩ := h.pair
  have hq : 0 < h.q := Nat.pos_of_ne_zero h.chi.ne.out
  exact Nat.mul_pos (Nat.lcm_pos (by positivity) hq) (Nat.mul_pos hq1.pos hd.pos)

instance : NeZero h.N := ⟨h.N_pos.ne'⟩

theorem two_pow_dvd : 2 ^ (h.ex + 2) ∣ h.N := (Nat.dvd_lcm_left _ _).mul_right _
theorem q_dvd : h.q ∣ h.N := (Nat.dvd_lcm_right _ _).mul_right _
theorem host_q1_dvd_N : h.q1 ∣ h.N := (dvd_mul_right _ _).mul_left _
theorem d_dvd : h.d ∣ h.N := (dvd_mul_left _ _).mul_left _

def H : Subgroup (ZMod h.N)ˣ :=
  (Subgroup.zpowers (-1)).comap (ZMod.unitsMap h.two_pow_dvd) ⊓ h.chi.χ.toUnitHom.ker.comap (ZMod.unitsMap h.q_dvd) ⊓
    (powMonoidHom 2).range.comap (ZMod.unitsMap h.host_q1_dvd_N) ⊓ (powMonoidHom 2).range.comap (ZMod.unitsMap h.d_dvd)

def IsChar {O : Type} [CommRing O] (ψ : DirichletCharacter O h.N) : Prop := h.H ≤ ψ.toUnitHom.ker

def Big (t : ℕ) : Prop :=
  2 * t + 1 < h.q1 ∧ 2 * t + 1 < h.d ∧ ∀ y ∈ [h.q, h.o, pK h.chi, hStar h.chi, pOne h.chi], y < h.q1 ∧ y < h.d

def X {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) (e : ℕ) : Finset (DirichletCharacter c.O h.N) :=
  (Set.toFinite (Chr.evenSet c.O h.H e)).toFinset

theorem mem_X {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O h.N} :
    ψ ∈ h.X c e ↔ h.IsChar ψ ∧ PsiEven e ψ := Set.Finite.mem_toFinset _

theorem host_q1_mod_four_eq_three : h.q1 % 4 = 3 := by
  obtain ⟨-, h8, -⟩ := h.pair
  omega
theorem d_mod : h.d % 4 = 3 := by
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, h16, -⟩ := h.pair
  omega
theorem firstPrime_ne_secondPrime : h.q1 ≠ h.d := by
  obtain ⟨-, h8, -, h16, -⟩ := h.pair
  omega

variable {O : Type} [CommRing O]

def R1 (l : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O h.N) : ℕ := 16 * h.o * (l * h.q) ^ 2 * 4 ^ n0 l ψ

def CaseI (l e : ℕ) : Prop := l ∉ Bchi h.chi ∧ ¬ l - 1 ∣ 2 * e

def CaseZ (l e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O h.N) : Prop := l ∉ Bchi h.chi ∧ l - 1 ∣ 2 * e ∧ ψ = 1

def CaseII (l e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O h.N) : Prop := 2 ≤ e ∧ EV l ψ ∧ (l ∈ Bchi h.chi ∨ l - 1 ∣ 2 * e)

def CaseIII (l e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O h.N) : Prop :=
  l ∉ Bchi h.chi ∧ l - 1 ∣ 2 * e ∧ Odd (orderOf ψ) ∧ ψ ≠ 1

def CaseIV (l e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O h.N) : Prop := l ∈ Bchi h.chi ∧ 2 ≤ e ∧ Odd (orderOf (chiP l ψ))

def CaseV (l e : ℕ) : Prop := l ∈ Bchi h.chi ∧ e = 1

def nII (l : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O h.N) : ℕ :=
  Nat.clog 2 (max (l * h.N * 4 ^ n0 l ψ) (2 ^ (c2 ψ + 6) * h.o * l ^ 3 * h.q ^ 2))

def nIII (l : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter O h.N) : ℕ :=
  Nat.clog 2 (max (l * h.q * pOne h.chi * 4 ^ n0 l ψ) (8 * l ^ 2))

end Host

theorem exists_coef (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (n : ℕ) [NeZero n] : ∃ c : Coef l, HasEnoughRootsOfUnity c.O n := by
  obtain ⟨O, a, b, c⟩ := existsO l n
  exact ⟨⟨O, a, b⟩, c⟩

example (χ : PChar) (h : (3 * 7).Coprime χ.N) : Host := ⟨χ, 2, 3, 7, le_rfl, by norm_num, by norm_num, by norm_num, by norm_num, h⟩

end UpperTheory.V.HV

end


[AFE_SRC_GD] Full original twelve-field GD antecedent
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/HeightAndVanishing/Counts.lean#L1-L77
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.HeightAndVanishing.DirichletPrimeToLPartMulLPowerPart

noncomputable section

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual Formalism.HC ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.GaloisCohomology UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.Top

namespace UpperTheory.V.HV

structure GD (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (e : ℕ) {O : Type} [CommRing O] {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : Prop where
  l_odd : l ≠ 2
  e_pos : 1 ≤ e
  N_even : Even N
  ord : ¬ l ∣ orderOf ψ
  ine : comp l ψ ^ 2 = 1
  par : PsiEven e ψ
  quad : Module.finrank ℚ K = 2
  imag : IsTotallyComplex K
  l_cop : ¬ l ∣ (discr K).natAbs * classNumber K
  cop : (discr K).natAbs.Coprime N
  eta : ¬ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker ⊓ (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (chiP l ψ)).ker ≤ galOf ℚ K Ω
  gen : ¬ l - 1 ∣ 2 * e

def CorG : Prop :=
  ∃ c5 : ℝ, ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    (e N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (H : Subgroup (ZMod N)ˣ) (p p' : ℕ), p.Prime → p'.Prime → p ≠ p' →
    ¬ p ∣ 30 * N * (discr K).natAbs → ¬ p' ∣ 30 * N * (discr K).natAbs →
    ∀ (c : ∀ l : Nat.Primes, Coef l) (Ls : Finset Nat.Primes), (∀ l ∈ Ls, ∃ ψ ∈ orb (c l) H, GD Ω K l e ψ) →
      (∀ l ∈ Ls, ∀ ψ ∈ orb (c l) H, Vlen Ω (c l) e ψ ≠ ⊤) ∧
      ∑ l ∈ Ls, Real.log (l : ℕ) * ∑ ψ ∈ orb (c l) H, Vn Ω (c l) e ψ ≤
        4 * c5 * (Nat.totient H.index) * ((discr K).natAbs : ℝ) ^ 5 * (N : ℝ) ^ 18 * (e : ℝ) ^ 7 * (Real.log p + Real.log p')

def ColumnFamilyFiltrationProperties : Prop :=
  ∃ C2 : ℝ, ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (h : Host) (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) (e : ℕ),
    l ≠ 2 → 1 ≤ e → ∀ ψ ∈ h.X c e, h.R1 l ψ ≤ 2 ^ c2 ψ →
      VLe Ω c e ψ (C2 * Nat.totient (orderOf ψ) * (h.N : ℝ) ^ 18 * (e : ℝ) ^ 7 * Real.log (Real.log ((l : ℝ) * h.N)))

def pZero (m : ℕ) : ℕ := sInf {p : ℕ | p.Prime ∧ 5 ≤ p ∧ ¬ p ∣ m}

def leSqrtOfDivSqGeOne (c4 : ℝ) (o l n' N p1 e : ℕ) : ℝ :=
  c4 / 21 * o * l * 2 ^ n' * ((l : ℝ) * 2 ^ n' * N * p1) ^ 16 * (e : ℝ) ^ 5 * Real.log (pZero (l * N * p1))

def ColumnFamilyStandardVectorsBasis : Prop :=
  ∃ c4 : ℝ, ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (h : Host) (t : ℕ), h.Big t →
    ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) (e : ℕ), l ≠ 2 → 0 < e → e < t → ∀ ψ ∈ h.X c e,
      (h.CaseII l e ψ → VLe Ω c e ψ (leSqrtOfDivSqGeOne c4 h.o l (h.nII l ψ) h.N 1 e)) ∧
      (h.CaseIII l e ψ → VLe Ω c e ψ (leSqrtOfDivSqGeOne c4 h.o l (h.nIII l ψ) h.N (pOne h.chi) e))

def PropZ : Prop :=
  ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) (e : ℕ),
    5 ≤ l → 2 ≤ e → Even e → l - 1 ∣ 2 * e → Chr.V l e (1 : G ℚ Ω →* c.Oˣ) = 0

def CountI : Prop :=
  ∀ χ : PChar, ∃ (C r₁ r₂ : ℕ), r₁.Prime ∧ r₂.Prime ∧ ∀ h : Host, h.chi = χ → r₁ < h.q1 → r₁ < h.d → r₂ < h.q1 → r₂ < h.d →
    ∀ t : ℕ, h.Big t → ∀ e : ℕ, 0 < e → e < t → ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω]
      (c : ∀ l : Nat.Primes, Coef l) (Ls : Finset Nat.Primes), (∀ l ∈ Ls, (l : ℕ) ≠ 2 ∧ h.CaseI l e) →
      (∀ l ∈ Ls, ∀ ψ ∈ h.X (c l) e, Vlen Ω (c l) e ψ ≠ ⊤) ∧
      ∑ l ∈ Ls, Real.log (l : ℕ) * ∑ ψ ∈ h.X (c l) e, Vn Ω (c l) e ψ ≤ ((h.N : ℝ) * t) ^ C

def CountShape (P : ∀ (h : Host) (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) (_ : ℕ), DirichletCharacter c.O h.N → Prop) : Prop :=
  ∀ χ : PChar, ∃ C : ℕ, ∀ h : Host, h.chi = χ → ∀ t : ℕ, h.Big t →
    ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) (e : ℕ),
      l ≠ 2 → 0 < e → e < t → ∀ ψ ∈ h.X c e, P h l c e ψ → VLe Ω c e ψ (((l : ℝ) * h.N * e) ^ C)

def CountII : Prop := CountShape fun h l _ _ e ψ => h.CaseII l e ψ

def CountIII : Prop := CountShape fun h l _ _ e ψ => h.CaseIII l e ψ

def CountV : Prop := CountShape fun h l _ _ e ψ => h.CaseV l e ∧ h.R1 l ψ ≤ 2 ^ c2 ψ

def CountVs : Prop := CountShape fun h l _ _ e ψ => h.CaseV l e ∧ 2 ^ c2 ψ < h.R1 l ψ

def CountZ : Prop := CountShape fun h l _ _ e ψ => h.CaseZ l e ψ

end UpperTheory.V.HV

end


[AFE_SRC_DAT] Full original Dat, its character and specified conjugation
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/RhoKApply.lean#L1-L98
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.Rigid0
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.HeightAndVanishing.Counts
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PadicHodgeTheory.IsInertiaInfiniteCyclotomic

noncomputable section

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual Formalism.HC ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.BasicNotions.Loc UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.Alg
open IsLocalRing Matrix Formalism.MainNotions.N GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp

namespace UpperTheory.V.U3

abbrev CharCont {O Γ : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] (Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ) : Prop :=
  ∀ n : ℤ, AdicCont (maximalIdeal O) fun g => ((Ψ g ^ n : Oˣ) : O)

structure Dat (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) where
  gd : GD Ω K l e ψ
  η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ
  η_ker : η.ker = galOf ℚ K Ω
  cc : G ℚ Ω
  conj : ∃ φ : Ω →+* ℂ, ∀ x, φ (cc x) = starRingEnd ℂ (φ x)
  j : Ω →+* Om l
  ι : GQ l →* G ℚ Ω
  ι_cont : Continuous ι
  ι_spec : ∀ σ x, σ (j x) = j (ι σ x)
  Kv : FinExt l
  Kv_spec : ∀ g, η (ι g) = 1 ↔ g ∈ GK Kv
  η_unr : ∀ g ∈ Loc.Iq l, η (ι g) = 1
  τ_open : IsOpen (((Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (comp l ψ)).comp ι).ker : Set (GQ l))
  Ψ_cont : CharCont (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ))
  Ψ_I : ∀ g ∈ Loc.Iq l, Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) (ι g) =
    Loc.cycA l l c.O g ^ e * Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (comp l ψ) (ι g)

namespace Dat

variable {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
  {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ)

abbrev Ψ (_ : Dat Ω K c e ψ) : G ℚ Ω →* c.Oˣ := Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)

def ΨQ : G ℚ Ω →* (PadicAlgCl l)ˣ := (Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom).comp D.Ψ

abbrev loc : Loc.Dat3 l c.O where
  η := D.η.comp D.ι
  Ψ := D.Ψ.comp D.ι
  τ := (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (comp l ψ)).comp D.ι
  e := e
  Kv := D.Kv
  odd := D.gd.l_odd
  e_pos := D.gd.e_pos
  η_ker := D.Kv_spec
  η_unr := D.η_unr
  τ_open := D.τ_open
  Ψ_cont n := by
    have h := (D.Ψ_cont 1 n).comp_continuous D.ι_cont
    simp only [zpow_one] at h
    exact h
  Ψ_I := D.Ψ_I

def Kill : Set (G ℚ Ω) :=
  {g | ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧ g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A ∧ rbar c.O D.η D.Ψ g = 1}

abbrev ULoc : Type 1 := Univ (GCond.noCondition c.O (D.η.comp D.ι) (D.Ψ.comp D.ι))

abbrev Ring (U : D.ULoc) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) : Type 1 := SRing D.Kill D.ι_cont U (D.loc.It U) D.cc g₁

abbrev Marked (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) : Prop := Marks D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁

def rhoK {κ : Type} [CommRing κ] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 κ) (hr : HasMult D.η r) : G K Ω →* GL (Fin 3) κ :=
  (glPart D.η r hr).comp ((galEmb ℚ K Ω).codRestrict D.η.ker fun s => D.η_ker ▸ galEmb_mem ℚ K Ω s)

theorem rhoK_apply {κ : Type} [CommRing κ] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 κ) (hr : HasMult D.η r) (s : G K Ω) :
    D.rhoK r hr s = (r (galEmb ℚ K Ω s)).left := rfl

structure IsPt (U : D.ULoc) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l)) : Prop where
  mult : HasMult D.η r
  cont : Continuous (mats r)
  int : ∀ g a b, ‖(r g).mat a b‖ ≤ 1
  red : ∀ g a b, ‖(r g).mat a b - (r0 D.η D.ΨQ g).mat a b‖ < 1
  norm : Normalised D.cc g₁ r
  kill : ∀ g ∈ D.Kill, r g = 1
  loc : ∃ f : U.R →ₐ[c.O] PadicAlgCl l, IsLocPt f ∧ D.loc.It U ≤ RingHom.ker f ∧ push f U.r.hom = r.comp D.ι

def IsExt {κ : Type} [CommRing κ] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 κ) (W : G ℚ Ω →* GL (Fin 2) κ) : Prop :=
  (∀ g ∈ D.η.ker, (W g : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) κ) = corner (r g).mat) ∧ (W D.cc : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) κ).trace = 0

def IsAutI [IsCMField K] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 (PadicAlgCl l)) (hr : HasMult D.η r) : Prop :=
  Af.IsAutCsd ι (Af.tw (D.rhoK r hr) (cycBar K Ω l)⁻¹)

def IsModular (_ : Dat Ω K c e ψ) (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (W : G ℚ Ω →* GL (Fin 2) (PadicAlgCl l)) : Prop :=
  ∃ f : Af.Newform (2 * e + 1), f.Att ι (Af.tw W (cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ (-(e : ℤ))))

end Dat

end UpperTheory.V.U3

end


[AFE_SRC_GEN] All nineteen Gen fields and full HasFrameW
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum.lean#L1-L111
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.HostFrameEx
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisCochains.GLinkQuot
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.WeilGroupCharacters.ContinuousResP

noncomputable section

open NumberField Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr
open scoped Pointwise

namespace UpperTheory.V.Fr

variable {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O]
  [CharP (ResidueField O) l]

variable (Ω O) in

abbrev omO : G ℚ Ω →* (ResidueField O)ˣ := GC.cycF ℚ Ω l (ResidueField O)

structure Gen (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ) (Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) : Prop where
  odd : l ≠ 2
  fin : Finite (ResidueField O)
  M_ne : M ≠ 0
  l_dvd : l ∣ M
  two_dvd : 2 ∣ M
  cc : IsCC σ
  over_l : B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1
  quad : ∃ (D : ℕ) (δ : Ω), 0 < D ∧ (∀ q : ℕ, q.Prime → q ∣ D → q ∣ M) ∧ δ ^ 2 = -(D : Ω) ∧ ∀ s : G ℚ Ω, s δ = (η s : ℤ) • δ
  cyc : ∃ a : ℕ, Af.KillsMu (M ^ a) η ∧ Af.KillsMu (M ^ a) (barChar O Ψ)
  η_l : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → Ine (F := ℚ) B' ≤ η.ker
  even : barChar O Ψ σ = 1
  Ψ_l : ∃ j : ℕ, ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B', barChar O Ψ s = omO Ω l O s ^ j
  g4 : ¬ (omO Ω l O).ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker ≤ η.ker
  g5 : ∃ s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) B, η s = 1 ∧ barChar O Ψ s ^ 2 ≠ 1
  Hb_open : IsOpen (Hb : Set (G ℚ Ω))
  Hb_ab : commutator (G ℚ Ω) ≤ Hb
  Hb_idx : ∃ a : ℕ, Hb.index = l ^ a
  Hb_l : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → Ine (F := ℚ) B' ≤ Hb
  teich : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ Hb, barChar O Ψ s = 1 → Ψ s = 1

variable (Ω) in

def Hth (w : ℕ) : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω) where
  carrier := {s | ∃ a : ℕ, ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ w = 1 → s ζ = ζ ^ a ^ l} ∩ {s | ∃ a : ℕ, ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ w = 1 → s⁻¹ ζ = ζ ^ a ^ l}
  one_mem' := ⟨⟨1, by simp⟩, ⟨1, by simp⟩⟩
  mul_mem' := by
    rintro s t ⟨⟨a, ha⟩, ⟨a', ha'⟩⟩ ⟨⟨b, hb⟩, ⟨b', hb'⟩⟩
    refine ⟨⟨a * b, fun ζ h => ?_⟩, ⟨b' * a', fun ζ h => ?_⟩⟩
    · rw [AlgEquiv.mul_apply, hb ζ h, map_pow, ha ζ h, ← pow_mul, mul_pow]
    · rw [mul_inv_rev, AlgEquiv.mul_apply, ha' ζ h, map_pow, hb' ζ h, ← pow_mul, mul_pow]
  inv_mem' := by
    rintro s ⟨h1, h2⟩
    exact ⟨h2, by show ∃ a : ℕ, ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ w = 1 → s⁻¹⁻¹ ζ = ζ ^ a ^ l; rw [inv_inv]; exact h1⟩

variable (Ω) in

def Hlay (m : ℕ) : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω) := (cycMod ℚ Ω l (m + 1) ^ (l - 1)).ker

structure FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (M : ℕ) (H₀ : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) : Prop where
  isOpen : IsOpen (H₀ : Set (G ℚ Ω))
  idx : H₀.index = 2
  real : ∀ τ : G ℚ Ω, IsCC τ → τ ∈ H₀
  loc : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (M : Ω) < 1 → Dec (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ H₀ = Dec (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ η.ker
  ram : ∃ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (M : Ω) < 1 ∧ ¬ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ≤ H₀

structure FrobeniusWitnessPrime (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (M w : ℕ) : Prop where
  prime : w.Prime
  cop : ¬ w ∣ M
  one : w ≡ 1 [MOD l]
  split : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (w : Ω) < 1 →
    Dec (F := ℚ) B' ≤ η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker ⊓ (omO Ω l O).ker

def Upl (Hb H₀ : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) (w m : ℕ) : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω) := H₀ ⊓ Hth Ω l w ⊓ Hb ⊓ Hlay Ω l m

structure UFacts (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (M m : ℕ) (U : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) : Prop where
  isOpen : IsOpen (U : Set (G ℚ Ω))
  ab : commutator (G ℚ Ω) ≤ U
  real : ∀ τ : G ℚ Ω, IsCC τ → τ ∈ U
  unr : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ U ≤ η.ker
  split : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (M : Ω) < 1 → Dec (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ U ≤ η.ker
  teich : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ U, barChar O Ψ s = 1 → Ψ s = 1
  mu : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ U, cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 s ≠ 1
  deg : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → l ^ m ≤ U.relIndex (Dec (F := ℚ) B')
  f2b : ∀ j : ℤ, j = 1 ∨ j = -1 → ∃ s ∈ U ⊓ η.ker, omO Ω l O s * barChar O Ψ s ^ j ≠ 1
  f2c : ∀ j : ℤ, j = 2 ∨ j = -2 → ∃ s ∈ U ⊓ η.ker, omO Ω l O s * barChar O Ψ s ^ j ≠ 1
  f3 : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃ s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ U, barChar O Ψ s ^ 2 ≠ 1
  f5 : ¬ ∃ j : ℤ, ∀ s ∈ U ⊓ η.ker, omO Ω l O s = barChar O Ψ s ^ j

abbrev etaO (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) : G ℚ Ω →* (ResidueField O)ˣ :=
  (Units.map (Int.castRingHom (ResidueField O)).toMonoidHom).comp η

def StrictCl (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (κ : G ℚ Ω →* (ResidueField O)ˣ) (U : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) : Prop :=
  ∃ y : G ℚ Ω → ResidueField O, IsLocallyConstant y ∧ (∀ s t, y (s * t) = y s + κ s * y t) ∧
    (¬ ∃ b, ∀ s ∈ U, y s = κ s * b - b) ∧
    (∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃ b, ∀ s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) B', y s = κ s * b - b) ∧
    ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ U, η s = 1 → barChar O Ψ s = 1 → y s = 0

def Linked (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ g₁ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ)
    (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Algebra L Ω] (Φ : Frame l (ResidueField O)) : Prop :=
  ∃ (k : GalLink η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ), ∃ S π ι pl e c, GC.FLink (F := W.Lp L) (Ω := Ω) l Φ S
      ((η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker).comap (galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω)) π ι k.pl pl e c ∧
      (∀ (g : G (W.Lp L) Ω) (h : galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω g ∈ upl Ω L), π g = k.p ⟨_, h⟩) ∧
      ∀ v s, galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω (ι v s) = k.j v s

def HasFrameW (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M Q : ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∃ (L : Type) (_ : Field L) (_ : NumberField L) (_ : IsCMField L) (_ : Algebra L Ω) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω)
    (Φ : Frame l (ResidueField O)) (C₀ w : ℕ), Linked l η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ ∧ Φ.Ok C₀ ∧ Φ.Big C₀ ∧
      FrobeniusWitnessPrime l η Ψ M w ∧ w ≡ 1 [MOD Q] ∧ upl Ω L ≤ Hth Ω l w

end UpperTheory.V.Fr

end


[AFE_SRC_GD_ENTRY] Original gen_gd; discriminant and residue dictionary
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/SqrtDiscr.lean#L1-L284
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.FrobeniusSplitPrimeDatum
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.DatFacts
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.SubSmulMulSubSmulEqZeroOfQuadratic
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.HeightAndVanishing.GdFacts
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.LocalRingsCompletion.dvrFinite

open NumberField Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V

private theorem hom_ext {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [CommRing k] [CharP k l] (f g : ℤ_[l] →+* k) : f = g := by
  ext x
  obtain ⟨y, hy⟩ : (l : ℤ_[l]) ∣ x - (x.zmodRepr : ℤ_[l]) := by
    have hm := PadicInt.sub_zmodRepr_mem x
    rwa [PadicInt.maximalIdeal_eq_span_p, Ideal.mem_span_singleton] at hm
  have hx : x = (x.zmodRepr : ℤ_[l]) + l * y := by rw [← hy]; ring
  rw [hx]
  simp only [map_add, map_mul, map_natCast, CharP.cast_eq_zero k l, zero_mul]

private theorem sqrt_discr {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] (hK : Module.finrank ℚ K = 2) :
    ∃ δ : K, δ ^ 2 = (discr K : K) := by
  classical
  haveI : Algebra.IsQuadraticExtension ℚ K := ⟨hK⟩
  set b := NumberField.integralBasis K with hb
  have hcard : Fintype.card (Module.Free.ChooseBasisIndex ℤ (𝓞 K)) = Fintype.card Gal(K/ℚ) := by
    rw [← Module.finrank_eq_card_basis b, ← Nat.card_eq_fintype_card]
    exact (IsGalois.card_aut_eq_finrank ℚ K).symm
  let e := Fintype.equivOfCardEq hcard
  let M : Matrix (Module.Free.ChooseBasisIndex ℤ (𝓞 K)) (Module.Free.ChooseBasisIndex ℤ (𝓞 K)) K :=
    fun i j => (e j) (b i)
  refine ⟨M.det, ?_⟩
  have h1 : (discr K : K) = algebraMap ℚ K (Algebra.discr ℚ b) := by
    rw [hb, ← NumberField.coe_discr]
    exact (map_intCast _ _).symm
  have h2 : (algebraMap ℚ K).mapMatrix (Algebra.traceMatrix ℚ b) = M * M.transpose := by
    ext i j
    rw [RingHom.mapMatrix_apply, Matrix.map_apply, Algebra.traceMatrix_apply, Algebra.traceForm_apply,
      trace_eq_sum_automorphisms, Matrix.mul_apply]
    rw [← Fintype.sum_equiv e (fun j' => (e j') (b i) * (e j') (b j)) (fun σ => σ (b i * b j))
      (fun j' => by simp only [map_mul])]
    rfl
  rw [h1, Algebra.discr_def, RingHom.map_det, h2, Matrix.det_mul, Matrix.det_transpose, sq]

private theorem discr_neg {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] (hK : Module.finrank ℚ K = 2) (h : IsTotallyComplex K) :
    discr K < 0 := by
  haveI := h
  have h1 := NumberField.InfinitePlace.card_add_two_mul_card_eq_rank K
  rw [IsTotallyComplex.nrRealPlaces_eq_zero, hK] at h1
  have hc : NumberField.InfinitePlace.nrComplexPlaces K = 1 := by omega
  have hs := NumberField.sign_discr K
  rw [hc, pow_one] at hs
  exact Int.sign_eq_neg_one_iff_neg.mp hs

private theorem quad_ex {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    (hK : Module.finrank ℚ K = 2) (h : IsTotallyComplex K) (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (hη : η.ker = galOf ℚ K Ω) (σ : G ℚ Ω)
    (φ : Ω →+* ℂ) (hφ : ∀ x, φ (σ x) = starRingEnd ℂ (φ x)) (hσ : η σ = -1) :
    ∃ δ : Ω, δ ^ 2 = -((discr K).natAbs : Ω) ∧ ∀ s : G ℚ Ω, s δ = (η s : ℤ) • δ := by
  obtain ⟨δ0, hδ0⟩ := sqrt_discr hK
  have hneg := discr_neg hK h
  have hnd : ((discr K).natAbs : ℤ) = -discr K := Int.ofNat_natAbs_of_nonpos hneg.le
  have hirr : ∀ r : ℚ, r • (1 : K) ≠ δ0 := by
    intro r hr
    rw [Rat.smul_def, mul_one] at hr
    have h2 : ((r ^ 2 : ℚ) : K) = ((discr K : ℚ) : K) := by
      rw [Rat.cast_pow, hr, hδ0, Rat.cast_intCast]
    have h3 := Rat.cast_injective h2
    have h4 : (0 : ℚ) ≤ r ^ 2 := sq_nonneg r
    rw [h3] at h4
    have h5 : (discr K : ℚ) < 0 := by exact_mod_cast hneg
    linarith
  have hli : LinearIndependent ℚ ![(1 : K), δ0] :=
    (LinearIndependent.pair_iff' one_ne_zero).mpr hirr
  have hsp := hli.span_eq_top_of_card_eq_finrank' (by rw [hK]; rfl)
  have hspan : ∀ x : K, ∃ a b : ℚ, x = (a : K) + (b : K) * δ0 := by
    intro x
    have hx : x ∈ Submodule.span ℚ (Set.range ![(1 : K), δ0]) := by rw [hsp]; trivial
    obtain ⟨c, hc⟩ := (Submodule.mem_span_range_iff_exists_fun ℚ).mp hx
    refine ⟨c 0, c 1, ?_⟩
    rw [← hc, Fin.sum_univ_two]
    simp [Rat.smul_def]
  have hfix : ∀ s : G ℚ Ω, s (algebraMap K Ω δ0) = algebraMap K Ω δ0 → s ∈ galOf ℚ K Ω := by
    intro s hs x
    obtain ⟨a, b, rfl⟩ := hspan x
    rw [map_add, map_mul, map_ratCast, map_ratCast, map_add, map_mul, hs, map_ratCast, map_ratCast]
  have hsq : (algebraMap K Ω δ0) ^ 2 = -((discr K).natAbs : Ω) := by
    rw [← map_pow, hδ0, map_intCast]
    rw [← Int.cast_natCast, hnd, Int.cast_neg, neg_neg]
  refine ⟨algebraMap K Ω δ0, hsq, ?_⟩
  intro s
  have hs2 : (s (algebraMap K Ω δ0)) ^ 2 = (algebraMap K Ω δ0) ^ 2 := by
    rw [← map_pow, hsq, map_neg, map_natCast]
  rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (η s) with he | he
  · have hk : s ∈ η.ker := he
    rw [hη] at hk
    rw [he, Units.val_one, one_smul]
    exact hk δ0
  · rw [he, Units.val_neg, Units.val_one, neg_smul, one_smul]
    have hm : (s (algebraMap K Ω δ0) - algebraMap K Ω δ0) * (s (algebraMap K Ω δ0) + algebraMap K Ω δ0) = 0 := by
      linear_combination hs2
    rcases mul_eq_zero.mp hm with h1 | h1
    · exfalso
      have hk : s ∈ η.ker := by
        rw [hη]
        exact hfix s (sub_eq_zero.mp h1)
      have h1' : η s = 1 := hk
      rw [he] at h1'
      exact absurd h1' (by decide)
    · exact eq_neg_of_add_eq_zero_left h1

private theorem kills_mono {Ω C : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [Monoid C] {M M' : ℕ} (h : M ∣ M') {χ : G ℚ Ω →* C}
    (hχ : Af.KillsMu M χ) : Af.KillsMu M' χ := fun s hs => hχ s fun ζ hζ => hs ζ (by
  obtain ⟨k, rfl⟩ := h
  rw [pow_mul, hζ, one_pow])

private theorem cyc_ine (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime]
    (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (hA : ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1) {σ : G ℚ Ω} (hσ : σ ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A) :
    cycZ ℚ Ω l σ = 1 := by
  haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
  have hl1 : 1 ≤ A.valuation (l : Ω) := not_lt.mp hA
  have fix : ∀ (n : ℕ) (t : Ω), t ^ (l ^ n) = 1 → σ t = t := by
    intro n t ht
    haveI : NeZero (l ^ n) := ⟨pow_ne_zero _ (Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero⟩
    have hker : σ ∈ (UpperTheory.V.Chr.cls ℚ Ω (l ^ n)).ker := by
      apply (UpperTheory.V.Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω (l ^ n)).2.2.2.2 A
      · rw [Nat.cast_pow, map_pow, not_lt]
        exact one_le_pow₀ hl1
      · exact hσ
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker] at hker
    rw [UpperTheory.V.Chr.cls_spec (l ^ n) σ t ht, hker]
    rcases n with _ | n
    · simp only [pow_zero, pow_one] at ht
      simp [ht]
    · have h1 : l ^ (n + 1) ≠ 1 := (Nat.one_lt_pow (Nat.succ_ne_zero n) (Fact.out : l.Prime).one_lt).ne'
      rw [Units.val_one, ZMod.val_one'' h1, pow_one]
  show cyclotomicCharacter Ω l (MulSemiringAction.toRingAut (G ℚ Ω) Ω σ) = 1
  apply Units.ext
  apply PadicInt.ext_of_toZModPow.mp
  intro n
  rw [cyclotomicCharacter.toZModPow, Units.val_one, map_one]
  symm
  apply modularCyclotomicCharacter.unique
  intro t ht
  have htn : (t : Ω) ^ (l ^ n) = 1 := by
    rw [mem_rootsOfUnity] at ht
    rw [← Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, ht, Units.val_one]
  change σ (t : Ω) = (t : Ω) ^ (1 : ZMod (l ^ n)).val
  rw [fix n t htn]
  rcases n with _ | n
  · simp only [pow_zero, pow_one] at htn
    simp [htn]
  · have h1 : l ^ (n + 1) ≠ 1 := (Nat.one_lt_pow (Nat.succ_ne_zero n) (Fact.out : l.Prime).one_lt).ne'
    rw [ZMod.val_one'' h1, pow_one]

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.gen_gd :
    ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K] [Algebra K Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l] (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) (B : ValuationSubring Ω), B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → Gen l D.η D.Ψ D.cc B (l * N * (discr K).natAbs) ⊤ := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ _ l _ c _ N _ e ψ D B hB
  have gd := D.gd
  obtain ⟨hcc2, hηcc, hΨcc, -, -, -, -, -, -, -, hIneL⟩ := dat_facts D
  obtain ⟨hηd, hd1, hψN, -⟩ := eta_killsMu D
  obtain ⟨⟨w, hw, huniq⟩, hwF⟩ := omT Ω l c
  obtain ⟨hwker1, hwcls, -, -, -, -, -, -⟩ := hwF w hw
  obtain ⟨w', η', a, hlit⟩ := (gdIff Ω K l c e N ψ).1.mp gd
  have hrel := (gdIff Ω K l c e N ψ).2 w' η' a hlit
  have hww : w' = w := huniq w' hlit.om
  subst hww
  have hbar : ∀ s, Units.map (residue c.O : c.O →* ResidueField c.O) (chiO ℚ Ω l c.O s) = omO Ω l c.O s := by
    intro s
    apply Units.ext
    exact RingHom.congr_fun (hom_ext ((residue c.O).comp (algebraMap ℤ_[l] c.O))
      ((ZMod.castHom (dvd_pow_self l one_ne_zero) (ResidueField c.O)).comp (PadicInt.toZModPow 1))) _
  have hPsi : ∀ s, D.Ψ s = chiO ℚ Ω l c.O s ^ e * Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ s := fun s => rfl
  have hbarPsi : ∀ s, barChar c.O D.Ψ s = omO Ω l c.O s ^ e * Units.map (residue c.O : c.O →* ResidueField c.O) (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ s) := by
    intro s
    show Units.map _ (D.Ψ s) = _
    rw [hPsi, map_mul, map_pow]
    congr 2
    exact hbar s
  have hbarw : ∀ s, Units.map (residue c.O : c.O →* ResidueField c.O) (w' s) = omO Ω l c.O s := by
    intro s
    rw [← hbar s]
    apply Units.ext
    change residue c.O (w' s : c.O) = residue c.O (chiO ℚ Ω l c.O s : c.O)
    exact Ideal.Quotient.eq.mpr (hw.2 s)
  have homO : ∀ s, s ∈ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker → omO Ω l c.O s = 1 := by
    intro s hs
    show Units.map _ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 s) = 1
    rw [hs, map_one]
  refine
    { odd := gd.l_odd
      fin := ?_
      M_ne := ?_
      l_dvd := ⟨N * (discr K).natAbs, by ring⟩
      two_dvd := ?_
      cc := D.conj
      over_l := hB
      quad := ?_
      cyc := ?_
      η_l := fun B' hB' => (hIneL B' hB').1
      even := ?_
      Ψ_l := ?_
      g4 := ?_
      g5 := ?_
      Hb_open := ?_
      Hb_ab := le_top
      Hb_idx := ⟨0, by simp⟩
      Hb_l := fun _ _ => le_top
      teich := ?_ }
  · haveI := c.dvr
    haveI := c.fin
    exact (abelian₂.dvr_finite l c.O).2.1
  · have hl := (Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero
    have hN := NeZero.ne N
    have hd : (discr K).natAbs ≠ 0 := by omega
    exact Nat.mul_ne_zero (Nat.mul_ne_zero hl hN) hd
  · exact Dvd.dvd.mul_right (Dvd.dvd.mul_left (even_iff_two_dvd.mp gd.N_even) l) _
  · obtain ⟨φ, hφ⟩ := D.conj
    obtain ⟨δ, hδ, hs⟩ := quad_ex gd.quad gd.imag D.η D.η_ker D.cc φ hφ hηcc
    exact ⟨_, δ, by omega, fun q _ hq => Dvd.dvd.mul_left hq _, hδ, hs⟩
  · refine ⟨1, ?_, ?_⟩
    · rw [pow_one]
      exact kills_mono (Dvd.intro_left _ rfl) hηd
    · rw [pow_one]
      intro s hs
      have hdiv : ∀ k, k ∣ l * N * (discr K).natAbs → ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ k = 1 → s ζ = ζ := by
        intro k hk ζ hζ
        apply hs
        obtain ⟨m, hm⟩ := hk
        rw [hm, pow_mul, hζ, one_pow]
      have h1 : Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ s = 1 :=
        hψN s (hdiv N (Dvd.dvd.mul_right (Dvd.intro_left l rfl) _))
      haveI : NeZero l := ⟨(Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero⟩
      haveI : Fact (1 < l) := ⟨(Fact.out : l.Prime).one_lt⟩
      have h2 : s ∈ (Chr.cls ℚ Ω l).ker := by
        rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker]
        apply (Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω l).2.2.1
        intro ζ hζ
        rw [Units.val_one, ZMod.val_one, pow_one]
        exact hdiv l ⟨N * (discr K).natAbs, by ring⟩ ζ hζ
      rw [← hwcls, hwker1] at h2
      rw [hbarPsi, homO s h2, h1, one_pow, map_one, one_mul]
  · show Units.map _ (D.Ψ D.cc) = 1
    rw [hΨcc, map_one]
  · refine ⟨e + a, fun B' hB' s hs => ?_⟩
    rw [hbarPsi, hlit.ine B' hB' s hs, map_pow, hbarw s, pow_add]
  · intro hle
    apply gd.eta
    intro s hs
    obtain ⟨hs1, hs2⟩ := Subgroup.mem_inf.mp hs
    rw [← D.η_ker]
    apply hle
    have hw1 : w' s = 1 := by
      rw [← MonoidHom.mem_ker, hwker1]
      exact hs1
    have hψ1 : Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ s = 1 := by
      have h3 := hs2
      rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, hrel] at h3
      simpa [hw1] using h3
    refine Subgroup.mem_inf.mpr ⟨homO s hs1, ?_⟩
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, hbarPsi, homO s hs1, hψ1, one_pow, map_one, one_mul]
  · obtain ⟨s, hs, hu⟩ := (hIneL B hB).2
    refine ⟨s, hs.1, (hIneL B hB).1 hs, ?_⟩
    intro h
    have h' : residue c.O ((D.Ψ s : c.O) ^ 2) = residue c.O 1 := by
      have h4 := congrArg Units.val h
      simpa [barChar] using h4
    exact (IsLocalRing.mem_maximalIdeal _).mp (Ideal.Quotient.eq.mp h') hu
  · exact isOpen_univ
  · intro B' hB' s hs hb
    have hs' : s ∈ Ine B' := (Subgroup.mem_inf.mp hs).1
    have hz : cycZ ℚ Ω l s = 1 := cyc_ine Ω l B' hB' hs'
    have hc : chiO ℚ Ω l c.O s = 1 := by
      show Units.map _ (cycZ ℚ Ω l s) = 1
      rw [hz, map_one]
    have hx : Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ s = 1 := by
      have hm : ¬ l ∣ orderOf (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) := hlit.ord
      have hpow : Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ s ^ orderOf (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) = 1 := by
        have h5 : (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ ^ orderOf (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) s = 1 := by
          rw [pow_orderOf_eq_one, MonoidHom.one_apply]
        rwa [MonoidHom.pow_apply] at h5
      apply (congrFacts Ω l c).2.2.2.1 _ 1 _ hm hpow (one_pow _)
      rw [hbarPsi, ← hbar, hc, map_one, one_pow, one_mul] at hb
      have hb' : residue c.O (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ s : c.O) = residue c.O 1 := by
        have h6 := congrArg Units.val hb
        simpa using h6
      exact Ideal.Quotient.eq.mp hb'
    rw [hPsi, hc, hx, one_pow, one_mul]


[AFE_SRC_HOST_DATA] Full HostData, eta4 and ten IsHostFrame fields
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeDeformations/IsHostFrame.lean#L1-L52
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeDeformations.IsCharZeroPt

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 IsLocalRing

namespace UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia

structure HostData (l e : ℕ) {O : Type} [CommRing O] {N : ℕ} (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) (o₁ : ℕ) : Prop where
  l_odd : l ≠ 2
  e_pos : 1 ≤ e
  N_even : Even N
  par : PsiEven e ψ
  o_pos : 1 ≤ o₁
  ord : ∀ r : ℕ, r.Prime → r ≠ 2 → comp r (th0 l ψ) ^ (2 * o₁) = 1
  R : ∀ v ∈ Tb l ψ, 8 * o₁ * v ^ 2 < 2 ^ c2 ψ
  four_le : 4 ≤ c2 ψ

abbrev Pl : Type := HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ)

variable (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω]

def eta4 : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ := Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (ZMod.χ₄ : DirichletCharacter ℤ 4)

abbrev Gam (S : Finset Pl) : Type := GS Ω (S : Set Pl)

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) {N : ℕ} [NeZero N]

structure IsHostFrame (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (S : Finset Pl) (A : S → ValuationSubring Ω)
    (F : Frame c.O (G ℚ Ω) (Gam Ω S) S) : Prop where
  mem : ∀ v : Pl, v ∈ S ↔ ((l * N : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) ∈ v.asIdeal
  isOver : ∀ v, IsPlaceOver (A v) v.1
  π : F.π = toGS Ω _
  η : F.η.comp F.π = eta4 Ω
  Ψ : F.Ψ.comp F.π = Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)
  cc : ∃ σ : G ℚ Ω, IsCC σ ∧ F.π σ = F.c
  D : ∀ v, F.D v = Dec (F := ℚ) (A v)
  I : ∀ v, F.I v = Ine (F := ℚ) (A v)
  vl : ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) ∈ F.vl.1.asIdeal
  Sb : ∀ v, v ∈ F.Sb ↔ ∃ p ∈ HV.Sb l ψ, ((p : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) ∈ v.1.asIdeal

def vl1 (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (x : PadicAlgCl l) : ℝ := -Real.log ‖x‖ / Real.log l

def XData (l : ℕ) {O : Type} [CommRing O] {N : ℕ} (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) (q : ℕ) : Prop :=
  0 < q ∧ (th1 l ψ ^ 2).conductor ∣ q ∧ 2 * (l * q) * 4 ^ n0 l ψ ≤ 2 ^ c2 ψ

end UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia

end

open NumberField Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V

section SlopeTestRemainingChoices

variable {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω]

private theorem gh_not_both (A : ValuationSubring Ω) {a b : ℕ} (hab : a.Coprime b) (ha : A.valuation (a : Ω) < 1) :
    ¬ A.valuation (b : Ω) < 1 := fun hb => by
  obtain ⟨x, y, h⟩ : IsCoprime (a : A) (b : A) := hab.cast
  have ha' : (a : A) ∈ maximalIdeal A := (A.valuation_lt_one_iff _).2 (by simpa using ha)
  have hb' : (b : A) ∈ maximalIdeal A := (A.valuation_lt_one_iff _).2 (by simpa using hb)
  exact (maximalIdeal.isMaximal A).ne_top ((Ideal.eq_top_iff_one _).2
    (h ▸ Ideal.add_mem _ (Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ ha') (Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ hb')))

private theorem gh_dir_one {O : Type} [CommRing O] {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] (χ : DirichletCharacter O N) {s : G ℚ Ω}
    (h : Chr.cls ℚ Ω N s = 1) : Chr.dirG ℚ Ω χ s = 1 := by
  rw [Chr.dirG, MonoidHom.comp_apply, h, map_one]

private theorem gh_cls_one {M : ℕ} (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (h : N ∣ M) {s : G ℚ Ω} (hs : ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ M = 1 → s ζ = ζ) :
    Chr.cls ℚ Ω N s = 1 := by
  refine (Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω N).2.2.1 s 1 fun ζ hζ => ?_
  obtain ⟨k, rfl⟩ := h
  rw [hs ζ (by rw [pow_mul, hζ, one_pow]), Units.val_one, ← Nat.cast_one, ZMod.val_natCast, ← pow_eq_pow_mod _ hζ, pow_one]

private theorem gh_quad : ∃ δ : Ω, δ ^ 2 = -((1 : ℕ) : Ω) ∧ ∀ s : G ℚ Ω, s δ = (UnitaryThreeInertia.eta4 Ω s : ℤ) • δ := by
  haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
  obtain ⟨δ, hδ⟩ := IsAlgClosed.exists_pow_nat_eq (-1 : Ω) two_pos
  have h4 : δ ^ 4 = 1 := by rw [show 4 = 2 * 2 from rfl, pow_mul, hδ]; norm_num
  refine ⟨δ, by simpa using hδ, fun s => ?_⟩
  rw [Chr.cls_spec 4 s δ h4]
  show _ = (ZMod.χ₄ ((Chr.cls ℚ Ω 4 s : (ZMod 4)ˣ) : ZMod 4) : ℤ) • δ
  generalize Chr.cls ℚ Ω 4 s = u
  have hu : u = 1 ∨ u = -1 := by revert u; decide
  rcases hu with rfl | rfl
  · show δ ^ 1 = (1 : ℤ) • δ
    simp
  · show δ ^ 3 = (-1 : ℤ) • δ
    rw [pow_succ, hδ]
    simp

private theorem gh_bar (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l] (e : ℕ) (θ : G ℚ Ω →* c.Oˣ) (s : G ℚ Ω) :
    barChar c.O (Chr.Psi l e θ) s = omO Ω l c.O s ^ e * barChar c.O θ s := by
  have h : barChar c.O (chiO ℚ Ω l c.O) s = omO Ω l c.O s :=
    Units.ext ((X.cycKFacts Ω l _).2 c (residue c.O) s).symm
  rw [← h, Chr.Psi, zpow_natCast]
  simp only [barChar, MonoidHom.comp_apply, MonoidHom.mul_apply, MonoidHom.pow_apply, map_mul, map_pow]

private theorem gh_om_one (l : ℕ) [hl : Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] {s : G ℚ Ω}
    (h : Chr.cls ℚ Ω (l ^ 1) s = 1) : omO Ω l O s = 1 := by
  have : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
  have : NeZero ((l : ℕ) : Ω) := ⟨Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr hl.out.ne_zero⟩
  have h1 : Chr.cls ℚ Ω (l ^ 1) s = cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 s := (Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω (l ^ 1)).2.2.1 s _ fun ζ hζ =>
      cyclotomicCharacter.spec l (MulSemiringAction.toRingAut (Ω ≃ₐ[ℚ] Ω) Ω s) ζ hζ
  rw [omO, GC.cycF, MonoidHom.comp_apply, ← h1, h, map_one]

private theorem gh_ine_l (l : ℕ) [hl : Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l] {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] (e : ℕ)
    (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) : ∃ j : ℕ, ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B',
      barChar c.O (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) s = omO Ω l c.O s ^ j := by
  obtain ⟨w, hw, -⟩ := (omT Ω l c).1
  obtain ⟨b, -, -, hb⟩ := (psiBar Ω l c w hw N ψ).1
  refine ⟨e + b, fun B' hB s hs => Units.ext ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨-, hmul, -, hinv, -, h6, -⟩ := compAlg c.O N l hl.out
  have h1 : comp l (chiP l ψ) = 1 := by rw [chiP, hmul, hinv, (h6 _).1, mul_inv_cancel]
  have h2 : Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (chiP l ψ) s = 1 := ((compIne Ω N l hl.out B' hB).2 c.O _).2.2 h1 hs
  have h3 := (residue_eq_zero_iff _).2 (hb e s)
  have h4 := (residue_eq_zero_iff _).2 (hw.2 s)
  rw [map_sub, sub_eq_zero] at h3 h4
  rw [MonoidHom.mul_apply, h2, mul_one, MonoidHom.pow_apply, Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, map_pow, h4,
    ← (X.cycKFacts Ω l _).2 c (residue c.O) s] at h3
  exact h3

private theorem gh_sq {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] {x : Oˣ} (h : x ^ 2 = 1) : x = 1 ∨ x = -1 := by
  have h1 : (x : O) * x = 1 := by rw [← sq, ← Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, h, Units.val_one]
  exact (mul_self_eq_one_iff.1 h1).imp (fun h => Units.ext h) fun h => Units.ext h

private theorem gh_two {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) (hc : 4 ≤ c2 ψ) :
    ∃ (h : 4 ∣ N) (u : (ZMod N)ˣ), ZMod.unitsMap h u = -1 ∧ (psi2 ψ).toUnitHom u = 1 := by
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, h1, h2, -⟩ := compCond O N ψ 2 Nat.prime_two
  have h4 : 4 ∣ N := (pow_dvd_pow 2 (show 2 ≤ c2 ψ by omega)).trans h2
  obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := (compOrd O N ψ 2 Nat.prime_two).2.1
  have hm : ZMod.unitsMap h4 (-1) = -1 := by ext; simp [ZMod.unitsMap_def]
  refine ⟨h4, ?_⟩
  have hv : ∃ v : (ZMod N)ˣ, ZMod.unitsMap h4 v = 1 ∧ (psi2 ψ).toUnitHom v ≠ 1 := by
    by_contra hn
    push Not at hn
    have h5 : (psi2 ψ).conductor ∣ 4 := by
      rw [← DirichletCharacter.mem_conductorSet_iff_conductor_dvd _ h4, DirichletCharacter.mem_conductorSet_iff,
        DirichletCharacter.factorsThrough_iff_ker_unitsMap h4]
      exact hn
    rw [← h1] at h5
    have := (Nat.pow_dvd_pow_iff_le_right one_lt_two).1 (show 2 ^ c2 ψ ∣ 2 ^ 2 from h5)
    omega
  obtain ⟨v, hv1, hv2⟩ := hv
  have hv3 : (psi2 ψ).toUnitHom v ^ 2 ^ k = 1 := by
    ext
    rw [Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, MulChar.coe_toUnitHom, ← MulChar.pow_apply_coe, ← hk, psi2, pow_orderOf_eq_one,
      MulChar.one_apply_coe, Units.val_one]
  obtain ⟨j, -, hj⟩ := (Nat.dvd_prime_pow Nat.prime_two).1 (orderOf_dvd_of_pow_eq_one hv3)
  obtain ⟨i, rfl⟩ : ∃ i, j = i + 1 := Nat.exists_eq_succ_of_ne_zero (by
    rintro rfl
    exact hv2 (orderOf_eq_one_iff.1 hj))
  have h6 : (psi2 ψ).toUnitHom (v ^ 2 ^ i) = -1 := by
    have a1 : (psi2 ψ).toUnitHom (v ^ 2 ^ i) ^ 2 = 1 := by rw [map_pow, ← pow_mul, ← pow_succ, ← hj, pow_orderOf_eq_one]
    have a2 : (psi2 ψ).toUnitHom (v ^ 2 ^ i) ≠ 1 := by
      rw [map_pow]
      refine pow_ne_one_of_lt_orderOf (by positivity) ?_
      rw [hj]
      exact Nat.pow_lt_pow_right one_lt_two (Nat.lt_succ_self i)
    exact (gh_sq a1).resolve_left a2
  have h7 : (psi2 ψ).toUnitHom (-1) ^ 2 = 1 := by rw [← map_pow]; simp
  rcases gh_sq h7 with h | h
  · exact ⟨-1, hm, h⟩
  · exact ⟨-1 * v ^ 2 ^ i, by rw [map_mul, map_pow, hv1, one_pow, mul_one, hm], by rw [map_mul, h, h6]; simp⟩

private theorem kernel_not_contained_eta (l : ℕ) [hl : Fact l.Prime] (hl2 : l ≠ 2) (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l] {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] (e : ℕ)
    (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (hc : 4 ≤ c2 ψ) :
    ¬ (omO Ω l c.O).ker ⊓ (barChar c.O (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ))).ker ≤ (UnitaryThreeInertia.eta4 Ω).ker := by
  intro hle
  obtain ⟨h4, u, hu1, hu2⟩ := gh_two ψ hc
  obtain ⟨A, hA⟩ := (Chr.placesQ Ω).1 2 Nat.prime_two
  obtain ⟨s, hs, hsu⟩ : proj 2 N u ∈ (Ine (F := ℚ) A).map (Chr.cls ℚ Ω N) := by
    rw [(compIne Ω N 2 Nat.prime_two A hA).1]
    exact ⟨u, rfl⟩
  have h1 : omO Ω l c.O s = 1 := gh_om_one l c.O ((Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω (l ^ 1)).2.2.2.2 A
    (gh_not_both A (((Nat.coprime_primes Nat.prime_two hl.out).2 hl2.symm).pow_right 1) hA) hs)
  have h2 : Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ s = 1 := by
    rw [Chr.dirG, MonoidHom.comp_apply, hsu, ← (compAlg c.O N 2 Nat.prime_two).1]
    exact hu2
  have h3 : UnitaryThreeInertia.eta4 Ω s = 1 := hle ⟨h1, by
    show barChar c.O _ s = 1
    rw [gh_bar, h1, one_pow, one_mul, barChar, MonoidHom.comp_apply, h2, map_one]⟩
  have hP : 4 ∣ ordProj[2] N := pow_dvd_pow 2 ((Nat.prime_two.pow_dvd_iff_le_factorization (NeZero.ne N)).1 (show 2 ^ 2 ∣ N from h4))
  have hc' : ∀ x, ZMod.unitsMap h4 x = ZMod.unitsMap hP (ZMod.unitsMap (Nat.ordProj_dvd N 2) x) := fun x =>
    (DFunLike.congr_fun (ZMod.unitsMap_comp hP (Nat.ordProj_dvd N 2)) x).symm
  have h5 : Chr.cls ℚ Ω 4 s = -1 := by
    rw [← (Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω N).2.1 4 h4, MonoidHom.comp_apply, hsu, hc', (projFacts N 2 Nat.prime_two).1, ← hc', hu1]
  rw [UnitaryThreeInertia.eta4, Chr.dirG, MonoidHom.comp_apply, h5] at h3
  have h6 := congrArg Units.val h3
  rw [MulChar.coe_toUnitHom] at h6
  exact absurd h6 (by decide)

private theorem exists_decomposition_nonquadratic (l : ℕ) [hl : Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (o₁ : ℕ)
    (hd : UnitaryThreeInertia.HostData l e ψ o₁) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (hB : B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1) :
    ∃ s ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) B, UnitaryThreeInertia.eta4 Ω s = 1 ∧ barChar c.O (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) s ^ 2 ≠ 1 := by
  obtain ⟨S, A, F, hF⟩ := UnitaryThreeInertia.exists_hostFrame Ω c e ψ o₁ hd
  obtain ⟨x, h1, h2, -⟩ := UnitaryThreeInertia.exists_sigma Ω c e ψ o₁ hd S A F hF F.vl (Or.inl rfl)
  have hA : (A F.vl).valuation (l : Ω) < 1 := by simpa using (hF.isOver F.vl _).2 hF.vl
  obtain ⟨u, hu, -⟩ := (Chr.placesQ Ω).2.2 l _ B hl.out hA hB
  have h3 : UnitaryThreeInertia.eta4 Ω x.1 = 1 := hF.η ▸ h1
  have h4 : IsUnit ((Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) x.1 : c.O) ^ 2 - 1) := hF.Ψ ▸ h2
  refine ⟨u⁻¹ * x.1 * u, hu _ (hF.D F.vl ▸ x.2), ?_, fun h => ?_⟩
  · rw [map_mul, map_mul, h3, mul_one, map_inv, inv_mul_cancel]
  · rw [map_mul, map_mul, mul_right_comm, map_inv, inv_mul_cancel, one_mul] at h
    rw [← residue_ne_zero_iff_isUnit, map_sub, map_pow, map_one, sub_ne_zero] at h4
    exact h4 (congrArg Units.val h)

private theorem gh_idx (l : ℕ) [hl : Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] {N : ℕ} [NeZero N]
    (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) : ∃ a : ℕ, (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (kapB l ψ)).ker.index = l ^ a := by
  have h1 : kap l ψ ^ l ^ (orderOf ψ).factorization l = 1 := by
    rw [← (partOrd _ l hl.out ψ ψ.orderOf_pos).1]
    exact pow_orderOf_eq_one _
  obtain ⟨-, -, hone, -, hpow, -⟩ := compAlg O N l hl.out
  have h2 : kapB l ψ ^ l ^ (orderOf ψ).factorization l = 1 := by
    rw [kapB, kapL, mul_pow, inv_pow, ← hpow, h1, hone, inv_one, one_mul]
  haveI : Finite (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (kapB l ψ)).range := by
    refine Set.Finite.subset (Set.finite_range (kapB l ψ).toUnitHom) ?_
    rintro _ ⟨s, rfl⟩
    exact ⟨_, rfl⟩
  rw [Subgroup.index_ker, ← IsPGroup.iff_card]
  rintro ⟨_, s, rfl⟩
  refine ⟨(orderOf ψ).factorization l, Subtype.ext (Units.ext ?_)⟩
  show kapB l ψ ((Chr.cls ℚ Ω N s : (ZMod N)ˣ) : ZMod N) ^ _ = 1
  rw [← MulChar.pow_apply_coe, h2, MulChar.one_apply_coe]

private theorem gh_teich (l : ℕ) [hl : Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N)
    (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (hA : ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1) {s : G ℚ Ω} (hs : s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A)
    (hk : Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (kapB l ψ) s = 1) (h : barChar c.O (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) s = 1) :
    Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) s = 1 := by
  have h1 : cycZ ℚ Ω l s = 1 := (places ℚ Ω l A).2.2.2 hA hs
  have h2 : chiO ℚ Ω l c.O s = 1 := by rw [chiO, MonoidHom.comp_apply, h1, map_one]
  haveI : NeZero (ordProj[l] N) := ⟨(Nat.ordProj_pos N l).ne'⟩
  have hA' : ¬ A.valuation ((ordProj[l] N : ℕ) : Ω) < 1 := fun h' => by
    obtain ⟨p, hp, hp'⟩ := ((Chr.placesQ Ω).2.1 A _ (NeZero.ne _)).1 h'
    have := (Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq (Nat.prime_of_mem_primeFactors hp) hl.out).1
      ((Nat.prime_of_mem_primeFactors hp).dvd_of_dvd_pow (Nat.dvd_of_mem_primeFactors hp))
    exact hA (this ▸ hp')
  have h3 : proj l N (Chr.cls ℚ Ω N s) = 1 := by
    rw [← MonoidHom.mem_ker, (projFacts N l hl.out).2.2.2.2.2.2.1, MonoidHom.mem_ker, ← MonoidHom.comp_apply,
      (Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω N).2.1]
    exact (Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω _).2.2.2.2 A hA' hs
  have h4 : (kapL l ψ).toUnitHom (Chr.cls ℚ Ω N s) = 1 := by rw [kapL, (compAlg c.O N l hl.out).1, h3, map_one]
  have h5 : Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ) s = Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (th0 l ψ) s := by
    rw [Chr.Psi, MonoidHom.mul_apply, zpow_natCast, MonoidHom.pow_apply, h2, one_pow, one_mul]
    ext1
    show ψ ((Chr.cls ℚ Ω N s : (ZMod N)ˣ) : ZMod N) = th0 l ψ ((Chr.cls ℚ Ω N s : (ZMod N)ˣ) : ZMod N)
    have a1 : kapB l ψ ((Chr.cls ℚ Ω N s : (ZMod N)ˣ) : ZMod N) = 1 := congrArg Units.val hk
    have a2 : kapL l ψ ((Chr.cls ℚ Ω N s : (ZMod N)ˣ) : ZMod N) = 1 := congrArg Units.val h4
    have a3 : ψ = th0 l ψ * (kapB l ψ * kapL l ψ) := by rw [kapB, inv_mul_cancel_right, dirichletPrimeToLPart_mul_lPowerPart]
    conv_lhs => rw [a3]
    rw [MulChar.mul_apply, MulChar.mul_apply, a1, a2, one_mul, mul_one]
  refine (congrFacts Ω l c).2.2.2.1 _ 1 _ (partOrd _ l hl.out ψ ψ.orderOf_pos).2.2.1 ?_ (one_pow _) ?_
  · rw [h5]
    ext1
    show th0 l ψ ((Chr.cls ℚ Ω N s : (ZMod N)ˣ) : ZMod N) ^ _ = 1
    rw [← MulChar.pow_apply_coe, th0, pow_orderOf_eq_one, MulChar.one_apply_coe]
  · rw [← residue_eq_zero_iff, map_sub, Units.val_one, map_one, sub_eq_zero]
    exact congrArg Units.val h

end SlopeTestRemainingChoices

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.gen_host :
    ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l] (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (o₁ : ℕ), UnitaryThreeInertia.HostData l e ψ o₁ → ∀ σ : G ℚ Ω, IsCC σ → ∀ B : ValuationSubring Ω, B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃ Hb, Gen l (UnitaryThreeInertia.eta4 Ω) (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) σ B (l * N) Hb := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l hl c _ N _ e ψ o₁ hd σ hσ B hB
  have h2 : 2 ∣ l * N := hd.N_even.two_dvd.mul_left l
  refine ⟨(Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (kapB l ψ)).ker,
    { odd := hd.l_odd
      fin := ?_
      M_ne := mul_ne_zero hl.out.ne_zero (NeZero.ne N)
      l_dvd := dvd_mul_right _ _
      two_dvd := h2
      cc := hσ
      over_l := hB
      quad := ?_
      cyc := ⟨2, fun s hs => gh_dir_one _ (gh_cls_one 4 (pow_dvd_pow_of_dvd h2 2) hs), fun s hs => ?_⟩
      η_l := fun B' hB' s hs => gh_dir_one _ ((Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω 4).2.2.2.2 B'
        (gh_not_both B' (((Nat.coprime_primes hl.out Nat.prime_two).2 hd.l_odd).pow_right 2) hB') hs)
      even := ?_
      Ψ_l := gh_ine_l l c e ψ
      g4 := kernel_not_contained_eta l hd.l_odd c e ψ hd.four_le
      g5 := exists_decomposition_nonquadratic l c e ψ o₁ hd B hB
      Hb_open := Subgroup.isOpen_mono (fun s hs => gh_dir_one _ hs) (Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω N).1
      Hb_ab := Abelianization.commutator_subset_ker _
      Hb_idx := gh_idx l ψ
      Hb_l := fun B' hB' => ((compIne Ω N l hl.out B' hB').2 c.O _).2.2 ?_
      teich := fun B' hB' s hs h => gh_teich l c e ψ B' hB' hs.1 hs.2 h }⟩
  · haveI := (dvrFacts l c.O).2.2.1
    exact ResidueField.finite_of_finite (R := ℤ_[l]) (Finite.of_equiv _ PadicInt.residueField.symm.toEquiv)
  · obtain ⟨δ, h1, h3⟩ := gh_quad (Ω := Ω)
    exact ⟨1, δ, one_pos, fun q hq h => absurd (Nat.dvd_one.1 h) hq.one_lt.ne', h1, h3⟩
  · rw [gh_bar, gh_om_one l c.O (gh_cls_one _ (pow_dvd_pow_of_dvd_of_le (dvd_mul_right l N) one_le_two) hs), one_pow, one_mul,
      barChar, MonoidHom.comp_apply, gh_dir_one _ (gh_cls_one N ((dvd_mul_left N l).trans (dvd_pow_self _ two_ne_zero)) hs),
      map_one]
  · obtain ⟨w, hw, -⟩ := (omT Ω l c).1
    rw [barChar, MonoidHom.comp_apply, ((psiBar Ω l c w hw N ψ).2.1 e).1 hd.par σ hσ, map_one]
  · obtain ⟨-, hmul, -, hinv, -, h6, -⟩ := compAlg c.O N l hl.out
    rw [kapB, kapL, hmul, hinv, (h6 _).1, mul_inv_cancel]
