private theorem pow_two_pow (y b : ℤ) (t : ℕ) (ht : 2 ≤ t) (hb : Odd b) (hy : y = 1 + 2 ^ t * b) (j : ℕ) :
    ∃ b' : ℤ, Odd b' ∧ y ^ 2 ^ j = 1 + 2 ^ (t + j) * b' := by
  induction j with
  | zero => exact ⟨b, hb, by simpa using hy⟩
  | succ j ih =>
    obtain ⟨b', hb', h⟩ := ih
    obtain ⟨s, rfl⟩ := Nat.exists_eq_add_of_le ht
    refine ⟨b' + 2 * (2 ^ (s + j) * b' ^ 2), hb'.add_even (even_two_mul _), ?_⟩
    rw [pow_succ 2 j, pow_mul, h]
    ring

private theorem exists_pow_modEq (y b z : ℤ) (t : ℕ) (ht : 2 ≤ t) (hb : Odd b) (hy : y = 1 + 2 ^ t * b)
    (hz : (2 : ℤ) ^ t ∣ z - 1) (j : ℕ) : ∃ n : ℕ, (2 : ℤ) ^ (t + j) ∣ y ^ n - z := by
  induction j with
  | zero => exact ⟨0, by simpa using dvd_sub_comm.1 hz⟩
  | succ j ih =>
    obtain ⟨n, d, hd⟩ := ih
    obtain ⟨b', ⟨b₁, rfl⟩, h⟩ := pow_two_pow y b t ht hb hy j
    obtain ⟨s, rfl⟩ := Nat.exists_eq_add_of_le ht
    obtain ⟨w, hw⟩ := hz
    rcases Int.even_or_odd' d with ⟨d₁, rfl | rfl⟩
    · exact ⟨n, d₁, by rw [hd]; ring⟩
    · refine ⟨n + 2 ^ j, d₁ + b₁ + 1 + 2 ^ (s + 1) * w * (2 * b₁ + 1) + 2 ^ (s + j + 1) * (2 * d₁ + 1) * (2 * b₁ + 1), ?_⟩
      rw [pow_add, h, eq_add_of_sub_eq hd, eq_add_of_sub_eq hw]
      ring

private theorem cond_dvd_iff {O : Type} [CommRing O] {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) {M : ℕ} (h : M ∣ N) :
    ψ.conductor ∣ M ↔ ∀ u, ZMod.unitsMap h u = 1 → ψ.toUnitHom u = 1 := by
  rw [← DirichletCharacter.mem_conductorSet_iff_conductor_dvd _ h, DirichletCharacter.mem_conductorSet_iff,
    DirichletCharacter.factorsThrough_iff_ker_unitsMap h]
  rfl

private theorem pow_hom {O : Type} [CommRing O] {N : ℕ} (χ : DirichletCharacter O N) (k : ℕ) (u : (ZMod N)ˣ) :
    (χ ^ k).toUnitHom u = χ.toUnitHom u ^ k := by
  ext
  simp [MulChar.pow_apply_coe]

private theorem dyadic_character_power_ne_one {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] (ψ : DirichletCharacter O N) (p o : ℕ)
    (hp : Odd p) (hp1 : 1 < p) (ho : 1 ≤ o) (hR : 8 * o * p ^ 2 < 2 ^ c2 ψ) (u : (ZMod N)ˣ)
    (hu : ((ZMod.unitsMap (Nat.ordProj_dvd N 2) u : (ZMod (ordProj[2] N))ˣ) : ZMod (ordProj[2] N)) = p) :
    (psi2 ψ).toUnitHom u ^ (4 * o) ≠ 1 := by
  intro h
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, hc, hcN, -⟩ := compCond O N ψ 2 Nat.prime_two
  obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := (compOrd O N ψ 2 Nat.prime_two).2.1
  obtain ⟨j, m, hm, hom⟩ := Nat.exists_eq_two_pow_mul_odd (n := o) (by omega)
  have hp2 : 1 < p ^ 2 := Nat.one_lt_pow two_ne_zero hp1
  obtain ⟨s, b, hb, hsb⟩ := Nat.exists_eq_two_pow_mul_odd (n := p ^ 2 - 1) (by omega)
  have hs : 2 ≤ s := by
    obtain ⟨r, rfl⟩ := hp
    have h4 : 2 ^ 2 ∣ 2 ^ s * b := hsb ▸ ⟨r ^ 2 + r, by
      have : (2 * r + 1) ^ 2 = 2 ^ 2 * (r ^ 2 + r) + 1 := by ring
      omega⟩
    exact (Nat.pow_dvd_pow_iff_le_right one_lt_two).1
      ((Nat.Coprime.pow_left 2 (Nat.coprime_two_left.2 hb)).dvd_of_dvd_mul_right h4)
  have hy : ((p : ℤ) ^ 2) = 1 + 2 ^ s * (b : ℤ) := by
    have : p ^ 2 = 1 + 2 ^ s * b := by omega
    exact_mod_cast this
  obtain ⟨b', hb', hy'⟩ := pow_two_pow _ _ s hs (by exact_mod_cast hb) hy (j + 1)
  have hlt : s + (j + 1) < c2 ψ := by
    refine (Nat.pow_lt_pow_iff_right one_lt_two).1 (lt_of_le_of_lt ?_ hR)
    have h1 : 2 ^ s ≤ p ^ 2 := le_trans (Nat.le_mul_of_pos_right _ hb.pos) (by omega)
    have h2 : 2 ^ j ≤ o := hom ▸ Nat.le_mul_of_pos_right _ hm.pos
    calc 2 ^ (s + (j + 1)) = 2 * 2 ^ j * 2 ^ s := by ring
      _ ≤ 8 * o * p ^ 2 := by gcongr; norm_num
  have h1 : (psi2 ψ).toUnitHom (u ^ (2 * 2 ^ (j + 1))) = 1 := by
    have e1 : (psi2 ψ).toUnitHom (u ^ (2 * 2 ^ (j + 1))) ^ m = 1 := by
      rw [← h, map_pow, ← pow_mul, hom]; congr 1; ring
    have e2 : (psi2 ψ).toUnitHom (u ^ (2 * 2 ^ (j + 1))) ^ 2 ^ k = 1 := by
      rw [← pow_hom, ← hk, psi2, pow_orderOf_eq_one]; ext; simp
    exact (pow_eq_one_iff_of_coprime (Nat.Coprime.pow_left k (Nat.coprime_two_left.2 hm))).1 ⟨e2, e1⟩
  have hd : 2 ^ (s + (j + 1)) ∣ N := (pow_dvd_pow 2 hlt.le).trans hcN
  have hle : s + (j + 1) ≤ N.factorization 2 := (Nat.prime_two.pow_dvd_iff_le_factorization (NeZero.ne N)).1 hd
  obtain ⟨i, hi⟩ := Nat.exists_eq_add_of_le hle
  have hcd : (psi2 ψ).conductor ∣ 2 ^ (s + (j + 1)) := by
    refine (cond_dvd_iff _ hd).2 fun w hw => ?_
    have hz : (2 : ℤ) ^ (s + (j + 1)) ∣ (((w : ZMod N).val : ℕ) : ℤ) - 1 := by
      have e := congrArg Units.val hw
      rw [ZMod.unitsMap_val, ← ZMod.natCast_val, Units.val_one] at e
      have := (ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd ((((w : ZMod N).val : ℕ) : ℤ) - 1) (2 ^ (s + (j + 1)))).1 (by
        push_cast; rw [e, sub_self])
      exact_mod_cast this
    obtain ⟨n, hn⟩ := exists_pow_modEq _ b' _ _ (by omega) hb' hy' hz i
    rw [← hi] at hn
    have e : ZMod.unitsMap (Nat.ordProj_dvd N 2) ((u ^ (2 * 2 ^ (j + 1))) ^ n) = ZMod.unitsMap (Nat.ordProj_dvd N 2) w := by
      ext
      rw [map_pow, map_pow, Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, hu, ZMod.unitsMap_val, ← ZMod.natCast_val]
      have := (ZMod.intCast_zmod_eq_zero_iff_dvd _ (2 ^ N.factorization 2)).2 (dvd_trans (by simp) hn)
      rw [Int.cast_sub, sub_eq_zero, Int.cast_natCast] at this
      rw [← this, pow_mul]
      push_cast
      rfl
    have e' : proj 2 N ((u ^ (2 * 2 ^ (j + 1))) ^ n) = proj 2 N w := by
      rw [← MonoidHom.div_mem_ker_iff, (projFacts N 2 Nat.prime_two).2.2.2.2.2.2.1, MonoidHom.div_mem_ker_iff, e]
    have hA := (compAlg O N 2 Nat.prime_two).1 ψ
    rw [psi2, hA, ← e', ← hA, map_pow]
    rw [psi2] at h1
    rw [h1, one_pow]
  rw [← hc] at hcd
  exact absurd ((Nat.pow_dvd_pow_iff_le_right one_lt_two).1 hcd) (by omega)

private theorem teichmuller_power_eq {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {l e o : ℕ} (hl : l.Prime)
    {ψ : DirichletCharacter O N} (H : HostData l e ψ o) (hψ : 0 < orderOf ψ) : th0 l ψ ^ (4 * o) = psi2 ψ ^ (4 * o) := by
  obtain ⟨-, hmul, hone, -, hpow, -, hprod, -⟩ := compAlg O N 2 Nat.prime_two
  have hk : comp 2 (kap l ψ) = 1 := by
    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := (compOrd O N (kap l ψ) 2 Nat.prime_two).2.1
    have e1 : comp 2 (kap l ψ) ^ 2 ^ k = 1 := hk ▸ pow_orderOf_eq_one _
    have e2 : comp 2 (kap l ψ) ^ l ^ (orderOf ψ).factorization l = 1 := by
      rw [← hpow, ← (partOrd _ l hl ψ hψ).1, kap, pow_orderOf_eq_one, hone]
    exact (pow_eq_one_iff_of_coprime (Nat.Coprime.pow _ _ ((Nat.coprime_primes Nat.prime_two hl).2 H.l_odd.symm))).1 ⟨e1, e2⟩
  have h2 : psi2 ψ = comp 2 (th0 l ψ) := by
    rw [psi2]
    conv_lhs => rw [← dirichletPrimeToLPart_mul_lPowerPart l ψ, hmul, hk, mul_one]
  rw [h2]
  conv_lhs => rw [← hprod (th0 l ψ), ← Finset.prod_pow]
  refine Finset.prod_eq_single_of_mem 2 ?_ fun r hr h => ?_
  · exact Nat.mem_primeFactors.2 ⟨Nat.prime_two, H.N_even.two_dvd, NeZero.ne N⟩
  · rw [show 4 * o = 2 * o * 2 by ring, pow_mul, H.ord r (Nat.prime_of_mem_primeFactors hr) h, one_pow]

private theorem eq_one_of_congr {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {x : O} {n : ℕ} (hn : IsUnit (n : O)) (hx : x ^ n = 1)
    (h : residue O x = 1) : x = 1 := by
  have hu : IsUnit (∑ i ∈ Finset.range n, x ^ i) := by
    rw [← residue_ne_zero_iff_isUnit] at hn ⊢
    simpa [h] using hn
  have := geom_sum_mul x n
  rw [hx, sub_self] at this
  exact sub_eq_zero.1 (hu.mul_right_eq_zero.1 this)

private theorem res_ne {l : ℕ} [hl : Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e o : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N}
    (H : HostData l e ψ o) (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime) (hp2 : p ≠ 2) (hpT : p ∈ UpperTheory.V.HV.Tb l ψ) (u : (ZMod N)ˣ)
    (hu : ((ZMod.unitsMap (Nat.ordProj_dvd N 2) u : (ZMod (ordProj[2] N))ˣ) : ZMod (ordProj[2] N)) = p) :
    residue c.O (ψ.toUnitHom u : c.Oˣ) ^ 4 ≠ 1 := by
  intro h
  have hψ : 0 < orderOf ψ := orderOf_pos ψ
  obtain ⟨hk, -, hth, -⟩ := partOrd _ l hl.out ψ hψ
  set s := (orderOf ψ).factorization l
  have e0 : ψ ^ l ^ s = th0 l ψ ^ l ^ s := by
    conv_lhs => rw [← dirichletPrimeToLPart_mul_lPowerPart l ψ, mul_pow, ← hk, kap, pow_orderOf_eq_one, mul_one]
    rw [hk]
  have en : (th0 l ψ).toUnitHom u ^ orderOf (lPrime l ψ) = 1 := by
    rw [← pow_hom, th0, pow_orderOf_eq_one]; ext; simp
  have e1 : ((th0 l ψ).toUnitHom u ^ 4) ^ l ^ s = 1 := by
    ext1
    refine eq_one_of_congr ((UpperTheory.V.Base.dvrFacts l c.O).2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2 _ hth) ?_ ?_
    · rw [← Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, ← pow_mul, ← pow_mul, mul_comm _ (orderOf _), mul_comm 4, mul_assoc, pow_mul, en, one_pow,
        Units.val_one]
    · rw [pow_right_comm, ← pow_hom, ← e0, pow_hom, pow_right_comm, Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val,
        map_pow, map_pow, h, one_pow]
  have e2 : (th0 l ψ).toUnitHom u ^ 4 = 1 :=
    (pow_eq_one_iff_of_coprime (Nat.Coprime.pow_left s (hl.out.coprime_iff_not_dvd.2 hth))).1
      ⟨e1, by rw [pow_right_comm, en, one_pow]⟩
  refine dyadic_character_power_ne_one ψ p o (hp.odd_of_ne_two hp2) hp.one_lt H.o_pos (H.R p hpT) u hu ?_
  rw [← pow_hom, ← teichmuller_power_eq hl.out H hψ, pow_hom, pow_mul, e2, one_pow]

private theorem exists_dec (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [hl : Fact l.Prime] (p : ℕ) (hp : p.Prime)
    (hpl : p = l ∨ (p ≠ l ∧ p ≡ 1 [MOD l])) (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (hA : A.valuation (p : Ω) < 1) :
    ∃ σ ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) A, (∀ m : ℕ, m.Coprime p → ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ m = 1 → σ ζ = ζ ^ p) ∧
      PadicInt.toZModPow 1 (cycZ ℚ Ω l σ : ℤ_[l]) = 1 := by
  have : Fact p.Prime := ⟨hp⟩
  have : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
  obtain ⟨γ, hγ, hγ'⟩ := UpperTheory.V.LDataCardLeFinrank.decFrob Ω p A hA
  rcases hpl with rfl | ⟨h1, h2⟩
  · obtain ⟨τ, hτ, hτ'⟩ := GlobalTheory.CycInertia.cycZ_Ine_surjective Ω p A hA (cycZ ℚ Ω p γ)⁻¹
    refine ⟨γ * τ, mul_mem hγ (Ine_le_Dec A hτ), fun m hm ζ hζ => ?_, ?_⟩
    · rw [AlgEquiv.mul_apply, (UpperTheory.V.LDataCardLeFinrank.ineFixRoots ℚ Ω p A hA m hm ζ hζ).2 τ hτ, hγ' m hm ζ hζ]
    · rw [map_mul, hτ', mul_inv_cancel, Units.val_one, map_one]
  · refine ⟨γ, hγ, hγ', ?_⟩
    have : NeZero (l ^ 1) := ⟨pow_ne_zero _ hl.out.ne_zero⟩
    have : ∀ i, HasEnoughRootsOfUnity Ω (l ^ i) := fun i => by
      have : NeZero (l ^ i) := ⟨pow_ne_zero _ hl.out.ne_zero⟩
      have : NeZero ((l ^ i : ℕ) : Ω) := ⟨Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr (NeZero.ne _)⟩
      infer_instance
    obtain ⟨ζ, hζ⟩ := HasEnoughRootsOfUnity.exists_primitiveRoot Ω (l ^ 1)
    have ha : γ ζ = ζ ^ (PadicInt.toZModPow 1 (cycZ ℚ Ω l γ : ℤ_[l])).val :=
      cyclotomicCharacter.spec l γ.toRingEquiv ζ hζ.pow_eq_one
    rw [hγ' _ (((Nat.coprime_primes hl.out hp).2 h1.symm).pow_left 1) ζ hζ.pow_eq_one,
      (hζ.isOfFinOrder (NeZero.ne _)).pow_eq_pow_iff_modEq, ← hζ.eq_orderOf, ← ZMod.natCast_eq_natCast_iff,
      ZMod.natCast_zmod_val] at ha
    rw [← ha]
    exact ((ZMod.natCast_eq_natCast_iff p 1 (l ^ 1)).2 (by rwa [pow_one])).trans Nat.cast_one

private theorem res_one (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O]
    (hloc : IsLocalHom (algebraMap ℤ_[l] O)) (x : ℤ_[l]) (hx : PadicInt.toZModPow 1 x = 1) :
    residue O (algebraMap ℤ_[l] O x) = 1 := by
  have h5 : x - 1 ∈ maximalIdeal ℤ_[l] := by
    rw [PadicInt.maximalIdeal_eq_span_p, ← pow_one (l : ℤ_[l]), ← PadicInt.ker_toZModPow, RingHom.mem_ker, map_sub, hx,
      map_one, sub_self]
  have h6 := (residue_eq_zero_iff _).2 (_root_.map_nonunit (algebraMap ℤ_[l] O) _ h5)
  rwa [map_sub, map_sub, map_one, map_one, sub_eq_zero] at h6

theorem UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.exists_sigma :
    ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (o₁ : ℕ), HostData l e ψ o₁ →
    ∀ (S : Finset Pl) (A : S → ValuationSubring Ω) (F : Frame c.O (Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual.G ℚ Ω) (Gam Ω S) S), IsHostFrame Ω c e ψ S A F →
    ∀ v ∈ F.Tb, ∃ x : F.D v, F.ηv v x = 1 ∧ IsUnit ((F.Ψv v x : c.O) ^ 2 - 1) ∧ cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 x = 1 := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l hl c N _ e ψ o H S A F hF v hv
  obtain ⟨p, hp, hp2, hpT, hpl, hpv⟩ : ∃ p : ℕ, p.Prime ∧ p ≠ 2 ∧ p ∈ UpperTheory.V.HV.Tb l ψ ∧ (p = l ∨ (p ≠ l ∧ p ≡ 1 [MOD l])) ∧
      ((p : ℕ) : NumberField.RingOfIntegers ℚ) ∈ v.1.asIdeal := by
    rcases hv with rfl | hv
    · exact ⟨l, hl.out, H.l_odd, Finset.mem_insert_self _ _, Or.inl rfl, hF.vl⟩
    · obtain ⟨p, hp, hpv⟩ := (hF.Sb v).1 hv
      have hp' := hp
      simp only [UpperTheory.V.HV.Sb, Finset.mem_filter] at hp'
      obtain ⟨h1, h2, h3⟩ := hp'
      have hpp := Nat.prime_of_mem_primeFactors h1
      have h4 := ((compOrd c.O N (kap l ψ) p hpp).2.2.2 l ((orderOf ψ).factorization l) hl.out h2.symm
        (by rw [← (partOrd _ l hl.out ψ (orderOf_pos ψ)).1, kap, pow_orderOf_eq_one]) h3).1
      refine ⟨p, hpp, ?_, Finset.mem_insert_of_mem hp, Or.inr ⟨h2, h4⟩, hpv⟩
      rintro rfl
      exact hl.out.one_lt.ne' (Nat.dvd_one.1 ((Nat.modEq_iff_dvd' one_le_two).1 h4.symm))
  have hA : (A v).valuation (p : Ω) < 1 := by simpa using (hF.isOver v _).2 hpv
  obtain ⟨σ, hσ, h1, h2⟩ := exists_dec Ω l p hp hpl (A v) hA
  have hx : σ ^ 2 ∈ F.D v := hF.D v ▸ pow_mem hσ 2
  refine ⟨⟨σ ^ 2, hx⟩, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · show (F.η.comp F.π) (σ ^ 2) = 1
    rw [map_pow, Int.units_sq]
  · show IsUnit ((((F.Ψ.comp F.π) (σ ^ 2) : c.Oˣ) : c.O) ^ 2 - 1)
    have : NeZero (ordProj[2] N) := ⟨(Nat.ordProj_pos N 2).ne'⟩
    have hcop : (ordProj[2] N).Coprime p := ((Nat.coprime_primes Nat.prime_two hp).2 hp2.symm).pow_left _
    have hu : ZMod.unitsMap (Nat.ordProj_dvd N 2) (Chr.cls ℚ Ω N σ) = ZMod.unitOfCoprime p hcop.symm := by
      refine (DFunLike.congr_fun ((Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω N).2.1 _ (Nat.ordProj_dvd N 2)) σ).trans ?_
      refine (Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω _).2.2.1 σ _ fun ζ hζ => ?_
      rw [ZMod.coe_unitOfCoprime, ZMod.val_natCast, ← pow_eq_pow_mod _ hζ, h1 _ hcop ζ hζ]
    have h3 := res_ne c H p hp hp2 hpT (Chr.cls ℚ Ω N σ) (by rw [hu, ZMod.coe_unitOfCoprime])
    have h4 : residue c.O (UpperTheory.V.Base.chiO ℚ Ω l c.O σ : c.Oˣ) = 1 := by
      exact res_one l c.O (UpperTheory.V.Base.dvrFacts l c.O).2.2.1 _ h2
    rw [← residue_ne_zero_iff_isUnit, hF.Ψ, map_pow, Chr.Psi, zpow_natCast, MonoidHom.mul_apply, MonoidHom.pow_apply]
    simp only [Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, Units.val_mul, map_sub, map_pow, map_mul, map_one, h4, one_pow, one_mul, ← pow_mul]
    exact sub_ne_zero.2 h3
  · show cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 (σ ^ 2) = 1
    have : cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 σ = 1 := Units.ext h2
    rw [map_pow, this, one_pow]


[AFE_SRC_HOST_FRAME] Original actual GS/toGS auxiliary Host-frame construction
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeDeformations/HostEx.lean#L1-L113
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeDeformations.IsHostFrame
import Development.AbelianVarietyTheory.PublishedTheorems.GaloisCohomology₂.PoitouTateNormalised
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CharacterLevelBounds.ClsFacts
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.PlaceDict
import Formalism.GaloisCohomology.GaloisCohomologyProofs.LdataNonempty

open Matrix IsLocalRing ReciprocityTheory.Gal UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia

section

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Base

private theorem prime_l {l : ℕ} (hl : l.Prime) : Prime ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) := by
  rw [← MulEquiv.prime_iff Rat.ringOfIntegersEquiv, map_natCast]
  exact Nat.prime_iff_prime_int.mp hl

private noncomputable def vQ {l : ℕ} (hl : l.Prime) : Pl where
  asIdeal := Ideal.span {((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ)}
  isPrime := (Ideal.span_singleton_prime (prime_l hl).ne_zero).mpr (prime_l hl)
  ne_bot := by rw [Ne, Ideal.span_singleton_eq_bot]; exact (prime_l hl).ne_zero

private theorem fin_above {n : ℕ} (hn : n ≠ 0) : {v : Pl | ((n : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) ∈ v.asIdeal}.Finite := by
  have hI : (Ideal.span {((n : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ)} : Ideal (𝓞 ℚ)) ≠ 0 := by
    rw [ne_eq, Ideal.zero_eq_bot, Ideal.span_singleton_eq_bot]
    exact_mod_cast hn
  refine (Ideal.finite_factors hI).subset ?_
  intro v hv
  simp only [Set.mem_setOf_eq] at hv ⊢
  rw [Ideal.dvd_iff_le, Ideal.span_le, Set.singleton_subset_iff]
  exact hv

private theorem cls_exists (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (M : ℕ) [NeZero M] (S : Set Pl)
    (hS : ∀ v ∉ S, ((M : ℕ) : 𝓞 ℚ) ∉ v.asIdeal) :
    ∃ μ : GS Ω S →* (ZMod M)ˣ, μ.comp (toGS Ω S) = Chr.cls ℚ Ω M := by
  obtain ⟨hopen, -, -, -, hine⟩ := Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω M
  obtain ⟨-, -, -, hpl⟩ := Kum.placeDict ℚ Ω
  have hle : NS Ω S ≤ (Chr.cls ℚ Ω M).ker := by
    apply Subgroup.topologicalClosure_minimal _ _ (Subgroup.isClosed_of_isOpen _ hopen)
    apply Subgroup.normalClosure_le_normal
    intro σ hσ
    simp only [Set.mem_iUnion, Set.mem_setOf_eq, Set.mem_compl_iff] at hσ
    obtain ⟨v, hv, A, hA, hσA⟩ := hσ
    have hn : ¬ A.valuation ((M : ℕ) : Ω) < 1 := by
      rw [hpl A v M hA]
      exact hS v hv
    exact hine A hn hσA
  exact ⟨QuotientGroup.lift (NS Ω S) (Chr.cls ℚ Ω M) hle, QuotientGroup.lift_comp_mk' _ _ _⟩

private theorem cc_exists (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] : ∃ σ : G ℚ Ω, IsCC σ := by
  obtain ⟨σ, j, hj⟩ := exists_isConjOm (E := ℚ) (Ω := Ω)
  exact ⟨AlgEquiv.ofRingEquiv (f := σ) (fun q => by simp), j, fun x => hj x⟩

end

theorem UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia.exists_hostFrame :
    ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (o₁ : ℕ), HostData l e ψ o₁ →
    ∃ (S : Finset Pl) (A : S → ValuationSubring Ω) (F : Frame c.O (Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual.G ℚ Ω) (Gam Ω S) S), IsHostFrame Ω c e ψ S A F := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l _ c N _ e ψ o₁ hd
  have hl : l.Prime := Fact.out
  have hlN : l * N ≠ 0 := Nat.mul_ne_zero hl.ne_zero (NeZero.ne N)
  set S : Finset Pl := (fin_above hlN).toFinset with hSdef
  have hmem : ∀ v : Pl, v ∈ S ↔ (((l * N : ℕ)) : NumberField.RingOfIntegers ℚ) ∈ v.asIdeal :=
    fun v => Set.Finite.mem_toFinset _
  have hout : ∀ m : ℕ, m ∣ l * N → ∀ v ∉ (S : Set Pl),
      ((m : ℕ) : NumberField.RingOfIntegers ℚ) ∉ v.asIdeal := by
    intro m hm v hv hmv
    apply hv
    rw [Finset.mem_coe, hmem]
    obtain ⟨k, hk⟩ := hm
    rw [hk, Nat.cast_mul]
    exact v.asIdeal.mul_mem_right _ hmv
  have h4 : ∀ v ∉ (S : Set Pl), ((4 : ℕ) : NumberField.RingOfIntegers ℚ) ∉ v.asIdeal := by
    intro v hv h4v
    have h2 : ((2 : ℕ) : NumberField.RingOfIntegers ℚ) ∈ v.asIdeal := by
      have h22 : ((4 : ℕ) : NumberField.RingOfIntegers ℚ) =
          ((2 : ℕ) : NumberField.RingOfIntegers ℚ) * ((2 : ℕ) : NumberField.RingOfIntegers ℚ) := by
        rw [← Nat.cast_mul]
      rw [h22] at h4v
      rcases v.isPrime.mem_or_mem h4v with h | h <;> exact h
    exact hout 2 (dvd_mul_of_dvd_right (even_iff_two_dvd.mp hd.N_even) l) v hv h2
  obtain ⟨μN, hμN⟩ := cls_exists Ω N (S : Set Pl) (hout N (dvd_mul_left N l))
  obtain ⟨μ4, hμ4⟩ := cls_exists Ω 4 (S : Set Pl) h4
  obtain ⟨χl, hχl⟩ :=
    Formalism.GaloisCohomology.chi_exists (F := ℚ) (Ω := Ω) l (S : Set Pl) (hout l (dvd_mul_right l N))
  obtain ⟨σ, hσ⟩ := cc_exists Ω
  let A : S → ValuationSubring Ω := fun v => Classical.choose (exists_isPlaceOver Ω (v : Pl))
  have hA := fun v : S => Classical.choose_spec (exists_isPlaceOver Ω (v : Pl))
  have hvl : vQ hl ∈ S := by
    rw [hmem]
    exact Ideal.mem_span_singleton.mpr ⟨(N : NumberField.RingOfIntegers ℚ), by push_cast; ring⟩
  let F : Frame c.O (Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual.G ℚ Ω) (Gam Ω S) S :=
    { π := toGS Ω (S : Set Pl)
      η := (MulChar.toUnitHom ZMod.χ₄).comp μ4
      Ψ := ((Units.map (algebraMap ℤ_[l] c.O : ℤ_[l] →* c.O)).comp χl) ^ e *
        (MulChar.toUnitHom ψ).comp μN
      c := toGS Ω (S : Set Pl) σ
      D := fun v => Dec (A v)
      I := fun v => Ine (A v)
      vl := ⟨vQ hl, hvl⟩
      Sb := {v | ∃ p ∈ Sb l ψ, (p : NumberField.RingOfIntegers ℚ) ∈ (v : Pl).asIdeal} }
  refine ⟨S, A, F, ?_⟩
  refine ⟨hmem, hA, rfl, ?_, ?_, ⟨σ, hσ, rfl⟩, fun _ => rfl, fun _ => rfl, ?_, fun _ => Iff.rfl⟩
  · show ((MulChar.toUnitHom ZMod.χ₄).comp μ4).comp (toGS Ω (S : Set Pl)) = eta4 Ω
    rw [MonoidHom.comp_assoc, hμ4]
    unfold eta4 UpperTheory.V.Chr.dirG
    rfl
  · show (((Units.map (algebraMap ℤ_[l] c.O : ℤ_[l] →* c.O)).comp χl) ^ e *
        (MulChar.toUnitHom ψ).comp μN).comp (toGS Ω (S : Set Pl)) = _
    unfold UpperTheory.V.Chr.Psi UpperTheory.V.Chr.dirG UpperTheory.V.Base.chiO
    rw [← hχl, ← hμN]
    ext τ
    simp
  · exact Ideal.mem_span_singleton_self _


[AFE_SRC_CYCLOTOMIC] Exact finite cyclotomic action and inertia dictionary
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CharacterLevelBounds/ClsFacts.lean#L1-L143
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CharacterLevelBounds.ShaFamMono
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.PartsSign

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.GaloisCohomology UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Chr

private theorem exists_primitiveRoot_of_isAlgClosure' (k Ω : Type) [Field k] [NumberField k] [Field Ω] [Algebra k Ω]
    [IsAlgClosure k Ω] (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] : ∃ ζ : Ω, IsPrimitiveRoot ζ N := by
  haveI : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed k
  haveI : CharZero Ω := charZero_of_injective_algebraMap (algebraMap k Ω).injective
  exact HasEnoughRootsOfUnity.exists_primitiveRoot Ω N

private theorem cls_apply_root_of_unity_eq_pow (k Ω : Type) [Field k] [NumberField k] [Field Ω] [Algebra k Ω]
    [IsAlgClosure k Ω] (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (σ : G k Ω) (ζ : Ω) (hζ : ζ ^ N = 1) :
    σ ζ = ζ ^ ((cls k Ω N σ : (ZMod N)ˣ) : ZMod N).val := by
  have h0 : ζ ≠ 0 := by
    rintro rfl
    simp [NeZero.ne N] at hζ
  have ht : Units.mk0 ζ h0 ∈ rootsOfUnity N Ω := by
    rw [mem_rootsOfUnity]
    ext
    simpa using hζ
  obtain ⟨ξ, hξ⟩ := exists_primitiveRoot_of_isAlgClosure' k Ω N
  have := modularCyclotomicCharacter.spec Ω hξ.card_rootsOfUnity
    (MulSemiringAction.toRingAut (G k Ω) Ω σ) ht
  exact this

private theorem cls_eq_of_forall_root_of_unity_apply_eq_pow (k Ω : Type) [Field k] [NumberField k] [Field Ω] [Algebra k Ω]
    [IsAlgClosure k Ω] (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (σ : G k Ω) (u : (ZMod N)ˣ)
    (h : ∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ N = 1 → σ ζ = ζ ^ (u : ZMod N).val) : cls k Ω N σ = u := by
  obtain ⟨ζ, hζ⟩ := exists_primitiveRoot_of_isAlgClosure' k Ω N
  ext1
  refine (modularCyclotomicCharacter.unique Ω hζ.card_rootsOfUnity
    (MulSemiringAction.toRingAut (G k Ω) Ω σ) (c := (u : ZMod N)) ?_).symm
  intro t ht
  rw [mem_rootsOfUnity] at ht
  have ht' : (t : Ω) ^ N = 1 := by rw [← Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val, ht, Units.val_one]
  exact h t ht'

private theorem root_of_unity_pow_natCast_zmod_val {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (ζ : Ω) (hζ : ζ ^ N = 1)
    (b : ℕ) : ζ ^ ((b : ZMod N)).val = ζ ^ b := by
  rw [ZMod.val_natCast]
  conv_rhs => rw [← Nat.mod_add_div b N, pow_add, pow_mul, hζ, one_pow, mul_one]

private theorem cls_eq_one_of_fixes_primitiveRoot (k Ω : Type) [Field k] [NumberField k] [Field Ω] [Algebra k Ω]
    [IsAlgClosure k Ω] (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (σ : G k Ω) (ζ : Ω) (hζ : IsPrimitiveRoot ζ N)
    (hσ : σ ζ = ζ) : cls k Ω N σ = 1 := by
  apply cls_eq_of_forall_root_of_unity_apply_eq_pow
  intro ξ hξ
  obtain ⟨i, -, rfl⟩ := hζ.eq_pow_of_pow_eq_one hξ
  have h1 := root_of_unity_pow_natCast_zmod_val N _ hξ 1
  rw [Nat.cast_one, pow_one] at h1
  rw [map_pow, hσ, Units.val_one, h1]

theorem UpperTheory.V.Chr.clsFacts :
    ∀ (k Ω : Type) [Field k] [NumberField k] [Field Ω] [Algebra k Ω] [IsAlgClosure k Ω] (N : ℕ) [NeZero N],
          IsOpen ((cls k Ω N).ker : Set (G k Ω)) ∧ (∀ (M : ℕ) [NeZero M] (h : M ∣ N), (ZMod.unitsMap h).comp (cls k Ω N) = cls k Ω M) ∧
          (∀ (σ : G k Ω) (u : (ZMod N)ˣ), (∀ ζ : Ω, ζ ^ N = 1 → σ ζ = ζ ^ (u : ZMod N).val) → cls k Ω N σ = u) ∧
          (∀ σ : G k Ω, IsConjOm σ.toRingEquiv → cls k Ω N σ = -1) ∧
          ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (N : Ω) < 1 → Ine (F := k) A ≤ (cls k Ω N).ker := by
  intro k Ω _ _ _ _ _ N _
  refine ⟨?_, ?_, fun σ u h => cls_eq_of_forall_root_of_unity_apply_eq_pow k Ω N σ u h, ?_, ?_⟩
  · obtain ⟨ζ, hζ⟩ := exists_primitiveRoot_of_isAlgClosure' k Ω N
    haveI : Algebra.IsAlgebraic k Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgebraic
    have hi : IsIntegral k ζ := (Algebra.IsAlgebraic.isAlgebraic ζ).isIntegral
    haveI : FiniteDimensional k (IntermediateField.adjoin k {ζ}) :=
      IntermediateField.adjoin.finiteDimensional hi
    refine Subgroup.isOpen_mono ?_
      (IntermediateField.fixingSubgroup_isOpen (IntermediateField.adjoin k {ζ}))
    intro σ hσ
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker]
    exact cls_eq_one_of_fixes_primitiveRoot k Ω N σ ζ hζ ((IntermediateField.mem_fixingSubgroup_iff _ _).1 hσ ζ
      (IntermediateField.mem_adjoin_simple_self k ζ))
  · intro M _ hMN
    ext1 σ
    symm
    apply cls_eq_of_forall_root_of_unity_apply_eq_pow
    intro ζ hζ
    rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, ZMod.unitsMap_val, ZMod.cast_eq_val, root_of_unity_pow_natCast_zmod_val M ζ hζ]
    obtain ⟨d, hd⟩ := hMN
    exact cls_apply_root_of_unity_eq_pow k Ω N σ ζ (by rw [hd, pow_mul, hζ, one_pow])
  · intro σ hσ
    obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hσ
    apply cls_eq_of_forall_root_of_unity_apply_eq_pow
    intro ζ hζ
    have hN1 : 1 ≤ N := Nat.one_le_iff_ne_zero.2 (NeZero.ne N)
    have h0 : ζ ≠ 0 := by
      rintro rfl
      simp [NeZero.ne N] at hζ
    have hval : ((-1 : (ZMod N)ˣ) : ZMod N) = ((N - 1 : ℕ) : ZMod N) := by
      rw [Units.val_neg, Units.val_one, Nat.cast_sub hN1, ZMod.natCast_self, Nat.cast_one, zero_sub]
    rw [hval, root_of_unity_pow_natCast_zmod_val N ζ hζ, pow_sub₀ ζ h0 hN1, hζ, pow_one, one_mul]
    apply j.injective
    have hjζ := hj ζ
    simp only [AlgEquiv.toRingEquiv_eq_coe, AlgEquiv.coe_ringEquiv] at hjζ
    rw [hjζ, map_inv₀]
    have hz : (j ζ) ^ N = 1 := by rw [← map_pow, hζ, map_one]
    have hn : ‖j ζ‖ = 1 := Complex.norm_eq_one_of_pow_eq_one hz (NeZero.ne N)
    rw [Complex.inv_def, Complex.normSq_eq_norm_sq, hn]
    simp
  · intro A hA σ hσ
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker]
    obtain ⟨ζ, hζ⟩ := exists_primitiveRoot_of_isAlgClosure' k Ω N
    apply cls_eq_one_of_fixes_primitiveRoot k Ω N σ ζ hζ
    have hin : ∀ x : Ω, A.valuation x ≤ 1 → A.valuation (σ x - x) < 1 := hσ.2
    by_contra hne
    have h0 : ζ ≠ 0 := hζ.ne_zero (NeZero.ne N)
    set ξ := σ ζ * ζ⁻¹ with hξdef
    have hξN : ξ ^ N = 1 := by
      rw [hξdef, mul_pow, inv_pow, ← map_pow, hζ.pow_eq_one, map_one, one_mul, inv_one]
    have hξ1 : ξ ≠ 1 := by
      intro h
      apply hne
      rw [hξdef, mul_inv_eq_one₀ h0] at h
      exact h
    have hv1 : ∀ x : Ω, x ^ N = 1 → A.valuation x = 1 := by
      intro x hx
      have hp : A.valuation x ^ N = 1 := by rw [← map_pow, hx, map_one]
      exact (pow_eq_one_iff.1 hp).resolve_right (NeZero.ne N)
    have hsum : ∑ i ∈ Finset.range N, ξ ^ i = 0 := by
      have hg := geom_sum_mul ξ N
      rw [hξN, sub_self] at hg
      exact (mul_eq_zero.1 hg).resolve_right (sub_ne_zero.2 hξ1)
    have hNeq : (N : Ω) = (1 - ξ) * ∑ i ∈ Finset.range N, ∑ j ∈ Finset.range i, ξ ^ j := by
      rw [Finset.mul_sum]
      simp_rw [mul_neg_geom_sum]
      rw [Finset.sum_sub_distrib, hsum]
      simp
    have hξA : ξ ∈ A := (A.valuation_le_one_iff ξ).1 (hv1 ξ hξN).le
    have hSA : (∑ i ∈ Finset.range N, ∑ j ∈ Finset.range i, ξ ^ j) ∈ A :=
      sum_mem fun i _ => sum_mem fun j _ => pow_mem hξA j
    have hS : A.valuation (∑ i ∈ Finset.range N, ∑ j ∈ Finset.range i, ξ ^ j) ≤ 1 :=
      (A.valuation_le_one_iff _).2 hSA
    have hdiff : σ ζ - ζ = -(ζ * (1 - ξ)) := by
      rw [hξdef]
      field_simp
      ring
    have hlt : A.valuation (1 - ξ) < 1 := by
      have := hin ζ (hv1 ζ hζ.pow_eq_one).le
      rw [hdiff, Valuation.map_neg, map_mul, hv1 ζ hζ.pow_eq_one, one_mul] at this
      exact this
    apply hA
    rw [hNeq, map_mul]
    exact lt_of_le_of_lt (mul_le_of_le_one_right' hS) hlt


[AFE_SRC_DAT_FACTS] Source context for original Dat facts; full dat_facts is not separately claimed

open UpperTheory.V UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3

private theorem locConst {Γ A : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [Group A] (ψ : Γ →* A)
    (h : IsOpen (ψ.ker : Set Γ)) : IsLocallyConstant ψ :=
  _root_.UpperTheory.Reformulated.B.LocallyConstantOfKernel.lc_of_ker ψ h

private theorem open_galOf (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K]
    [Algebra K Ω] : IsOpen (galOf ℚ K Ω : Set (G ℚ Ω)) := by
  let b := Module.finBasis ℚ K
  have e : (galOf ℚ K Ω : Set (G ℚ Ω)) = ⋂ i, {σ : G ℚ Ω | σ (algebraMap K Ω (b i)) = algebraMap K Ω (b i)} := by
    ext σ
    simp only [Set.mem_iInter, Set.mem_setOf_eq, SetLike.mem_coe, mem_galOf]
    refine ⟨fun h i => h _, fun h x => ?_⟩
    have := b.ext (f₁ := σ.toLinearMap ∘ₗ (IsScalarTower.toAlgHom ℚ K Ω).toLinearMap)
      (f₂ := (IsScalarTower.toAlgHom ℚ K Ω).toLinearMap) h
    exact LinearMap.congr_fun this x
  rw [e]
  exact isOpen_iInter_of_finite fun i => isOpen_setOf_apply (F := ℚ) _ (fun t => t = algebraMap K Ω (b i))

private theorem cyc_ine (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime]
    (A : ValuationSubring Ω) (hA : ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1) {σ : G ℚ Ω} (hσ : σ ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A) :
    cycZ ℚ Ω l σ = 1 := by
  have : IsAlgClosed Ω := IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ
  have : NeZero ((l : ℕ) : Ω) := ⟨Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr (Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero⟩
  show cyclotomicCharacter Ω l σ.toRingEquiv = 1
  refine Units.ext (PadicInt.ext_of_toZModPow.mp fun n => ?_)
  obtain ⟨ζ, hζ⟩ := HasEnoughRootsOfUnity.exists_primitiveRoot Ω (l ^ n)
  have hN0 : l ^ n ≠ 0 := pow_ne_zero _ (Fact.out : l.Prime).ne_zero
  have : NeZero (l ^ n) := ⟨hN0⟩
  have hspec := cyclotomicCharacter.spec (L := Ω) l σ.toRingEquiv ζ hζ.pow_eq_one
  have hv : ¬ A.valuation ((l ^ n : ℕ) : Ω) < 1 := by
    rw [Nat.cast_pow, map_pow, not_lt]
    exact one_le_pow₀ (not_lt.1 hA)
  have h1 : Chr.cls ℚ Ω (l ^ n) σ = 1 := (Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω (l ^ n)).2.2.2.2 A hv hσ
  have h2 := Chr.cls_spec (l ^ n) σ ζ hζ.pow_eq_one
  rw [h1] at h2
  have h3 := hζ.pow_inj (ZMod.val_lt _) (ZMod.val_lt _) (hspec.symm.trans h2)
  rw [ZMod.val_injective _ h3]
  simp

private theorem tor_eq_one {R : Type} [CommRing R] {t : Rˣ} (h : tor R t = 1) : t = 1 := by
  have h1 := congrArg (fun x : GL (Fin 3) R => (x : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) 0 0) h
  apply Units.ext
  simpa [tor_val, wt1] using h1

private theorem diagonal_rep_eq_one_iff {Γ R : Type} [Group Γ] [CommRing R] (η : Γ →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : Γ →* Rˣ) (g : Γ) :
    r0 η Ψ g = 1 ↔ η g = 1 ∧ Ψ g = 1 := by
  refine ⟨fun h => ⟨congrArg SemidirectProduct.right h, tor_eq_one (congrArg SemidirectProduct.left h)⟩, fun h => ?_⟩
  refine SemidirectProduct.ext ?_ h.1
  rw [unitaryThreeReflection_left, h.2, map_one]
  rfl

private theorem bar_lc {O Γ : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] (Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ)
    (hΨ : CharCont Ψ) : IsLocallyConstant (barChar O Ψ) := by
  refine IsLocallyConstant.desc _ Units.val ?_ Units.val_injective
  have h := (hΨ 1 1).comp (Ideal.Quotient.factor (pow_one (maximalIdeal O)).le)
  simp only [zpow_one] at h
  exact h

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.dat_facts :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {K : Type} [Field K] [NumberField K] [Algebra K Ω]
      {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {c : Coef l} {N : ℕ} [NeZero N] {e : ℕ} {ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N} (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ),
      D.cc * D.cc = 1 ∧ D.η D.cc = -1 ∧ D.Ψ D.cc = 1 ∧ IsLocallyConstant D.η ∧ IsLocallyConstant (barChar c.O D.Ψ) ∧
      (∀ g ∈ D.Kill, r0 D.η D.Ψ g = 1) ∧ IsUnit (2 : c.O) ∧ IsCMField K ∧ Continuous D.ΨQ ∧
      (∀ g, g ∈ D.Kill ↔ ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧ g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A ∧ D.η g = 1 ∧ Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ g = 1) ∧
      (∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → Ine (F := ℚ) A ≤ D.η.ker ∧
        ∃ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A, IsUnit ((D.Ψ s : c.O) ^ 2 - 1)) := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ l hl c N _ e ψ D
  obtain ⟨w, η', a, hlit⟩ := (gdIff Ω K l c e N ψ).1.1 D.gd
  have himag := D.gd.imag
  have hΨ : ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A,
      D.Ψ g = Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ g := fun A hA g hg => by
    show (chiO ℚ Ω l c.O g) ^ (e : ℤ) * Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ g = _
    have : chiO ℚ Ω l c.O g = 1 := by
      unfold chiO
      rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, cyc_ine Ω l A hA hg, map_one]
    rw [this, _root_.one_zpow, one_mul]
  have hkill : ∀ g, g ∈ D.Kill ↔ ∃ A : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ A.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧ g ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A ∧
      D.η g = 1 ∧ Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ g = 1 := fun g => by
    refine exists_congr fun A => and_congr_right fun hA => and_congr_right fun hg => ?_
    unfold rbar
    rw [diagonal_rep_eq_one_iff]
    refine and_congr_right fun _ => ?_
    unfold barChar
    rw [MonoidHom.comp_apply, hΨ A hA g hg]
    refine ⟨fun h => ?_, fun h => by rw [h, map_one]⟩
    refine (congrFacts Ω l c).2.2.2.1 _ 1 _ hlit.ord ?_ (one_pow _) ?_
    · rw [← MonoidHom.pow_apply, pow_orderOf_eq_one, MonoidHom.one_apply]
    · rw [← residue_eq_zero_iff, map_sub, sub_eq_zero, Units.val_one, map_one]
      exact congrArg Units.val h
  obtain ⟨φ, hφ⟩ := D.conj
  refine ⟨?_, ?_, hlit.par D.cc ⟨φ, hφ⟩, ?_, ?_, fun g hg => ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_, hkill, fun A hA => ?_⟩
  · refine AlgEquiv.ext fun x => φ.injective ?_
    rw [AlgEquiv.mul_apply, hφ, hφ]
    simp
  · refine (Int.units_eq_one_or _).resolve_left fun h => ?_
    have h1 : D.cc ∈ galOf ℚ K Ω := D.η_ker ▸ h
    refine IsTotallyComplex.complexEmbedding_not_isReal (φ.comp (algebraMap K Ω)) ?_
    rw [ComplexEmbedding.isReal_iff]
    ext x
    rw [ComplexEmbedding.conjugate_coe_eq, RingHom.comp_apply, ← hφ, h1 x]
  · exact locConst _ (D.η_ker ▸ open_galOf Ω K)
  · exact bar_lc _ D.Ψ_cont
  · obtain ⟨A, hA, hg, h1, h2⟩ := (hkill g).1 hg
    exact (diagonal_rep_eq_one_iff _ _ _).2 ⟨h1, (hΨ A hA g hg).trans h2⟩
  · have := (dvrFacts l c.O).2.2.2.2.2.2.2.2 2 fun h => D.gd.l_odd ((Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq hl.out Nat.prime_two).1 h)
    simpa using this
  · have : Algebra.IsQuadraticExtension ℚ K := ⟨D.gd.quad⟩
    exact IsCMField.ofCMExtension ℚ K
  · have hf : Continuous (algebraMap ℤ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom := by
      refine ((continuous_algebraMap ℚ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l)).comp continuous_subtype_val).congr fun x => ?_
      exact (IsScalarTower.algebraMap_apply ℤ_[l] ℚ_[l] (PadicAlgCl l) x).symm
    have h1 := ((hf.units_map _).comp (continuous_cycZ ℚ Ω l)).zpow (e : ℤ)
    have h2 := (((locConst _ (Chr.clsFacts ℚ Ω N).1).comp ψ.toUnitHom).comp
      (Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom)).continuous
    refine (h1.mul h2).congr fun g => ?_
    show _ = Units.map (algebraMap c.O (PadicAlgCl l)).toMonoidHom (chiO ℚ Ω l c.O g ^ (e : ℤ) * Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ g)
    rw [map_mul, map_zpow]
    rfl
  · obtain ⟨h1, s, hs, h2⟩ := (gdLoc Ω K l c e N ψ w η' a hlit).1 A hA
    refine ⟨D.η_ker ▸ h1, s, hs, ?_⟩
    rw [Units.val_pow_eq_pow_val] at h2
    exact not_not.1 h2


[AFE_SRC_HAS_FRAME] GalLink, SeedClause, HasFrame, GdFrameEx and HostFrameEx
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/HostFrameEx.lean#L1-L83

namespace UpperTheory.V.Pr

variable (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω]

def upl (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω] : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω) where
  carrier := {s | ∀ x ∈ maximalRealSubfield L, s (algebraMap L Ω x) = algebraMap L Ω x}
  one_mem' _ _ := rfl
  mul_mem' {a b} ha hb x hx := by rw [AlgEquiv.mul_apply, hb x hx, ha x hx]
  inv_mem' {a} ha x hx := a.symm_apply_eq.2 (ha x hx).symm

variable {Ω} {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O]

structure GalLink (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ g₁ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ)
    (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω] (Φ : Fr.Frame l (ResidueField O)) where
  ab : IsAbelianGalois ℚ L
  gal : galOf ℚ L Ω = upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker
  unr : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ upl Ω L ≤ η.ker
  split : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (M : Ω) < 1 → Dec (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ upl Ω L ≤ η.ker
  teich : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ⊓ upl Ω L,
    barChar O Ψ s = 1 → Ψ s = 1
  p : upl Ω L →* Φ.Γ
  p_cont : Continuous p
  p_surj : Function.Surjective p
  p_ker : p.ker = (Subgroup.normalClosure {s : upl Ω L | ∃ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧
    s.1 ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B' ∧ η s = 1 ∧ barChar O Ψ s = 1}).topologicalClosure
  del : ∀ s, Φ.del (p s) = η s
  eps : ∀ s, Φ.eps (p s) = cycZ ℚ Ω l s
  psi : ∀ s, Φ.Ψ (p s) = barChar O Ψ s
  c : ∃ h : σ ∈ upl Ω L, p ⟨σ, h⟩ = Φ.c
  g0 : ∃ h : g₁ ∈ upl Ω L, p ⟨g₁, h⟩ = Φ.g0
  pl : Φ.V → ValuationSubring Ω
  pl_conj : ∀ v, ∃ γ : G ℚ Ω, η γ = 1 ∧ pl v = γ • B
  pl_all : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∃! v, ∃ u ∈ upl Ω L, B' = u • pl v
  j : ∀ v, Φ.Gv v →* upl Ω L
  j_cont : ∀ v, Continuous (j v)
  j_inj : ∀ v, Function.Injective (j v)
  j_range : ∀ v (s : upl Ω L), s ∈ (j v).range ↔ s.1 ∈ Dec (F := ℚ) (pl v)
  j_loc : ∀ v g, Φ.loc v g = p (j v g)
  j_ine : ∀ v g, g ∈ Φ.Iv v ↔ (j v g).1 ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) (pl v)
  dv : ∀ v, Φ.dv v = (upl Ω L).relIndex (Dec (F := ℚ) (pl v))
  mu : ∀ v, ∃ g, cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 (j v g) ≠ 1

def GalLink.p0 {η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ} {σ g₁ : G ℚ Ω} {B : ValuationSubring Ω} {M : ℕ}
    {L : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω] {Φ : Fr.Frame l (ResidueField O)} (k : GalLink η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ)
    (s : G L Ω) : Φ.G0 :=
  have h : galEmb ℚ L Ω s ∈ upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker := k.gal ▸ galEmb_mem ℚ L Ω s
  ⟨k.p ⟨galEmb ℚ L Ω s, h.1⟩, (k.del _).trans h.2⟩

def SeedClause (c : Coef l) (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* c.Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω] : Prop :=
  ∃ (K : Type) (_ : Field K) (_ : NumberField K) (_ : Algebra K Ω) (N w : ℕ) (χ' η' θ : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ) (j : ℕ),
    Sd.FieldPairCharacterTripleConditions K L σ N w χ' η' θ j ∧ χ' ≠ η' ∧ ¬ l ∣ orderOf χ' ∧ (∀ s, (η' s).val = (((η s).val : ℤ) : Qbar l)) ∧
    (∀ s, ‖((cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ j * χ') s).val - (Ψ s).val.1‖ < 1) ∧ ¬ (l : ℤ) ∣ discr K

def HasFrame (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* c.Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω)
    (M : ℕ) : Prop :=
  ∃ (L : Type) (_ : Field L) (_ : NumberField L) (_ : IsCMField L) (_ : Algebra L Ω) (g₁ : G ℚ Ω)
    (Φ : Fr.Frame l (ResidueField c.O)) (C₀ : ℕ), Nonempty (GalLink η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ) ∧ Φ.Ok C₀ ∧ Φ.Big C₀ ∧
      SeedClause c η Ψ σ L

def HostFrameEx : Prop :=
  ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l]
    (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (o₁ : ℕ), UnitaryThreeInertia.HostData l e ψ o₁ →
  ∀ σ : G ℚ Ω, IsCC σ → ∀ B : ValuationSubring Ω, B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 →
    HasFrame c (UnitaryThreeInertia.eta4 Ω) (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) σ B (l * N)

def GdFrameEx : Prop :=
  ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l] (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ)
    (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ) (B : ValuationSubring Ω), B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 →
    HasFrame c D.η D.Ψ D.cc B (l * N * (discr K).natAbs)

end UpperTheory.V.Pr

end

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.gd_ex :
    GC.Hypotheses₂ → GdFrameEx := by
  intro hP Ω _ _ _ K _ _ _ _ l _ c _ N _ e ψ D B hB
  have hG := gen_gd Ω K l c N e ψ D B hB
  obtain ⟨Q, hQ, hS⟩ := seed_cl Ω l c _ _ _ B _ _ hG
  obtain ⟨L, i1, i2, i3, i4, g₁, Φ, C₀, w, ⟨k, -⟩, hOk, hBig, hw, hwQ, hle⟩ :=
    frame_ex hP l _ _ _ B _ _ hG Q hQ
  refine ⟨L, i1, i2, i3, i4, g₁, Φ, C₀, ⟨k⟩, hOk, hBig, hS L w k.ab k.gal hw hwQ hle ?_⟩
  obtain ⟨h, hg⟩ := k.g0
  refine ⟨g₁, ?_, ?_⟩
  · rw [k.gal]
    refine ⟨h, ?_⟩
    have h1 := k.del ⟨g₁, h⟩
    rw [hg] at h1
    exact h1.symm.trans Φ.g0.2
  · have h1 := k.psi ⟨g₁, h⟩
    rw [hg] at h1
    exact h1 ▸ hOk.baseSubgroup_spec

open NumberField Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V.Fr UpperTheory.V

private theorem cycMod_emb (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] [Algebra F Ω]
    (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (g : G F Ω) : cycMod ℚ Ω l 1 (galEmb ℚ F Ω g) = cycMod F Ω l 1 g := rfl

private theorem one_of_cast (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l] (x : ℤ_[l])
    (h : ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl l) k (PadicInt.toZMod x) = 1) : PadicInt.toZModPow 1 x = 1 := by
  have h1 : x - 1 ∈ RingHom.ker (PadicInt.toZMod : ℤ_[l] →+* ZMod l) := by
    rw [RingHom.mem_ker, map_sub, map_one, sub_eq_zero]
    exact (ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl l) k).injective (h.trans (map_one _).symm)
  rw [PadicInt.ker_toZMod, PadicInt.maximalIdeal_eq_span_p, ← pow_one (l : ℤ_[l]), ← PadicInt.ker_toZModPow,
    RingHom.mem_ker, map_sub, map_one, sub_eq_zero] at h1
  exact h1

private theorem rk_link {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (L : Type) [Field L]
    [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Algebra L Ω] (A A' : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) :
    GC.clRk l ((A ⊓ A').comap (galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω) ⊓ (cycMod (W.Lp L) Ω l 1).ker) =
      GC.clRk l (upl Ω L ⊓ A ⊓ A' ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker) := by
  have hU : upl Ω L ⊓ A ⊓ A' ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker ≤ galOf ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω :=
    fun g hg x => hg.1.1.1 x.1 x.2
  rw [← clrk_tr Ω l (W.Lp L) _ hU]
  congr 1
  ext g
  simp only [Subgroup.mem_inf, Subgroup.mem_comap, MonoidHom.mem_ker]
  rw [cycMod_emb]
  constructor
  · rintro ⟨⟨ha, ha'⟩, hc⟩
    exact ⟨⟨⟨fun x hx => galEmb_mem ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω g ⟨x, hx⟩, ha⟩, ha'⟩, hc⟩
  · rintro ⟨⟨⟨_, ha⟩, ha'⟩, hc⟩
    exact ⟨⟨ha, ha'⟩, hc⟩

private theorem ok_link (P : GC.Hypotheses₂) {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type}
    [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] {η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ} {σ : G ℚ Ω}
    {B : ValuationSubring Ω} {M : ℕ} {Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)} (hG : Gen l η Ψ σ B M Hb) (m C₀ : ℕ) (L : Type) [Field L]
    [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Algebra L Ω] (g₁ : G ℚ Ω) (Φ : Frame l (ResidueField O))
    (hU : UFacts l η Ψ M m (upl Ω L)) (hL : Linked l η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ) (hg : barChar O Ψ g₁ ^ 2 ≠ 1)
    (hS : ∀ a : ℕ, a = 1 ∨ a = 2 → StrictCl l η Ψ ((barChar O Ψ)⁻¹ ^ a * etaO O η) (upl Ω L))
    (hC : GC.clRk l (upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker) ≤ C₀) (hm : 3 * C₀ + 7 < l ^ m) :
    Φ.Ok C₀ ∧ Φ.Big C₀ := by
  haveI : IsAlgClosure (W.Lp L) Ω :=
    ⟨IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ, Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := ℚ) (W.Lp L)⟩
  have hb := ok_basic l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG m L g₁ Φ hU hL hg
  obtain ⟨h_del_open, h_csq, h_delc, h_loc, h_eps, h_Ψopen, h_Ψc, h_g0, h_card, h_cw, h_delcw,
    h_f2a, h_f2b, h_f2c, h_f3, h_f5, h_ΨN, h_dv, h_ne⟩ := hb
  have hf4 := frobenius_galoisField_four_compatibility l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG m L g₁ Φ hU hL 1 (Or.inl rfl) (hS 1 (Or.inl rfl))
  have hf4' := frobenius_galoisField_four_compatibility l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG m L g₁ Φ hU hL 2 (Or.inr rfl) (hS 2 (Or.inr rfl))
  rw [pow_one] at hf4
  obtain ⟨k, S, π, ι, pl, e, c, hF, hπ, hι⟩ := hL
  have hrk : GC.clRk l (((η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker).comap (galEmb ℚ (W.Lp L) Ω)) ⊓
      (cycMod (W.Lp L) Ω l 1).ker) ≤ C₀ := by
    rw [rk_link l L η.ker (barChar O Ψ).ker]
    exact hC
  have hom : ∀ v : Φ.V, ∃ s, (Φ.om (ResidueField O)) ((Φ.loc v) s) ≠ 1 := by
    intro v
    obtain ⟨g, hg'⟩ := k.mu v
    refine ⟨g, fun h => hg' ?_⟩
    rw [k.j_loc] at h
    simp only [Frame.om, MonoidHom.comp_apply, k.eps] at h
    have h1 := congrArg Units.val h
    simp only [Units.coe_map, Units.val_one, MonoidHom.coe_coe, RingHom.coe_comp,
      Function.comp_apply] at h1
    apply Units.ext
    simp only [cycMod, MonoidHom.comp_apply, Units.coe_map, MonoidHom.coe_coe, Units.val_one]
    exact one_of_cast l (ResidueField O) _ h1
  have hfin : Finite (ResidueField O) := hG.fin
  have hok : Φ.Ok C₀ :=
    { l_odd := hG.odd
      fin := hfin
      del_open := h_del_open
      c_sq := h_csq
      del_c := h_delc
      loc_cont := h_loc
      eps_cont := h_eps
      Ψ_open := h_Ψopen
      Ψ_c := h_Ψc
      baseSubgroup_spec := h_g0
      phi := GC.phi _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF
      card_inf := h_card
      cw_sq := h_cw
      del_cw := h_delcw
      f2a := h_f2a
      f2b := h_f2b
      f2c := h_f2c
      f3 := h_f3
      f4 := hf4
      f4' := hf4'
      f5 := h_f5
      Ψ_N := h_ΨN
      gen := GC.gen P _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF hom
      cl := GC.cl _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF
      ec := GC.ec P _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF
      b1p := GC.b1p _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF h_f3 C₀ hrk
      b1 := GC.b1 _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF h_f3 C₀ hrk
      h1 := GC.h1 P _ Ω l _ Φ S _ π ι k.pl pl e c hF hfin hom C₀ hrk }
  refine ⟨hok, ?_, ?_⟩
  · obtain ⟨v⟩ := h_ne
    have h1 : Φ.dv v ≤ Φ.d := by
      unfold Frame.d
      exact Finset.single_le_sum (fun _ _ => Nat.zero_le _) (Finset.mem_univ v)
    have := h_dv v
    omega
  · intro v
    have := h_dv v
    omega

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.frame_ex :
    GC.Hypotheses₂ → ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CharP (ResidueField O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ) (Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)), Gen l η Ψ σ B M Hb → ∀ Q : ℕ, Q ≠ 0 → HasFrameW l η Ψ σ B M Q := by
  intro P Ω _ _ _ l _ O _ _ _ η Ψ σ B M Hb hG Q hQ
  obtain ⟨H₀, h0⟩ := exists_twistCharacter_continuousCohomology l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG
  obtain ⟨w, hw, hwQ⟩ := auxw_ex l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG Q hQ
  obtain ⟨C₀, hC₀⟩ := b0 l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG H₀ w h0 hw
  have hl : l.Prime := Fact.out
  set m := 3 * C₀ + 7 with hm_def
  have hm : 3 * C₀ + 7 < l ^ m := Nat.lt_pow_self hl.one_lt
  have hU := ufacts l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG H₀ w h0 hw m
  obtain ⟨L, iF, iN, iC, iA, hab, hup, hgal⟩ := field_ex l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG m _ hU
  have hM : B.valuation (M : Ω) < 1 := by
    obtain ⟨t, ht⟩ := hG.l_dvd
    rw [ht, Nat.cast_mul, map_mul]
    have hle : B.valuation (t : Ω) ≤ 1 := B.valuation_le_one ⟨(t : Ω), natCast_mem B t⟩
    exact lt_of_le_of_lt (mul_le_of_le_one_right' hle) hG.over_l
  obtain ⟨s, ⟨hsD, hsU⟩, hs2⟩ := hU.f3 B hG.over_l
  have hsη : s ∈ η.ker := hU.split B hM ⟨hsD, hsU⟩
  have hs : s ∈ galOf ℚ L Ω := by
    rw [hgal]
    exact ⟨hsU, hsη⟩
  rw [← hup] at hU
  obtain ⟨Φ, hL⟩ := link_ex l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG m L s hU hab (by rw [hgal, hup]) hs
  have hS : ∀ a : ℕ, a = 1 ∨ a = 2 → StrictCl l η Ψ ((barChar O Ψ)⁻¹ ^ a * etaO O η) (upl Ω L) := by
    intro a ha
    rw [hup]
    exact strict P l η Ψ σ B M Hb hG H₀ w h0 hw m a ha
  have hC : GC.clRk l (upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker ⊓ (barChar O Ψ).ker ⊓ (cycMod ℚ Ω l 1).ker) ≤ C₀ := by
    rw [hup]
    exact hC₀ m
  obtain ⟨hok, hbig⟩ := ok_link P l hG m C₀ L s Φ hU hL hs2 hS hC hm
  refine ⟨L, iF, iN, iC, iA, s, Φ, C₀, w, hL, hok, hbig, hw, hwQ, ?_⟩
  rw [hup]
  intro x hx
  exact hx.1.1.2


[AFE_SRC_SEED] Q-before-field coefficient-DVR character seed

private theorem km_mono {Ω C : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [Monoid C] {m n : ℕ} (h : m ∣ n) {χ : G ℚ Ω →* C}
    (hχ : Af.KillsMu m χ) : Af.KillsMu n χ := fun s hs => hχ s fun ζ hζ => hs ζ (by
  obtain ⟨k, rfl⟩ := h
  rw [pow_mul, hζ, one_pow])

private theorem split_of {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {N w : ℕ} (hw : w ≡ 1 [MOD N])
    {χ : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ} (hχ : Af.KillsMu N χ) : Sd.SplitAt N w χ :=
  ⟨hχ, fun s hs => hχ s fun ζ hζ => by
    rw [hs ζ hζ, pow_eq_pow_mod w hζ, hw, ← pow_eq_pow_mod 1 hζ, pow_one]⟩

theorem UpperTheory.V.Fr.seed_cl :
    ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) [CharP (ResidueField c.O) l] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* c.Oˣ) (σ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω) (M : ℕ) (Hb : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)), Gen l η Ψ σ B M Hb → ∃ Q : ℕ, Q ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Algebra L Ω] (w : ℕ), IsAbelianGalois ℚ L → galOf ℚ L Ω = upl Ω L ⊓ η.ker → FrobeniusWitnessPrime l η Ψ M w → w ≡ 1 [MOD Q] → upl Ω L ≤ Hth Ω l w → (∃ s ∈ galOf ℚ L Ω, barChar c.O Ψ s ^ 2 ≠ 1) → SeedClause c η Ψ σ L := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l _ c _ η Ψ σ B M Hb hG
  have hlp : l.Prime := Fact.out
  obtain ⟨a, hηa, hΨa⟩ := hG.cyc
  obtain ⟨j, hj⟩ := hG.Ψ_l
  obtain ⟨D, δ, hD, -, hδ, hηδ⟩ := hG.quad
  obtain ⟨K, iK, nK, aK, hK2, hKc, hKη⟩ := sc_quad Ω η D δ hD hδ hηδ
  obtain ⟨T, hT, hTa⟩ := sc_teich Ω l c hG.fin
  have hM : M ^ a * l ≠ 0 := mul_ne_zero (pow_ne_zero _ hG.M_ne) hlp.ne_zero
  obtain ⟨r, N, hlN, hN⟩ := Nat.exists_eq_pow_mul_and_not_dvd hM l hlp.one_lt.ne'
  have hN0 : N ≠ 0 := by
    rintro rfl
    exact hM (by rw [hN, mul_zero])
  let φ : G ℚ Ω →* (ResidueField c.O)ˣ := barChar c.O Ψ * (omO Ω l c.O ^ j)⁻¹
  let χ' : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ := T.comp φ
  let η' : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ := (Units.map (Int.castRingHom (Qbar l)).toMonoidHom).comp η
  have hφ : ∀ s, φ s = barChar c.O Ψ s * ((omO Ω l c.O) s ^ j)⁻¹ := fun s => rfl
  have hχ : ∀ s, χ' s = T (φ s) := fun s => rfl
  have hη'ker : η'.ker = η.ker := by
    ext s
    simp only [MonoidHom.mem_ker]
    exact map_eq_one_iff _ (Units.map_injective (f := (Int.castRingHom (Qbar l)).toMonoidHom) Int.cast_injective)
  have hφK : Af.KillsMu (M ^ a * l) φ := by
    intro s hs
    have h1 : barChar c.O Ψ s = 1 := km_mono (dvd_mul_right _ _) hΨa s hs
    have h2 : omO Ω l c.O s = 1 := km_mono (dvd_mul_left _ _) (sc_om Ω l c.O).1 s hs
    rw [hφ, h1, h2, one_pow, inv_one, mul_one]
  have hχK : Af.KillsMu (M ^ a * l) χ' := by
    intro s hs
    rw [hχ, hφK s hs, map_one]
  have hη'K : Af.KillsMu (M ^ a * l) η' := by
    intro s hs
    have h1 : η s = 1 := km_mono (dvd_mul_right _ _) hηa s hs
    show Units.map _ (η s) = 1
    rw [h1, map_one]
  have hχN : Af.KillsMu N χ' := by
    refine sc_strip Ω l _ χ' r N hN0 hlN (hN ▸ hχK) B hG.over_l ?_
    intro s hs
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, hχ, hφ, hj B hG.over_l s hs, mul_inv_cancel, map_one]
  have hη'N : Af.KillsMu N η' := by
    refine sc_strip Ω l _ η' r N hN0 hlN (hN ▸ hη'K) B hG.over_l ?_
    rw [hη'ker]
    exact hG.η_l B hG.over_l
  have hT1 : T (-1) = -1 := by
    have hne : (-1 : (ResidueField c.O)ˣ) ≠ 1 := by
      intro h
      have h' : (-1 : ResidueField c.O) = 1 := by
        simpa using congrArg (fun u : (ResidueField c.O)ˣ => (u : ResidueField c.O)) h
      rw [neg_one_eq_one_iff, ringChar.eq (ResidueField c.O) l] at h'
      exact hG.odd h'
    have hsq : ((T (-1) : (Qbar l)ˣ) : Qbar l) * (T (-1) : Qbar l) = 1 := by
      rw [← Units.val_mul, ← map_mul, neg_one_mul, neg_neg, map_one, Units.val_one]
    rcases mul_self_eq_one_iff.1 hsq with h | h
    · exact absurd (hT (by rw [map_one]; exact Units.ext h)) hne
    · exact Units.ext (by rw [h, Units.val_neg, Units.val_one])
  have happ : ∀ s, (GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp.cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ j * χ') s =
      (GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp.cycBar ℚ Ω l) s ^ j * T ((Units.map ↑(residue c.O)) (Ψ s) * ((omO Ω l c.O) s ^ j)⁻¹) :=
    fun s => rfl
  have hord : ¬ l ∣ orderOf χ' := by
    classical
    haveI := Fintype.ofFinite (ResidueField c.O)
    have hpow : χ' ^ Fintype.card (ResidueField c.O)ˣ = 1 := MonoidHom.ext fun s => by
      show (T (φ s)) ^ Fintype.card (ResidueField c.O)ˣ = 1
      rw [← map_pow, pow_card_eq_one, map_one]
    intro hd
    have hd2 := hd.trans (orderOf_dvd_of_pow_eq_one hpow)
    obtain ⟨n, -, hcard⟩ := FiniteField.card (ResidueField c.O) l
    rw [Fintype.card_units, hcard] at hd2
    have hl1 : l ∣ l ^ (n : ℕ) := dvd_pow_self l n.ne_zero
    have hpos : 1 ≤ l ^ (n : ℕ) := Nat.one_le_pow _ _ hlp.pos
    have h3 : l ∣ (l ^ (n : ℕ) - 1) + 1 := by
      rw [Nat.sub_add_cancel hpos]
      exact hl1
    exact hlp.one_lt.ne' (Nat.dvd_one.1 ((Nat.dvd_add_right hd2).1 h3))
  refine ⟨N, hN0, ?_⟩
  intro L _ _ _ _ w hLab hLgal hw hwQ hLth hs0
  obtain ⟨s0, hs0L, hs0⟩ := hs0
  obtain ⟨θ, hθker, hθ1, hθl, hθw⟩ := sc_theta Ω l w hw.prime hw.one
  have hwN : ¬ w ∣ N := by
    intro hd
    have hd' : w ∣ M ^ a * l := hN ▸ dvd_mul_of_dvd_right hd _
    rcases (Nat.Prime.dvd_mul hw.prime).1 hd' with h | h
    · exact hw.cop (hw.prime.dvd_of_dvd_pow h)
    · have hwl := (Nat.prime_dvd_prime_iff_eq hw.prime hlp).1 h
      have h1 : w % l = 1 % l := hw.one
      rw [hwl, Nat.mod_self, Nat.mod_eq_of_lt hlp.one_lt] at h1
      exact zero_ne_one h1
  have hLK : galOf ℚ L Ω ≤ galOf ℚ K Ω := by
    rw [hKη, hLgal]
    exact inf_le_right
  refine ⟨K, iK, nK, aK, N, w, χ', η', θ, j, ?_, ?_, hord, fun s => rfl, fun s => happ s ▸ hTa (Ψ s) j s, ?_⟩
  · refine ⟨hG.cc, ⟨hG.odd, Nat.pos_of_ne_zero hN0, hlN, hw.prime, hwN, hθ1, hθl, hθw⟩, hK2, hKc, hLab, hLK,
      by rw [hη'ker, hKη], split_of hwQ hη'N, split_of hwQ hχN, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
    · rw [hθker, hLgal]
      exact inf_le_left.trans hLth
    · rw [hχ, hφ, hG.even, (sc_om Ω l c.O).2 σ hG.cc, one_mul, ← inv_pow, inv_neg_one, map_pow, hT1]
    · refine ⟨s0, hs0L, fun hc => hs0 ?_⟩
      exact sc_cong l c (Ψ s0) _ (happ s0 ▸ hTa (Ψ s0) j s0) hc
  · intro heq
    apply hG.g4
    intro s hs
    rw [Subgroup.mem_inf, MonoidHom.mem_ker, MonoidHom.mem_ker] at hs
    have h1 : χ' s = 1 := by
      rw [hχ, hφ, hs.1, hs.2, one_pow, inv_one, mul_one, map_one]
    rw [heq] at h1
    rw [← hη'ker]
    exact h1
  · exact sc_disc Ω l K hK2 (fun B' hB' => hKη ▸ hG.η_l B' hB')

import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.KummerTheory.Mk0IdMapCongr

namespace UpperTheory.V.GC

structure Hypotheses₂ : Prop extends X.HcPublished where
  uo : UnramifiedOrthogonality
  gw : GreenbergWiles

def eqHom {k Γ M : Type} [CommRing k] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [AddCommGroup M] [Module k M]
    (ρ : Representation k Γ M) (D J : Subgroup Γ) : Submodule k (J → M) where
  carrier := {f | IsLocallyConstant f ∧ (∀ a b, f (a * b) = f a + f b) ∧
    ∀ d ∈ D, ∀ j j' : J, j'.1 = d * j.1 * d⁻¹ → f j' = ρ d (f j)}
  zero_mem' := ⟨IsLocallyConstant.const _, by simp, by simp⟩
  add_mem' {a b} ha hb := ⟨ha.1.add hb.1, fun x y => by simp only [Pi.add_apply, ha.2.1, hb.2.1]; abel,
    fun d hd j j' h => by simp [ha.2.2 d hd j j' h, hb.2.2 d hd j j' h]⟩
  smul_mem' c a ha := ⟨ha.1.comp (c • ·), fun x y => by simp [ha.2.1], fun d hd j j' h => by simp [ha.2.2 d hd j j' h]⟩

variable (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime]

def cycF (k : Type) [Field k] [CharP k l] : G F Ω →* kˣ :=
  (Units.map (ZMod.castHom (dvd_pow_self l one_ne_zero) k).toMonoidHom).comp (cycMod F Ω l 1)

variable {F Ω}

def clRk (U : Subgroup (G F Ω)) : Cardinal := Module.rank (ZMod l) (Kum.XDual 1 U (ZMod l) (ZMod l))

def tameN (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) : Subgroup (G F Ω) :=
  (Subgroup.normalClosure (⋃ v ∈ Sᶜ, ⋃ A ∈ {A : ValuationSubring Ω | IsPlaceOver A v},
    ((Ine (F := F) A ⊓ K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) : Set (G F Ω)))).topologicalClosure

structure GLink (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω)) {Γ : Type} [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ]
    (π : G F Ω →* Γ) : Prop where
  odd : l ≠ 2
  above : ContainsAbove S l
  normal : K.Normal
  isOpen : IsOpen (K : Set (G F Ω))
  idx : ¬ l ∣ K.index
  cont : Continuous π
  onto : Function.Surjective π
  ker : π.ker = tameN (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) K

structure LLink {V : Type} {Gv : V → Type} [∀ v, Group (Gv v)] [∀ v, TopologicalSpace (Gv v)] (ι : ∀ v, Gv v →* G F Ω)
    (A : V → ValuationSubring Ω) (pl : V → HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F)) : Prop where
  isOver : ∀ v, IsPlaceOver (A v) (pl v)
  inj : Function.Injective pl
  mem : ∀ w, w ∈ Set.range pl ↔ ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∈ w.asIdeal
  cont : ∀ v, Continuous (ι v)
  ι_inj : ∀ v, Function.Injective (ι v)
  range : ∀ v, (ι v).range = Dec (F := F) (A v)

structure FLink {k : Type} [Field k] (Φ : Fr.Frame l k) (S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) (K : Subgroup (G F Ω))
    (π : G F Ω →* Φ.Γ) (ι : ∀ v, Φ.Gv v →* G F Ω) (A : Φ.V → ValuationSubring Ω) (pl : Φ.V → HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))
    (e : Φ.Inf ≃ InfinitePlace F) (c : Φ.Inf → G F Ω) : Prop where
  real : IsTotallyReal F
  g : GLink l S K π
  only : ∀ w ∈ S, ((l : ℕ) : 𝓞 F) ∈ w.asIdeal
  loc : LLink l ι A pl
  loc_eq : ∀ v s, Φ.loc v s = π (ι v s)
  ine : ∀ v, Φ.Iv v = (Ine (F := F) (A v)).comap (ι v)
  dv : ∀ v, Φ.dv v = locDeg (pl v)
  eps : ∀ g, Φ.eps (π g) = cycZ F Ω l g
  cc : ∀ w, IsCCAt (e w) (c w)
  cw : ∀ w, Φ.cw w = π (c w)
  del_open : IsOpen (Φ.del.ker : Set Φ.Γ)
  del_c : Φ.del Φ.c = -1
  K_del : ∀ g ∈ K, Φ.del (π g) = 1
  K_Ψ : ∀ g ∈ K, Φ.Ψ (π g) = 1

theorem gLink_quot (hl : l ≠ 2) {S : Finset (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))} (hS : ContainsAbove S l) {K : Subgroup (G F Ω)}
    (h1 : K.Normal) (h2 : IsOpen (K : Set (G F Ω))) (h3 : ¬ l ∣ K.index)
    [(tameN (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) K).Normal] :
    GLink l S K (QuotientGroup.mk' (tameN (S : Set (HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F))) K)) := by
  refine ⟨hl, hS, h1, h2, h3, ?_, QuotientGroup.mk'_surjective _, QuotientGroup.ker_mk' _⟩
  exact continuous_quot_mk

end UpperTheory.V.GC

end

import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.IdeleGenerators.SlopeTestRemainingChoices

noncomputable section

open NumberField IsDedekindDomain Neutral.B1 Formalism.L2.Host Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal Formalism.BasicNotions Formalism.MainNotions.N Polynomial
open GlobalTheory.CuspRep GlobalTheory.HeckeGal GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp UpperTheory.V.Af UpperTheory.V.Base UpperTheory.V.Published
open scoped Matrix

namespace UpperTheory.V.Sd

def ToNewform : Prop :=
  ∀ (k N : ℕ) [NeZero N], 2 ≤ k → ∀ χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N, χ.IsPrimitive → ∀ g : Eigenform k N χ,
    ∃ f : Newform k, f.M = N ∧ (∀ n, f.a n = g.a n) ∧ ∀ n : ℕ, f.χ (n : ZMod f.M) = χ (n : ZMod N)

def EigAut : Prop :=
  ∀ (k N : ℕ) [NeZero N], 2 ≤ k → ∀ χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ N, χ.IsPrimitive → ∀ (g : Eigenform k N χ) (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime]
    (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (ρ : G ℚ Ω →* GL (Fin 2) (Qbar l)), Continuous ρ → IsIrr ρ →
    (∀ᶠ w : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 ℚ) in Filter.cofinite, ∀ A : ValuationSubring Ω, IsPlaceOver A w →
      (∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) A, ρ s = 1) ∧ ∀ σ : G ℚ Ω, IsFrob A (normV w) σ →
        (ρ σ : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) (Qbar l)).charpoly.map ι.toRingHom =
          X ^ 2 - C (g.a (normV w)) * X + C (χ (normV w : ZMod N) * (normV w : ℂ) ^ (k - 1))) → IsAutR ι ρ

def DirOf : Prop :=
  ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (N : ℕ), 0 < N →
    ∀ φ : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ, KillsMu N φ → ∃ (M : ℕ) (_ : NeZero M) (χ : DirichletCharacter ℂ M), M ∣ N ∧ χ.IsPrimitive ∧
      φ = Chr.dirG ℚ Ω (χ.ringHomComp ι.symm.toRingHom)

def PropEG : Prop :=
  ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (c : G ℚ Ω), IsCC c →
    ∀ (N w : ℕ) (φ θ : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ), SlopeTestRemainingChoices N w θ → SplitAt N w φ → φ ≠ 1 → ∃ k₀ : ℕ, ∀ k ≥ k₀, φ c = (-1) ^ k →
      ∃ ρ, EisRep ι φ θ k ρ

def SIabs : Prop :=
  ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime], l ≠ 2 → ∀ (Γ : Type) [Group Γ] (ρ : Γ →* GL (Fin 2) (Qbar l)) (Φ : Γ →* (Qbar l)ˣ),
    (∀ s, ‖(Φ s : Qbar l)‖ = 1) → IsIrr ρ → ResSum ρ ![1, Φ] → ∀ H : Subgroup Γ, H.Normal →
    (∃ s ∈ H, (ρ s : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) (Qbar l)).charpoly.Separable) → ¬ IsIrr (ρ.comp H.subtype) →
      (∀ s ∈ H, Cong (Φ s) 1) ∧ ∀ s, Cong (Φ s ^ 2) 1

def EisReg : Prop :=
  ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (φ θ : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ)
    (k : ℕ), 2 ≤ k → ∀ ρ, EisRep ι φ θ k ρ → ∀ H : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω), IsOpen (H : Set (G ℚ Ω)) →
    ∃ s ∈ H, ∃ a : (Qbar l)ˣ, ¬ IsOfFinOrder a ∧
      (ρ s : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) (Qbar l)).charpoly = (X - C (a : Qbar l)) * (X - C 1)

def SI : Prop :=
  ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime], l ≠ 2 → ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ)
    (φ θ : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ) (k : ℕ), 2 ≤ k → IsOfFinOrder φ → ∀ ρ, EisRep ι φ θ k ρ →
    (∀ H : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω), IsOpen (H : Set (G ℚ Ω)) → H.Normal →
      (∃ s ∈ H, ¬ Cong ((cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ (k - 1) * φ) s) 1) → IsIrr (ρ.comp H.subtype)) ∧
    ((∃ s, ¬ Cong ((cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ (k - 1) * φ) s ^ 2) 1) → IrrOpen ρ)

def Ad0Irr : Prop :=
  ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (Γ : Type) [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] (ρ : Γ →* GL (Fin 2) (Qbar l)),
    Continuous ρ → IrrOpen ρ → IrrOpen (ad0 ρ)

def Sym3Irr : Prop :=
  ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (Γ : Type) [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] (ρ : Γ →* GL (Fin 2) (Qbar l)),
    Continuous ρ → IrrOpen ρ → (∀ H : Subgroup Γ, IsOpen (H : Set Γ) → ∃ s ∈ H, ∃ a b : (Qbar l)ˣ, ¬ IsOfFinOrder (a / b) ∧
      (ρ s : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) (Qbar l)).charpoly = (X - C (a : Qbar l)) * (X - C (b : Qbar l))) → IrrOpen (sym3 ρ)

def IrrAb : Prop :=
  ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (Γ : Type) [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] (n : ℕ) (r : Γ →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)),
    Continuous r → IrrOpen r → ∀ H H' : Subgroup Γ, IsOpen (H : Set Γ) → H' ≤ H → (∀ a ∈ H, ∀ b ∈ H, a * b * a⁻¹ * b⁻¹ ∈ H') →
      IsIrr (r.comp H'.subtype)

def Ad0Facts : Prop :=
  ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (Γ : Type) [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] (ρ : Γ →* GL (Fin 2) (Qbar l)),
    (Continuous ρ → Continuous (ad0 ρ)) ∧
    (∃ P : GL (Fin 3) (Qbar l), (P : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (Qbar l))ᵀ = P ∧ ∀ s,
      (ad0 ρ s : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (Qbar l))ᵀ * (P : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (Qbar l)) * (ad0 ρ s : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (Qbar l)) = P) ∧
    (∀ (H : Subgroup Γ) (a b : Γ →* (Qbar l)ˣ), IsDiagOn ρ H ![a, b] → IsDiagOn (ad0 ρ) H ![b / a, 1, a / b]) ∧
    (∀ (H : Subgroup Γ) (κ : Fin 2 → ↥H →* (Qbar l)ˣ), IsFlagOn ρ H κ → IsFlagOn (ad0 ρ) H ![κ 0 / κ 1, 1, κ 1 / κ 0]) ∧
    (∀ s (a b : (Qbar l)ˣ), (ρ s : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) (Qbar l)).charpoly = (X - C (a : Qbar l)) * (X - C (b : Qbar l)) →
      (ad0 ρ s : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (Qbar l)).charpoly =
        (X - C ((a / b : (Qbar l)ˣ) : Qbar l)) * (X - C 1) * (X - C ((b / a : (Qbar l)ˣ) : Qbar l))) ∧
    ∀ a b : Γ →* (Qbar l)ˣ, (∀ s, ‖(a s : Qbar l)‖ = 1 ∧ ‖(b s : Qbar l)‖ = 1) → ResSum ρ ![a, b] → ResSum (ad0 ρ) ![b / a, 1, a / b]

def Sym3Facts : Prop :=
  ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (Γ : Type) [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] (ρ : Γ →* GL (Fin 2) (Qbar l)),
    (Continuous ρ → Continuous (sym3 ρ)) ∧
    (∀ s, (sym3 ρ s : Matrix (Fin 4) (Fin 4) (Qbar l))ᵀ * !![0, 0, 0, 3; 0, 0, -1, 0; 0, 1, 0, 0; -3, 0, 0, 0] *
      (sym3 ρ s : Matrix (Fin 4) (Fin 4) (Qbar l)) =
        (ρ s : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) (Qbar l)).det ^ 3 • !![0, 0, 0, 3; 0, 0, -1, 0; 0, 1, 0, 0; -3, 0, 0, 0]) ∧
    (∀ (H : Subgroup Γ) (a b : Γ →* (Qbar l)ˣ), IsDiagOn ρ H ![a, b] → IsDiagOn (sym3 ρ) H ![a ^ 3, a ^ 2 * b, a * b ^ 2, b ^ 3]) ∧
    (∀ (H : Subgroup Γ) (κ : Fin 2 → ↥H →* (Qbar l)ˣ), IsFlagOn ρ H κ →
      IsFlagOn (sym3 ρ) H ![κ 0 ^ 3, κ 0 ^ 2 * κ 1, κ 0 * κ 1 ^ 2, κ 1 ^ 3]) ∧
    (∀ s (a b : Qbar l), (ρ s : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) (Qbar l)).charpoly = (X - C a) * (X - C b) →
      (sym3 ρ s : Matrix (Fin 4) (Fin 4) (Qbar l)).charpoly =
        (X - C (a ^ 3)) * (X - C (a ^ 2 * b)) * (X - C (a * b ^ 2)) * (X - C (b ^ 3))) ∧
    ∀ a b : Γ →* (Qbar l)ˣ, (∀ s, ‖(a s : Qbar l)‖ = 1 ∧ ‖(b s : Qbar l)‖ = 1) → ResSum ρ ![a, b] →
      ResSum (sym3 ρ) ![a ^ 3, a ^ 2 * b, a * b ^ 2, b ^ 3]

def ShapeFacts : Prop :=
  ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (Γ : Type) [Group Γ] (n : ℕ) (r : Γ →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) (δ : Γ →* (Qbar l)ˣ),
    (∀ (H : Subgroup Γ) (χ : Fin n → Γ →* (Qbar l)ˣ), IsDiagOn r H χ → IsDiagOn (tw r δ) H fun i => χ i * δ) ∧
    (∀ (H : Subgroup Γ) (χ χ' : Fin n → Γ →* (Qbar l)ˣ) (e : Equiv.Perm (Fin n)), IsDiagOn r H χ → (∀ i, ∀ s ∈ H, χ' i s = χ (e i) s) →
      IsDiagOn r H χ') ∧
    (∀ (H : Subgroup Γ) (κ : Fin n → ↥H →* (Qbar l)ˣ), IsFlagOn r H κ → IsFlagOn (tw r δ) H fun i => κ i * δ.comp H.subtype) ∧
    (∀ χ : Fin n → Γ →* (Qbar l)ˣ, (∀ s, ‖(δ s : Qbar l)‖ = 1) → ResSum r χ → ResSum (tw r δ) fun i => χ i * δ) ∧
    (∀ (χ χ' : Fin n → Γ →* (Qbar l)ˣ) (e : Equiv.Perm (Fin n)), (∀ i s, ‖(χ i s : Qbar l)‖ = 1) → ResSum r χ →
      (∀ i s, Cong (χ' i s) (χ (e i) s)) → ResSum r χ') ∧
    (IsIrr r → IsIrr (tw r δ))

def ResFacts : Prop :=
  ∀ (F E Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field E] [NumberField E] [Field Ω] [Algebra F E] [Algebra F Ω] [Algebra E Ω]
    [IsScalarTower F E Ω] [IsAlgClosure E Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (n : ℕ) (r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)),
    Continuous (galEmb F E Ω) ∧ IsOpen (galOf F E Ω : Set (G F Ω)) ∧ (cycBar F Ω l).comp (galEmb F E Ω) = cycBar E Ω l ∧
    (IsIrr (r.comp (galOf F E Ω).subtype) ↔ IsIrr (r.comp (galEmb F E Ω))) ∧
    (IrrOpen r → IrrOpen (r.comp (galEmb F E Ω))) ∧
    (∀ τ, TypeAway r τ → TypeAway (r.comp (galEmb F E Ω)) fun i => (τ i).comp (galEmb F E Ω)) ∧
    ∀ m ψ A, OrdAt r m ψ A → OrdAt (r.comp (galEmb F E Ω)) m (fun i => (ψ i).comp (galEmb F E Ω)) A

def SelfTwGal : Prop :=
  ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (n : ℕ)
    (r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) (sat : HeightOneSpectrum (𝓞 F) → Multiset ℂˣ → Prop), (∀ v B B', sat v B → sat v B' → B = B') →
    Continuous r → AttachedSys ι r sat → ∀ (η : HChar F) (m : ℕ), 0 < m → η ^ m = 1 → η ≠ 1 → SelfTw sat η →
    ∃ δ : G F Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ, δ ≠ 1 ∧ IsOpen (δ.ker : Set (G F Ω)) ∧ RepIso r (tw r δ) ∧
      ∀ (E : Type) [Field E] [NumberField E] [Algebra F E] [Algebra E Ω] [IsScalarTower F E Ω], IsNormChar E η →
        δ.comp (galEmb F E Ω) = 1

def AdAut : Prop :=
  ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [IsTotallyReal F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime]
    (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (ρ : G F Ω →* GL (Fin 2) (Qbar l)), Continuous ρ → IsAutR ι ρ → IrrOpen ρ →
    IsAutR ι (tw (ad0 ρ) (cycBar F Ω l)⁻¹)

def Sym3Aut : Prop :=
  ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [IsTotallyReal F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime]
    (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (ρ : G F Ω →* GL (Fin 2) (Qbar l)), Continuous ρ → IsAutR ι ρ → IrrOpen ρ → IsIrr (sym3 ρ) → IsAutR ι (sym3 ρ)

def UpQuad : Prop :=
  ∀ (F E Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [IsTotallyReal F] [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E] [Field Ω] [Algebra F E]
    [Algebra F Ω] [Algebra E Ω] [IsScalarTower F E Ω] [IsAlgClosure E Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ),
    Module.finrank F E = 2 → ∀ (n : ℕ) [Fact (n = 2)], 1 ≤ n → ∀ r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l), Continuous r → IsAutR ι r →
      IsIrr (r.comp (galEmb F E Ω)) → IsAutC ι (r.comp (galEmb F E Ω))

def UpCyc : Prop :=
  ∀ (F E Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [IsTotallyComplex F] [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E] [Field Ω] [Algebra F E]
    [Algebra F Ω] [Algebra E Ω] [IsScalarTower F E Ω] [IsAlgClosure E Ω] [IsGalois F E] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ),
    (Module.finrank F E).Prime → ∀ (n : ℕ), 1 ≤ n → ∀ r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l), Continuous r → IsAutC ι r →
      IsIrr (r.comp (galEmb F E Ω)) → IsAutC ι (r.comp (galEmb F E Ω))

def UpSol : Prop :=
  ∀ (F E Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [IsTotallyComplex F] [Field E] [NumberField E] [IsTotallyComplex E] [Field Ω] [Algebra F E]
    [Algebra F Ω] [Algebra E Ω] [IsScalarTower F E Ω] [IsAlgClosure E Ω] [IsGalois F E] [Group.IsSolvable (E ≃ₐ[F] E)] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime]
    (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (n : ℕ), 1 ≤ n → ∀ r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l), Continuous r → IsAutC ι r →
      IsIrr (r.comp (galEmb F E Ω)) → IsAutC ι (r.comp (galEmb F E Ω))

def TwC : Prop :=
  ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [IsTotallyComplex F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime]
    (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (n : ℕ) (r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) (κ : G F Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ), IsAlgChar ι κ → IsAutC ι r → IsAutC ι (tw r κ)

def TwR : Prop :=
  ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [IsTotallyReal F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime]
    (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (n : ℕ) (r : G F Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) (κ : G F Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ), IsAlgChar ι κ → IsAutR ι r → IsAutR ι (tw r κ)

def AlgCyc : Prop :=
  ∀ (F Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field Ω] [Algebra F Ω] [IsAlgClosure F Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ),
    (∀ (j : ℤ) (χ : G F Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ), IsOpen (χ.ker : Set (G F Ω)) → IsAlgChar ι (cycBar F Ω l ^ j * χ)) ∧
    (∀ κ κ' : G F Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ, IsAlgChar ι κ → IsAlgChar ι κ' → IsAlgChar ι (κ * κ')) ∧
    ∀ κ : G F Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ, IsAlgChar ι κ → IsAlgChar ι κ⁻¹

def AlgRes : Prop :=
  ∀ (F E Ω : Type) [Field F] [NumberField F] [Field E] [NumberField E] [Field Ω] [Algebra F E] [Algebra F Ω] [Algebra E Ω]
    [IsScalarTower F E Ω] [IsAlgClosure E Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (κ : G F Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ),
    IsAlgChar ι κ → IsAlgChar ι (κ.comp (galEmb F E Ω))

def ToCsd : Prop :=
  ∀ (L Ω : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] [Algebra L Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime]
    (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (n : ℕ), 1 ≤ n → ∀ (r : G L Ω →* GL (Fin n) (Qbar l)) (c : G ℚ Ω), IsCC c →
    (∃ P, IsPair c r (cycBar L Ω l ^ (1 - (n : ℤ))) P) → IsAutC ι r → IsAutCsd ι r

def Twist : Prop :=
  ∀ (K Ω : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsTotallyComplex K] [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] [Algebra K Ω],
    Module.finrank ℚ K = 2 → ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime], l ≠ 2 → ¬ (l : ℤ) ∣ discr K → ∀ (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (c : G ℚ Ω), IsCC c →
    ∃ κ : G K Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ, IsTwist ι c κ

def Seed3 : Prop :=
  ∀ (K L Ω : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω]
    [Algebra K Ω] [Algebra L Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (c : G ℚ Ω) (N w : ℕ) (χ' η θ : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ) (j : ℕ),
    FieldPairCharacterTripleConditions K L c N w χ' η θ j → χ' ≠ η → ∀ B : ℕ, ∃ m ≥ B, m ≡ j [MOD l - 1] ∧ ∃ r : G L Ω →* GL (Fin 3) (Qbar l), IrrOpen r ∧
      IsSeed ι c (fun i => (![cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ j * χ', 1, (cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ j * χ')⁻¹] i).comp (galEmb ℚ L Ω))
        (fun i => (![χ', 1, χ'⁻¹] i).comp (galEmb ℚ L Ω)) ![(m : ℤ), 0, -(m : ℤ)] (cycBar L Ω l)⁻¹ r

def Seed2 : Prop :=
  ∀ (K L Ω : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [Field L] [NumberField L] [IsCMField L] [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω]
    [Algebra K Ω] [Algebra L Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (c : G ℚ Ω) (N w : ℕ) (χ' η θ : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ) (j : ℕ),
    FieldPairCharacterTripleConditions K L c N w χ' η θ j → ∀ (κ : G K Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ) (ka : G L Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ), IsTwist ι c κ →
    (∀ (s : G L Ω) (s' : G K Ω), galEmb ℚ L Ω s = galEmb ℚ K Ω s' → ka s = κ s') →
    ∀ B : ℕ, ∃ m ≥ B, m ≡ j [MOD l - 1] ∧ ∃ σ : G ℚ Ω →* GL (Fin 2) (Qbar l), Continuous σ ∧
      Matrix.GeneralLinearGroup.det.comp σ = η * θ ∧ ResSum σ ![cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ j * χ' * η, (cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ j * χ')⁻¹] ∧
      IsSeed ι c (fun i => (![cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ j * χ', (cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ j * χ')⁻¹] i).comp (galEmb ℚ L Ω))
        (fun i => (![χ', χ'⁻¹] i).comp (galEmb ℚ L Ω)) ![(m : ℤ), -(m : ℤ)] ka (σ.comp (galEmb ℚ L Ω))

def SeedSym : Prop :=
  ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (c : G ℚ Ω), IsCC c →
    ∀ (N w : ℕ) (ξ θ : G ℚ Ω →* (Qbar l)ˣ), SlopeTestRemainingChoices N w θ → SplitAt N w ξ → ξ c = -1 → (∃ s, ¬ Cong (ξ s ^ 2) 1) →
    ∀ B : ℕ, ∃ k ≥ B, k ≡ 1 [MOD l - 1] ∧ ∃ ρ, EisRep ι ξ θ k ρ ∧ IrrOpen ρ ∧ IrrOpen (sym3 ρ) ∧ IsAutR ι (sym3 ρ)

def RibetLat : Prop :=
  ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (Γ : Type) [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ] [IsTopologicalGroup Γ] [CompactSpace Γ] [T2Space Γ]
    (ρ : Γ →* GL (Fin 2) (Qbar l)) (a b : Γ →* (Qbar l)ˣ), Continuous ρ → IsIrr ρ → (∀ s, ‖(a s : Qbar l)‖ = 1 ∧ ‖(b s : Qbar l)‖ = 1) →
    ResSum ρ ![a, b] → ∀ g₀ : Γ, ¬ Cong (a g₀) (b g₀) → ∃ ρ' : Γ →* GL (Fin 2) (Qbar l), RepIso ρ ρ' ∧
      ρ' g₀ 0 1 = 0 ∧ ρ' g₀ 1 0 = 0 ∧ (∀ s, ‖ρ' s 0 0 - a s‖ < 1 ∧ ‖ρ' s 1 1 - b s‖ < 1 ∧ ‖ρ' s 1 0‖ < 1 ∧ ‖ρ' s 0 1‖ ≤ 1) ∧
      ∃ s, ‖ρ' s 0 1‖ = 1

end UpperTheory.V.Sd


[AFE_SRC_ORDINARY_END] Exact OrdPt, FinEnd, AutEnd2/3, HostEnd and GdEnd
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/RatTorsionFree.lean#L1-L72
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.AutomorphicCases.AutomorphyClauseStatement
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.AutomorphicCases.AutomorphyUnramifiedCoefficients
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.IsUnitOrIsUnitOfAddOne

noncomputable section

open NumberField Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Aut

namespace UpperTheory.V.Pr

instance (priority := high) ratTorsionFree (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] : Module.IsTorsionFree ℚ Ω :=
  DivisionSemiring.to_moduleIsTorsionFree

variable {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω]

section Ord

variable (l : ℕ) {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ) (σ g₁ : G ℚ Ω)
  (B : ValuationSubring Ω)

structure OrdPt {A : Type} [CommRing A] [IsLocalRing A] [Algebra O A] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 A) : Prop where
  red : UnitaryThreeInertia.Red η Ψ r
  cont : UnitaryThreeInertia.AdicCont (maximalIdeal A) r
  norm : Normalised σ g₁ r
  away : ∀ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬ B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 → ∀ s ∈ Ine (F := ℚ) B', η s = 1 → Ψ s = 1 → r s = 1
  ord : UnitaryThreeInertia.CondT (η.comp (Dec (F := ℚ) B).subtype) (Ψ.comp (Dec (F := ℚ) B).subtype)
    ((Ine (F := ℚ) B).subgroupOf (Dec (F := ℚ) B)) (r.comp (Dec (F := ℚ) B).subtype)

def FinEnd : Prop :=
  ∃ U : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω), U.FiniteIndex ∧ ∀ (A : Type) [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] [IsCD O A],
    (∀ a ∈ maximalIdeal O, algebraMap O A a = 0) → ∀ r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 A, OrdPt l η Ψ σ g₁ B r →
      ∀ g ∈ U, η g = 1 → (r g).mat.charpoly = (((r0 η Ψ g).mat).map (algebraMap O A)).charpoly

end Ord

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) (η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* c.Oˣ) (σ g₁ : G ℚ Ω) (B : ValuationSubring Ω)

def AutEnd3 (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) : Prop :=
  ∀ (A : Subalgebra c.O (PadicAlgCl l)) [IsDiscreteValuationRing A] [Module.Finite c.O A] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 A),
    OrdPt l η Ψ σ g₁ B r →
    (∀ U : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω), IsOpen (U : Set (G ℚ Ω)) → U ≤ η.ker → MatIrr U (mats (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r))) →
    ∀ [Algebra K Ω] (ρ : G K Ω →* GL (Fin 3) (Qbar l)),
      (∀ s, (ρ s).val = (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r (Base.galEmb ℚ K Ω s)).mat) →
      Af.IsAutCsd ι (Af.tw ρ (cycBar K Ω l)⁻¹)

def AutEnd2 (e : ℕ) (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) : Prop :=
  ∀ (A : Subalgebra c.O (PadicAlgCl l)) [IsDiscreteValuationRing A] [Module.Finite c.O A] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 A),
    OrdPt l η Ψ σ g₁ B r → TypeII η (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r) → (∀ g ∈ η.ker, (r g).mat 1 1 = 1) →
    ∀ W : G ℚ Ω →* GL (Fin 2) (PadicAlgCl l),
      (∀ g ∈ η.ker, (W g).val = corner (UnitaryThreeInertia.push A.val r g).mat) →
      (W σ).val.trace = 0 →
      ∃ f : Af.Newform (2 * e + 1), f.Att ι (Af.tw W (cycBar ℚ Ω l ^ (-(e : ℤ))))

def HostEnd : Prop :=
  ∀ (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K], IsQi K →
  ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (c : Coef l)
    (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (o₁ : ℕ), UnitaryThreeInertia.HostData l e ψ o₁ →
  ∀ σ : G ℚ Ω, IsCC σ → ∀ B : ValuationSubring Ω, B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 →
  ∃ g₁, Marks (UnitaryThreeInertia.eta4 Ω) (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) σ g₁ ∧
    FinEnd l (UnitaryThreeInertia.eta4 Ω) (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) σ g₁ B ∧
    AutEnd3 c (UnitaryThreeInertia.eta4 Ω) (Chr.Psi l e (Chr.dirG ℚ Ω ψ)) σ g₁ B K ι

def GdEnd : Prop :=
  ∀ (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (K : Type) [Field K] [NumberField K] [IsCMField K] [Algebra K Ω]
    (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (c : Coef l) (N : ℕ) [NeZero N] (e : ℕ) (ψ : DirichletCharacter c.O N) (D : Dat Ω K c e ψ)
    (ι : Qbar l ≃+* ℂ) (B : ValuationSubring Ω), B.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 →
  ∃ g₁, Marks D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ ∧ FinEnd l D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ B ∧ AutEnd3 c D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ B K ι ∧
    AutEnd2 c D.η D.Ψ D.cc g₁ B e ι

end UpperTheory.V.Pr

end


[AFE_SRC_NREP] Full original ordinary-point-to-NRep transport
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/ExistsNrep3.lean#L1-L443
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.RatTorsionFree
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.HostFrameEx
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.PropagationTheoremGL2
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.MatGstar

open NumberField IsLocalRing Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Aut UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V
open Matrix
open scoped Pointwise

private def diagCh {D A : Type} [Group D] [CommRing A] (T : D →* Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A)
    (hT : ∀ g a b, b < a → T g a b = 0) (i : Fin 3) : D →* A where
  toFun g := T g i i
  map_one' := by simp
  map_mul' g h := by
    rw [map_mul, Matrix.mul_apply, Finset.sum_eq_single i]
    · intro b _ hb
      rcases lt_or_gt_of_ne hb with h1 | h1
      · rw [hT g i b h1, zero_mul]
      · rw [hT h b i h1, mul_zero]
    · exact fun h => absurd (Finset.mem_univ _) h

private theorem ord_zero {D A : Type} [CommRing A] (T R : D → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A)
    (hT : ∀ g a b, b < a → T g a b = 0) (P Q : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A) (hPQ : P * Q = 1) (hQP : Q * P = 1)
    (hR : ∀ g, R g = P * T g * Q) (g : Fin 3 → D) : Fr.ordProd R (fun i s => T s i i) g = 0 := by
  have h10 : ∀ s, T s 1 0 = 0 := fun s => hT s 1 0 (by decide)
  have h20 : ∀ s, T s 2 0 = 0 := fun s => hT s 2 0 (by decide)
  have h21 : ∀ s, T s 2 1 = 0 := fun s => hT s 2 1 (by decide)
  have conj : ∀ s (t : A), R s - t • (1 : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A) = P * (T s - t • 1) * Q := by
    intro s t
    rw [hR, Matrix.mul_sub, Matrix.sub_mul, Matrix.mul_smul, Matrix.smul_mul, Matrix.mul_one, hPQ]
  have key : (T (g 0) - T (g 0) 0 0 • (1 : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A)) *
      ((T (g 1) - T (g 1) 1 1 • 1) * (T (g 2) - T (g 2) 2 2 • 1)) = 0 := by
    ext i j
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>
      simp [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, Matrix.sub_apply, Matrix.one_apply, h10, h20, h21]
  simp only [Fr.ordProd, List.ofFn_succ, List.ofFn_zero, List.prod_cons, List.prod_nil, mul_one]
  simp only [Fin.succ_zero_eq_one, Fin.succ_one_eq_two]
  rw [conj, conj, conj]
  have e : P * (T (g 0) - T (g 0) 0 0 • 1) * Q * (P * (T (g 1) - T (g 1) 1 1 • 1) * Q *
      (P * (T (g 2) - T (g 2) 2 2 • 1) * Q)) =
      P * ((T (g 0) - T (g 0) 0 0 • 1) * ((T (g 1) - T (g 1) 1 1 • 1) * (T (g 2) - T (g 2) 2 2 • 1))) * Q := by
    simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc]
    rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc Q P, hQP, Matrix.one_mul, ← Matrix.mul_assoc Q P, hQP, Matrix.one_mul]
  rw [e, key, Matrix.mul_zero, Matrix.zero_mul]

private theorem descend {H Γ A : Type} [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H] [CompactSpace H] [Group Γ] [TopologicalSpace Γ]
    [T2Space Γ] [CommRing A] (I : Ideal A) (p : H →* Γ) (hp : Continuous p) (hs : Function.Surjective p)
    (r : H →* GG 3 A) (hk : p.ker ≤ r.ker) (hc : UnitaryThreeInertia.AdicCont I r) :
    ∃ r' : Γ →* GG 3 A, (∀ s, r' (p s) = r s) ∧ UnitaryThreeInertia.AdicCont I r' := by
  let r' : Γ →* GG 3 A :=
    p.liftOfRightInverse (Function.surjInv hs) (Function.rightInverse_surjInv hs) ⟨r, hk⟩
  have hr' : ∀ s, r' (p s) = r s := fun s => MonoidHom.liftOfRightInverse_comp_apply _ _ _ _ s
  refine ⟨r', hr', fun n => ?_⟩
  have hq : Topology.IsQuotientMap p := IsClosedMap.isQuotientMap hp.isClosedMap hp hs
  intro U
  have h := hc n U
  rw [← hq.isOpen_preimage]
  convert h using 1
  ext s
  simp [hr']
