private theorem closed_one {H A : Type} [Group H] [TopologicalSpace H] [CommRing A] [IsLocalRing A] [IsNoetherianRing A]
    (r : H →* GG 3 A) (hc : UnitaryThreeInertia.AdicCont (maximalIdeal A) r) : IsClosed {s | (r s).mat = 1} := by
  have hne : maximalIdeal A ≠ ⊤ := (maximalIdeal.isMaximal A).ne_top
  have hbot := Ideal.iInf_pow_eq_bot_of_isLocalRing (maximalIdeal A) hne
  have e : {s | (r s).mat = 1} = ⋂ n : ℕ, {s | (r s).mat.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal A ^ n)) =
      (1 : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A).map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal A ^ n))} := by
    ext s
    simp only [Set.mem_setOf_eq, Set.mem_iInter]
    constructor
    · intro h n
      rw [h]
    · intro h
      ext i j
      rw [← sub_eq_zero]
      have hm : (r s).mat i j - (1 : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A) i j ∈ ⨅ n, maximalIdeal A ^ n := by
        rw [Submodule.mem_iInf]
        intro n
        have := congrFun (congrFun (h n) i) j
        simp only [Matrix.map_apply] at this
        exact Ideal.Quotient.eq.mp this
      rw [hbot] at hm
      exact (Submodule.mem_bot _).mp hm
  rw [e]
  exact isClosed_iInter fun n => (hc n).isClosed_fiber _

private theorem z_diag {A : Type} [CommRing A] (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A)
    (h01 : (X - Jmat A 3 * Xᵀ * Jmat A 3) 0 1 = 0) (h10 : (X - Jmat A 3 * Xᵀ * Jmat A 3) 1 0 = 0) (i j : Fin 3) (h : i ≠ j) :
    (X - Jmat A 3 * Xᵀ * Jmat A 3) i j = 0 := by
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>
    simp_all [Jmat, Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, Matrix.sub_apply, Matrix.transpose_apply, Fin.rev] <;>
    first
    | rfl
    | linear_combination h01
    | linear_combination -h01
    | linear_combination h10
    | linear_combination -h10
    | exact absurd rfl h

private theorem upl_compact (Ω : Type) [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] (L : Type) [Field L] [NumberField L]
    [Algebra L Ω] : CompactSpace (upl Ω L) := by
  have stab : ∀ z : Ω, IsClosed {s : G ℚ Ω | s z = z} := by
    intro z
    have hz : IsIntegral ℚ z := (Algebra.IsAlgebraic.isAlgebraic (R := ℚ) z).isIntegral
    haveI := IntermediateField.adjoin.finiteDimensional hz
    have hle : (IntermediateField.adjoin ℚ {z}).fixingSubgroup ≤ MulAction.stabilizer (G ℚ Ω) z := by
      intro s hs
      rw [MulAction.mem_stabilizer_iff, AlgEquiv.smul_def]
      exact (IntermediateField.mem_fixingSubgroup_iff _ s).mp hs z
        (IntermediateField.mem_adjoin_simple_self ℚ z)
    have hopen := Subgroup.isOpen_mono hle (IntermediateField.fixingSubgroup_isOpen _)
    have hcl := Subgroup.isClosed_of_isOpen _ hopen
    convert hcl using 1
    ext s
    simp [MulAction.mem_stabilizer_iff, AlgEquiv.smul_def]
  have hcl : IsClosed ((upl Ω L : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) : Set (G ℚ Ω)) := by
    have e : ((upl Ω L : Subgroup (G ℚ Ω)) : Set (G ℚ Ω)) =
        ⋂ x ∈ maximalRealSubfield L, {s : G ℚ Ω | s (algebraMap L Ω x) = algebraMap L Ω x} := by
      ext s
      simp [upl]
      exact Iff.rfl
    rw [e]
    exact isClosed_biInter fun x _ => stab _
  exact isCompact_iff_compactSpace.mp hcl.isCompact

private theorem mat_mul {A : Type} [CommRing A] (x y : GG 3 A) (hx : x.right = 1) : (x * y).mat = x.mat * y.mat := by
  simp [GG.mat, hx]

private theorem conj_mat {G A : Type} [Group G] [CommRing A] (h : GL (Fin 3) A) (r : G →* GG 3 A) (s : G)
    (hs : (r s).right = 1) : (conjBy h⁻¹ r s).mat = (h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) A).val * (r s).mat * h.val := by
  simp [conjBy, GG.mat, MulAut.conj_apply, hs, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, SemidirectProduct.mul_right,
    mul_assoc]

private theorem local_chars {G H O A : Type} [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [IsTopologicalGroup G] [Group H]
    [TopologicalSpace H] [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CommRing A] [IsLocalRing A] [Algebra O A]
    (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* Oˣ) (D I : Subgroup G) (r : G →* GG 3 A) (hr : UnitaryThreeInertia.Red η Ψ r)
    (hc : UnitaryThreeInertia.AdicCont (maximalIdeal A) r)
    (ht : UnitaryThreeInertia.CondT (η.comp D.subtype) (Ψ.comp D.subtype) (I.subgroupOf D) (r.comp D.subtype))
    (φ : H →* G) (hφ : Continuous φ) (γ : G) (hγ : η γ = 1) (h1 : ∀ g, η (φ g) = 1) (hD : ∀ g, γ⁻¹ * φ g * γ ∈ D) :
    ∃ ψ : Fin 3 → H →* Aˣ, (∀ g, Fr.ordProd (fun s => (r (φ s)).mat) (fun i s => (ψ i s : A)) g = 0) ∧
      (∀ i, Alg.AdicCont (maximalIdeal A) fun g => (ψ i g : A)) ∧
      (∀ i g, residue A (ψ i g) = residue A (algebraMap O A (UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ i (φ g)))) ∧
      ∀ i g, γ⁻¹ * φ g * γ ∈ I → (ψ i g : A) = algebraMap O A (UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ i (φ g)) := by
  obtain ⟨h, hh, htri⟩ := ht
  have hmult : ∀ g, (r g).right = η g := hr.mult
  let d : H →* D :=
    { toFun := fun g => ⟨γ⁻¹ * φ g * γ, hD g⟩
      map_one' := by
        ext
        simp
      map_mul' := by
        intro a b
        ext
        simp only [map_mul, Subgroup.coe_mul]
        group }
  have hd : ∀ g, ((d g : D) : G) = γ⁻¹ * φ g * γ := fun _ => rfl
  have hd_eta : ∀ g, η ((d g : D) : G) = 1 := by
    intro g
    rw [hd, map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, hγ, h1]
    simp
  have hγinv : η γ⁻¹ = 1 := by rw [map_inv, hγ, inv_one]
  let R' := conjBy h⁻¹ (r.comp D.subtype)
  have hright : ∀ x : D, (R' x).right = (r x).right := by
    intro x
    simp [R', conjBy, MulAut.conj_apply]
  have hrd : ∀ g, (r ((d g : D) : G)).right = 1 := fun g => by rw [hmult]; exact hd_eta g
  let Tm : H →* Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A :=
    { toFun := fun g => (R' (d g)).mat
      map_one' := by
        simp [GG.mat]
      map_mul' := by
        intro a b
        rw [map_mul, map_mul, mat_mul _ _ (by rw [hright]; exact hrd a)] }
  have hTm : ∀ g, Tm g = (R' (d g)).mat := fun _ => rfl
  have hTconj : ∀ g, Tm g = (h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) A).val * (r ((d g : D) : G)).mat * h.val := by
    intro g
    rw [hTm]
    exact conj_mat h (r.comp D.subtype) (d g) (hrd g)
  have hwt : ∀ a b : Fin 3, b < a → wt a b < 0 := by
    unfold wt wt1
    decide
  have hT : ∀ g a b, b < a → Tm g a b = 0 := by
    intro g a b hab
    refine htri.tri (d g) ?_ a b (hwt a b hab)
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker, MonoidHom.comp_apply, Subgroup.coe_subtype]
    exact hd_eta g
  let ψ : Fin 3 → H →* Aˣ := fun i => (diagCh Tm hT i).toHomUnits
  have hψ : ∀ i g, (ψ i g : A) = Tm g i i := fun _ _ => rfl
  have hrγ : (r γ).right = 1 := by rw [hmult, hγ]
  have hrγi : (r γ⁻¹).right = 1 := by rw [hmult, hγinv]
  have hγγ : (r γ).mat * (r γ⁻¹).mat = 1 := by
    rw [← mat_mul _ _ hrγ, ← map_mul, mul_inv_cancel, map_one]
    simp [GG.mat]
  have hγγ' : (r γ⁻¹).mat * (r γ).mat = 1 := by
    rw [← mat_mul _ _ hrγi, ← map_mul, inv_mul_cancel, map_one]
    simp [GG.mat]
  have hhh : h.val * (h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) A).val = 1 := by
    rw [← Units.val_mul, mul_inv_cancel, Units.val_one]
  have hhh' : (h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) A).val * h.val = 1 := by
    rw [← Units.val_mul, inv_mul_cancel, Units.val_one]
  obtain ⟨P, hP⟩ : ∃ P : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A, P = (r γ).mat * h.val := ⟨_, rfl⟩
  obtain ⟨Q, hQ⟩ : ∃ Q : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A, Q = (h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) A).val * (r γ⁻¹).mat := ⟨_, rfl⟩
  have hPQ : P * Q = 1 := by
    rw [hP, hQ, Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Matrix.mul_assoc h.val, hhh, Matrix.one_mul, hγγ]
  have hQP : Q * P = 1 := by
    rw [hP, hQ, Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Matrix.mul_assoc (r γ⁻¹).mat, hγγ', Matrix.one_mul, hhh']
  have hφd : ∀ g, φ g = γ * ((d g : D) : G) * γ⁻¹ := by
    intro g
    rw [hd]
    group
  have hR : ∀ g, (r (φ g)).mat = P * Tm g * Q := by
    intro g
    rw [hφd g, map_mul, map_mul, mul_assoc, mat_mul _ _ hrγ, mat_mul _ _ (hrd g), hTconj, hP, hQ]
    simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc]
    rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc h.val (h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) A).val, hhh, Matrix.one_mul,
      ← Matrix.mul_assoc h.val (h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) A).val, hhh, Matrix.one_mul]
  have hT' : ∀ g, Tm g = Q * (r (φ g)).mat * P := by
    intro g
    rw [hR, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, ← Matrix.mul_assoc, hQP, Matrix.one_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc, hQP,
      Matrix.mul_one]
  have req : ∀ x y : A, x - y ∈ maximalIdeal A → residue A x = residue A y := by
    intro x y hxy
    rw [← sub_eq_zero, ← map_sub, residue_eq_zero_iff]
    exact hxy
  have hone : (1 : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A).map (residue A) = 1 :=
    Matrix.map_one _ (map_zero _) (map_one _)
  have hmh : h.val.map (residue A) = 1 := by
    rw [← hone]
    ext a b
    exact req _ _ (by simpa [Matrix.sub_apply] using hh a b)
  have hmhi : (h⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) A).val.map (residue A) = 1 := by
    have e := congrArg (fun X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A => X.map (residue A)) hhh'
    simp only [Matrix.map_mul, hmh, hone, Matrix.mul_one] at e
    exact e
  have hΨ : ∀ g i, UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ i ((d g : D) : G) = UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ i (φ g) := by
    intro g i
    unfold UnitaryThreeInertia.psi
    rw [hd, map_mul, map_mul, map_inv, mul_comm (Ψ γ)⁻¹, inv_mul_cancel_right]
  have hres : ∀ i g, residue A (ψ i g : A) = residue A (algebraMap O A (UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ i (φ g))) := by
    intro i g
    have e0 : (Tm g).map (residue A) = (r ((d g : D) : G)).mat.map (residue A) := by
      rw [hTconj g, Matrix.map_mul, Matrix.map_mul, hmh, hmhi, Matrix.mul_one, Matrix.one_mul]
    have e1 : residue A (Tm g i i) = residue A ((r ((d g : D) : G)).mat i i) :=
      congrFun (congrFun e0 i) i
    rw [hψ, e1, ← hΨ]
    refine req _ _ ?_
    have e2 := hr.red ((d g : D) : G) i i
    have e3 : (r0 η Ψ ((d g : D) : G)).mat = ((tor O (Ψ ((d g : D) : G)) : GL (Fin 3) O) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) O) := rfl
    rw [Matrix.sub_apply, Matrix.map_apply, e3, tor_val, Matrix.diagonal_apply_eq] at e2
    exact e2
  refine ⟨ψ, fun g => ord_zero Tm (fun s => (r (φ s)).mat) hT P Q hPQ hQP hR g, ?_, hres, ?_⟩
  · intro i N
    have e : (fun g => Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal A ^ N) (ψ i g : A)) =
        (fun M : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (A ⧸ maximalIdeal A ^ N) =>
          (Q.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal A ^ N)) * M * P.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal A ^ N))) i i) ∘
        (fun g => (r (φ g)).mat.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal A ^ N))) := by
      funext g
      simp only [Function.comp, hψ]
      rw [hT' g, ← Matrix.map_mul, ← Matrix.map_mul]
      rfl
    rw [e]
    exact ((hc N).comp_continuous hφ).comp _
  · intro i g hg
    have hmem : d g ∈ I.subgroupOf D := by
      rw [Subgroup.mem_subgroupOf]
      exact hg
    have e := htri.ine (d g) hmem (hd_eta g) i
    rw [hψ, hTm, e, ← hΨ]
    rfl

theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.exists_nrep3 :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ} {σ g₁ : G ℚ Ω} {B : ValuationSubring Ω} {M : ℕ}
      {L : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω] {Φ : Fr.Frame l (ResidueField O)} (k : GalLink η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ) [CharP (ResidueField O) l] {C₀ : ℕ}, Φ.Ok C₀ → l ∣ M → σ * σ = 1 →
    ∀ {A : Type} [CommRing A] [IsDomain A] [IsLocalRing A] [IsNoetherianRing A] [Algebra O A] [IsLocalHom (algebraMap O A)] (r : G ℚ Ω →* GG 3 A), OrdPt l η Ψ σ g₁ B r → ∃ ρ : Fr.NRep Φ 3 A, IsLiftA O ρ ∧ (∀ s : G L Ω, ρ.r (k.p0 s) = (r (Base.galEmb ℚ L Ω s)).mat) ∧
      ∀ v i, ∀ g ∈ Φ.Iv v, (ρ.ψ v i g : A) = algebraMap O A (UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ i (k.j v g)) := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l _ O _ _ η Ψ σ g₁ B M L _ _ _ Φ k _ C₀ hOk hlM hσ A _ _ _ _ _ _ r hr
  haveI := upl_compact Ω L
  let ru : ↥(upl Ω L) →* GG 3 A := r.comp (upl Ω L).subtype
  have ru_cont : UnitaryThreeInertia.AdicCont (maximalIdeal A) ru := fun n =>
    (hr.cont n).comp_continuous continuous_subtype_val
  have hk : k.p.ker ≤ ru.ker := by
    intro s hs
    have hs1 : k.p s = 1 := hs
    have hη : η s = 1 := by
      rw [← k.del s, hs1, map_one]
    have hN : Subgroup.normalClosure
        {s : ↥(upl Ω L) | ∃ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧ (s : G ℚ Ω) ∈ Ine B' ∧
          η s = 1 ∧ (barChar O Ψ) s = 1} ≤ ru.ker := by
      refine Subgroup.normalClosure_le_normal ?_
      rintro t ⟨B', hB', hI, ht1, hb⟩
      have hΨ : Ψ t = 1 := k.teich B' hB' t ⟨hI, t.2⟩ hb
      exact hr.away B' hB' t hI ht1 hΨ
    have hcl := closed_one ru ru_cont
    rw [k.p_ker] at hs
    have hs2 : s ∈ closure ((Subgroup.normalClosure
        {s : ↥(upl Ω L) | ∃ B' : ValuationSubring Ω, ¬B'.valuation (l : Ω) < 1 ∧ (s : G ℚ Ω) ∈ Ine B' ∧
          η s = 1 ∧ (barChar O Ψ) s = 1} : Subgroup ↥(upl Ω L)) : Set ↥(upl Ω L)) := by
      rw [← Subgroup.topologicalClosure_coe]
      exact hs
    have hmat : (ru s).mat = 1 := by
      refine closure_minimal (fun t ht => ?_) hcl hs2
      have h1 : ru t = 1 := hN ht
      show (ru t).mat = 1
      rw [h1]
      rfl
    rw [MonoidHom.mem_ker]
    refine SemidirectProduct.ext (Units.ext hmat) ?_
    rw [SemidirectProduct.one_right]
    exact (hr.red.mult s).trans hη
  obtain ⟨r', hr', hc'⟩ := descend (maximalIdeal A) k.p k.p_cont k.p_surj ru hk ru_cont
  have rt : ∀ x : Φ.Γ, (r' x).right = Φ.del x := by
    intro x
    obtain ⟨s, rfl⟩ := k.p_surj x
    rw [hr', k.del]
    exact hr.red.mult s
  have rt0 : ∀ g : Φ.G0, (r' g).right = 1 := fun g => (rt g).trans g.2
  let ρr : Φ.G0 →* Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A :=
    { toFun := fun g => (r' g).mat
      map_one' := by
        show (r' ((1 : Φ.G0) : Φ.Γ)).mat = 1
        rw [OneMemClass.coe_one, map_one]
        rfl
      map_mul' := fun g h => by
        show (r' ((g * h : Φ.G0) : Φ.Γ)).mat = (r' g).mat * (r' h).mat
        rw [Subgroup.coe_mul, map_mul]
        exact mat_mul _ _ (rt0 g) }
  have hρr : ∀ g : Φ.G0, ρr g = (r' g).mat := fun _ => rfl
  obtain ⟨hσu, hpσ⟩ := k.c
  obtain ⟨hg1u, hpg1⟩ := k.g0
  have rσ : r' Φ.c = r σ := by rw [← hpσ, hr']; rfl
  have rg1 : r' Φ.g0 = r g₁ := by rw [← hpg1, hr']; rfl
  have atc : (r' Φ.c).mat = 1 := by rw [rσ]; exact hr.norm.atc
  have rtc : (r' Φ.c).right = -1 := by rw [rt]; exact hOk.del_c
  have cinv : Φ.c⁻¹ = Φ.c := inv_eq_of_mul_eq_one_right hOk.c_sq
  have star_eq : ∀ g : Φ.G0, ((Φ.star g : Φ.G0) : Φ.Γ) = gstar Φ.c (g : Φ.Γ) := by
    intro g
    show ((MulAut.conjNormal Φ.c) g⁻¹ : Φ.Γ) = Φ.c * (g : Φ.Γ)⁻¹ * Φ.c
    rw [MulAut.conjNormal_apply, cinv]
    rfl
  have pol : ∀ g : Φ.G0, ρr (Φ.star g) = Jmat A 3 * (ρr g)ᵀ * Jmat A 3 := by
    intro g
    rw [hρr, hρr, star_eq]
    exact (mat_gstar r' Φ.c g atc rtc (rt0 g)).1
  have diag : ∀ i j : Fin 3, i ≠ j → (ρr Φ.g0 - ρr (Φ.star Φ.g0)) i j = 0 := by
    intro i j hij
    have hσr : (r σ).right = -1 := by rw [← rσ]; exact rtc
    have hg1r : (r g₁).right = 1 := by rw [← rg1]; exact rt0 _
    have hz := (mat_gstar r σ g₁ hr.norm.atc hσr hg1r).1
    have e : ρr Φ.g0 - ρr (Φ.star Φ.g0) = (r g₁).mat - Jmat A 3 * (r g₁).matᵀ * Jmat A 3 := by
      rw [pol, hρr, rg1]
    rw [e]
    have z12 := hr.norm.z12
    have z21 := hr.norm.z21
    simp only [zmat, hz] at z12 z21
    exact z_diag _ z12 z21 i j hij
  have LC : ∀ v : Φ.V, ∃ ψ : Fin 3 → Φ.Gv v →* Aˣ,
      (∀ g, Fr.ordProd (fun s => (r (k.j v s)).mat) (fun i s => (ψ i s : A)) g = 0) ∧
      (∀ i, Alg.AdicCont (maximalIdeal A) fun g => (ψ i g : A)) ∧
      (∀ i g, residue A (ψ i g) = residue A (algebraMap O A (UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ i (k.j v g)))) ∧
      ∀ i, ∀ g ∈ Φ.Iv v, (ψ i g : A) = algebraMap O A (UnitaryThreeInertia.psi Ψ i (k.j v g)) := by
    intro v
    obtain ⟨γ, hγ, hpl⟩ := k.pl_conj v
    have hlift : IsLift (RingEquiv.refl ℚ) (γ : Ω ≃ₐ[ℚ] Ω).toRingEquiv := fun x => γ.commutes x
    have hmemiff : ∀ x : Ω, (γ : Ω ≃ₐ[ℚ] Ω).toRingEquiv x ∈ k.pl v ↔ x ∈ B := by
      intro x
      rw [hpl]
      exact ValuationSubring.smul_mem_pointwise_smul_iff
    have hconj : ∀ g : Φ.Gv v, γ⁻¹ * ((k.j v g : G ℚ Ω)) * γ = conjGal hlift (k.j v g) := by
      intro g
      apply AlgEquiv.ext
      intro x
      rfl
    have hD : ∀ g, γ⁻¹ * ((upl Ω L).subtype.comp (k.j v)) g * γ ∈ Dec B := by
      intro g
      show γ⁻¹ * ((k.j v g : G ℚ Ω)) * γ ∈ Dec B
      rw [hconj]
      exact conjGal_mem_Dec hlift hmemiff ((k.j_range v _).mp ⟨g, rfl⟩)
    have h1 : ∀ g, η (((upl Ω L).subtype.comp (k.j v)) g) = 1 := by
      intro g
      show η (k.j v g : G ℚ Ω) = 1
      rw [← k.del, ← k.j_loc]
      exact (Φ.loc0 v g).2
    obtain ⟨ψ, h1', h2', h3', h4'⟩ := local_chars η Ψ (Dec B) (Ine B) r hr.red hr.cont hr.ord
      ((upl Ω L).subtype.comp (k.j v)) (continuous_subtype_val.comp (k.j_cont v)) γ hγ h1 hD
    refine ⟨ψ, h1', h2', h3', fun i g hg => h4' i g ?_⟩
    show γ⁻¹ * ((k.j v g : G ℚ Ω)) * γ ∈ Ine B
    rw [hconj]
    exact conjGal_mem_Ine hlift hmemiff ((k.j_ine v g).mp hg)
  choose ψ hψ1 hψ2 hψ3 hψ4 using LC
  have rloc : ∀ v (s : Φ.Gv v), ρr (Φ.loc0 v s) = (r (k.j v s)).mat := by
    intro v s
    rw [hρr]
    show (r' (Φ.loc v s)).mat = _
    rw [k.j_loc, hr']
    rfl
  have redg : ∀ (s : G ℚ Ω) (i j : Fin 3), residue A ((r s).mat i j) =
      residue A (algebraMap O A ((Matrix.diagonal fun a => ((Ψ s ^ wt1 a : Oˣ) : O)) i j)) := by
    intro s i j
    have h0 : (r0 η Ψ s).mat = Matrix.diagonal fun a => ((Ψ s ^ wt1 a : Oˣ) : O) := tor_val (Ψ s)
    have h := hr.red.red s i j
    rw [h0, Matrix.sub_apply, Matrix.map_apply] at h
    rw [← sub_eq_zero, ← map_sub]
    exact (residue_eq_zero_iff _).mpr h
  have bar_eq : ∀ (s : ↥(upl Ω L)) (i : Fin 3),
      ResidueField.map (algebraMap O A) ((Φ.Ψ (k.p s) ^ Fr.w 3 i : (ResidueField O)ˣ) : ResidueField O) =
        residue A (algebraMap O A ((Ψ s ^ wt1 i : Oˣ) : O)) := by
    intro s i
    have hw : Fr.w 3 i = wt1 i := by simp [Fr.w, wt1]
    rw [k.psi, hw, barChar, MonoidHom.comp_apply, ← map_zpow, Units.coe_map]
    simp [ResidueField.map_residue]
  refine ⟨⟨ρr, ψ, pol, diag, fun v g => ?_⟩, ⟨⟨?_, ?_⟩, ⟨?_, ?_⟩, ?_⟩, ?_, hψ4⟩
  · have e : (fun s => ρr (Φ.loc0 v s)) = fun s => (r (k.j v s)).mat := funext (rloc v)
    show Fr.ordProd (fun s => ρr (Φ.loc0 v s)) (fun i s => ((ψ v i s : Aˣ) : A)) g = 0
    rw [e]
    exact hψ1 v g
  · intro i j N
    have h := (hc' N).comp_continuous (continuous_subtype_val : Continuous (fun g : Φ.G0 => (g : Φ.Γ)))
    exact h.comp (fun X => X i j)
  · intro v i
    exact hψ2 v i
  · intro g i
    obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := k.p_surj (g : Φ.Γ)
    show residue A (ρr g i i) = _
    rw [hρr, ← hs, hr', bar_eq]
    show residue A ((r s).mat i i) = _
    rw [redg, Matrix.diagonal_apply_eq]
  · intro v i g
    show residue A ((ψ v i g : Aˣ) : A) = _
    rw [hψ3, Fr.Frame.loc, MonoidHom.comp_apply]
    show _ = ResidueField.map (algebraMap O A) ((Φ.Ψ (Φ.loc v g) ^ Fr.w 3 i : (ResidueField O)ˣ) : ResidueField O)
    rw [k.j_loc, bar_eq]
    rfl
  · intro g i j hij
    obtain ⟨s, hs⟩ := k.p_surj (g : Φ.Γ)
    show residue A (ρr g i j) = 0
    rw [hρr, ← hs, hr']
    show residue A ((r s).mat i j) = 0
    rw [redg, Matrix.diagonal_apply_ne _ hij, map_zero, map_zero]
  · intro s
    rw [hρr]
    show (r' (k.p _)).mat = _
    rw [hr']
    rfl


[AFE_SRC_FIN_END] Full original finEnd_of_finPt with subgroup before coefficients
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/FinEndOfFinPt.lean#L1-L48
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.RatTorsionFree
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.HostFrameEx
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.PropagationTheoremGL2
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.ExistsNrep3
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.GaloisLibrary.SelmerTransport.GalEmbTop

open NumberField IsLocalRing Formalism.ConjugateSelfDual ReciprocityTheory.Gal GlobalTheory.GalOfCusp UpperTheory.V.HV UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Aut UpperTheory.V.Pr UpperTheory.V

theorem UpperTheory.V.Pr.finEnd_of_finPt :
    ∀ {Ω : Type} [Field Ω] [CharZero Ω] [IsAlgClosure ℚ Ω] {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {η : G ℚ Ω →* ℤˣ} {Ψ : G ℚ Ω →* Oˣ} {σ g₁ : G ℚ Ω} {B : ValuationSubring Ω} {M : ℕ}
      {L : Type} [Field L] [NumberField L] [Algebra L Ω] {Φ : Fr.Frame l (ResidueField O)} (k : GalLink η Ψ σ g₁ B M L Φ) [CharP (ResidueField O) l] {C₀ : ℕ}, Φ.Ok C₀ → l ∣ M → σ * σ = 1 →
    FinPt O Φ 3 → FinEnd l η Ψ σ g₁ B := by
  intro Ω _ _ _ l _ O _ _ η Ψ σ g₁ B M L _ _ _ Φ k _ C₀ hok hl hσ hF
  haveI : Module.IsTorsionFree L Ω := DivisionSemiring.to_moduleIsTorsionFree
  haveI : IsAlgClosure L Ω := ⟨IsAlgClosure.isAlgClosed ℚ, Algebra.IsAlgebraic.tower_top (K := ℚ) L⟩
  have hidx : (Base.galOf ℚ L Ω).index = Module.finrank ℚ L := (Base.galEmbTop ℚ L Ω).2.2
  refine ⟨Base.galOf ℚ L Ω, ⟨by rw [hidx]; exact Module.finrank_pos.ne'⟩, ?_⟩
  intro A _ _ _ h0 r hr g hg _
  haveI : IsNoetherianRing A := PrincipalIdealRing.isNoetherianRing
  let f : ResidueField O →+* A := Ideal.Quotient.lift (maximalIdeal O) (algebraMap O A) h0
  have hf : ∀ a : O, f (residue O a) = algebraMap O A a := fun a => rfl
  letI : Algebra (ResidueField O) A := f.toAlgebra
  haveI : IsScalarTower O (ResidueField O) A := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun a => (hf a).symm
  obtain ⟨ρ, h1, h2, h3⟩ := exists_nrep3 k hok hl hσ r hr
  have hpow : ∀ (s : G ℚ Ω) (n : ℤ), algebraMap O A ((Ψ s ^ n : Oˣ) : O) =
      f ((barChar O Ψ s ^ n : (ResidueField O)ˣ) : ResidueField O) := by
    intro s n
    rw [← hf]
    congr 1
    exact congrArg Units.val (map_zpow (Units.map (residue O).toMonoidHom) (Ψ s) n)
  have key := hF A ρ h1 (by
    intro v i g hg h
    apply Units.ext
    rw [h3 v i g hg, UnitaryThreeInertia.psi, hpow]
    rw [k.j_loc, k.psi] at h
    rw [h]; simp)
  obtain ⟨s, rfl⟩ : ∃ s, Base.galEmb ℚ L Ω s = g := (Base.range_galEmb ℚ L Ω).ge hg
  rw [← h2, key]
  have : ((r0 η Ψ (Base.galEmb ℚ L Ω s)).mat).map (algebraMap O A) = Matrix.diagonal fun i =>
      algebraMap O A ((Ψ (Base.galEmb ℚ L Ω s) ^ wt1 i : Oˣ) : O) := by
    rw [GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, tor_val, Matrix.diagonal_map (map_zero _)]
  rw [this, Matrix.charpoly_diagonal]
  refine Finset.prod_congr rfl fun i _ => ?_
  rw [hpow]
  have h5 : Φ.Ψ (k.p0 s) = barChar O Ψ (Base.galEmb ℚ L Ω s) := k.psi _
  have h6 : Fr.w 3 i = wt1 i := by fin_cases i <;> rfl
  rw [h5, h6]
  rfl


[AFE_SRC_FIN_PT] Literal IsLiftA and retained FinPt
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/PropagationTheoremGL2.lean#L1-L135
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.FlipTwoThree
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CurveDVRSpecialization.IsUnitOrIsUnitOfAddOne
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.LeAdicClosure
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.WeilGroupCharacters.UniversalOrdinaryDeterminantRing

noncomputable section

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Fr

namespace UpperTheory.V.Pr

section Nice

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] {F : Frame l k} {n : ℕ} {R : Type} [CommRing R]

structure Nice (ρ : NRep F n R) (p : Ideal R) : Prop where
  prime : p.IsPrime
  dim : qdim p = 1
  char : (l : R) ∈ p
  off : ρ.OffTri p
  sep : ∀ v i j, i ≠ j → ¬ ρ.Coin p v i j

def ThickLink [IsLocalRing R] (ρ : NRep F 3 R) (p q : Ideal R) : Prop :=
  Nice ρ p ∧ ρ.TE p ∧ Nice ρ q ∧ ρ.TE q ∧ ∃ e : Ideal R, e.IsPrime ∧ Thick ρ e ∧ e ≤ p ∧ e ≤ q

end Nice

section Input

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {n : ℕ}

def P1' (S : Space O F n) (M : Ideal S.B) : Prop :=
  ∀ (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x) (p : Ideal L.R), Nice L.ρ p → L.ρ.TI p → M ≤ L.π p →
    ∀ P : Ideal L.R, P.IsPrime → P ≤ p → M ≤ L.π P

def P23' (S : Space O F 3) (M : Ideal S.B) : Prop :=
  ∀ (x : Ideal S.A) (L : S.Loc x) (p q : Ideal L.R), Nice L.ρ p → L.ρ.TE p → Relation.ReflTransGen (ThickLink L.ρ) p q →
    (∃ P : Ideal L.R, P.IsPrime ∧ P ≤ p ∧ L.ρ.TI P ∧ M ≤ L.π P) →
    ∀ P' : Ideal L.R, P'.IsPrime → P' ≤ q → L.ρ.TI P' → M ≤ L.π P'

def SeedIn (S : Space O F n) (M : Ideal S.B) : Prop :=
  ∃ (ξ : Ideal S.A) (Z : Ideal S.B), ξ.IsPrime ∧ S.u.TI ξ ∧ Z.IsPrime ∧ M ≤ Z ∧ Z ≤ S.π ξ ∧
    qdim Z = ((1 + n * F.d : ℕ) : WithBot ℕ∞)

def AllGood (S : Space O F n) (M : Ideal S.B) : Prop := ∀ p : Ideal S.A, p.IsPrime → S.u.TI p → M ≤ S.π p

def OffFin (S : Space O F n) : Prop := ∃ I : Ideal S.B, S.FinLam I ∧ ∀ p : Ideal S.A, p.IsPrime → S.u.OffTri p → I ≤ S.π p

end Input

def PropagationTheoremGL2 : Prop :=
  ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O]
    (F : Frame l (ResidueField O)) (C₀ : ℕ), F.Ok C₀ → F.Big C₀ → ∀ (S : Space O F 2) (M : Ideal S.B),
    S.FinLam M → P1' S M → SeedIn S M → AllGood S M

def PropagationTheoremGL3 : Prop :=
  ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O]
    (F : Frame l (ResidueField O)) (C₀ : ℕ), F.Ok C₀ → F.Big C₀ → ∀ (S₂ : Space O F 2) (M₂ : Ideal S₂.B),
    S₂.FinLam M₂ → AllGood S₂ M₂ → ∀ (S : Space O F 3) (M : Ideal S.B),
    S.FinLam M → P1' S M → P23' S M → SeedIn S M → AllGood S M ∧ OffFin S

section Points

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {F : Frame l (ResidueField O)} {n : ℕ}

structure IsLiftA {A : Type} [CommRing A] [IsLocalRing A] [Algebra O A] [IsLocalHom (algebraMap O A)]
    (ρ : NRep F n A) : Prop where
  cont : ρ.Cont (maximalIdeal A)
  bar : (ρ.map (residue A)).IsBar (ResidueField.map (algebraMap O A))
  diag : (ρ.map (residue A)).IsDiag

variable (F n) in

def FinPt : Prop :=
  ∀ (A : Type) [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] [IsCD O A] [Algebra (ResidueField O) A] [IsScalarTower O (ResidueField O) A]
    (ρ : NRep F n A), IsLiftA O ρ → (∀ v i, ∀ g ∈ F.Iv v, F.Ψ (F.loc v g) = 1 → ρ.ψ v i g = 1) →
    ∀ g, (ρ.r g).charpoly =
      ∏ i, (Polynomial.X - Polynomial.C (algebraMap (ResidueField O) A ((F.Ψ g ^ w n i : (ResidueField O)ˣ) : ResidueField O)))

end Points

def FinPtOf : Prop :=
  ∀ (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsCoef l O]
    (F : Frame l (ResidueField O)) (C₀ : ℕ), F.Ok C₀ → ∀ S : Space O F 3, OffFin S → FinPt O F 3

section Link

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [Algebra ℤ_[l] O]
  (F : Frame l (ResidueField O)) {n : ℕ} {G : W.GFrame l} (r : W.Resid G O n)

structure TwLink where
  π : G.G₀ →* F.G0
  π_cont : Continuous π
  π_surj : Function.Surjective π
  π_conj : ∀ g, π (G.conj g) = F.conj (π g)
  π_eps : ∀ g : G.G₀, F.eps (π g) = G.ε g
  e : G.Vl ≃ F.V
  lam : ∀ v, G.Gv v ≃ₜ* F.Gv (e v)
  lam_loc : ∀ v g, F.loc0 (e v) (lam v g) = π (G.φ v g)
  lam_I : ∀ v g, lam v g ∈ F.Iv (e v) ↔ g ∈ G.Iv v
  κ : G.G₀ →* Oˣ
  κ_cont : AdicCont (maximalIdeal O) fun g => (κ g : O)
  κ_conj : ∀ g, κ g * κ (G.conj g) = G.eps O O g ^ (1 - (n : ℤ))
  chib : ∀ i g, r.chib i g = F.Ψ (π g) ^ w n i * W.pushCh (residue O) κ g
  psib : ∀ v i g, r.psib v i g = r.chib i (G.φ v g)
  mu : ∀ v, G.mu v ≤ (commutator (G.Gv v)).topologicalClosure
  tame : ∀ u, ∀ g ∈ G.Iu u, ∃ m : ℕ, ¬ l ∣ m ∧ π g ^ m = 1 ∧ κ g ^ m = 1
  ramκ : ∀ g ∈ G.I0, κ g = 1
  ker : ∀ N : Subgroup G.G₀, N.Normal → IsClosed (N : Set G.G₀) → (∀ g ∈ N, G.conj g ∈ N) →
    (∀ u, ∀ g ∈ G.Iu u, F.Ψ (π g) = 1 → g ∈ N) → G.I0 ≤ N → π.ker ≤ N

namespace TwLink

variable {F r} (K : TwLink F r) {A : Type} [CommRing A] [Algebra O A]

def κA (A : Type) [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] : G.G₀ →* Aˣ := W.pushCh (algebraMap O A) K.κ

def tw (ρ : NRep F n A) : G.G₀ →* Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) A where
  toFun g := ((K.κA A g : Aˣ) : A) • ρ.r (K.π g)
  map_one' := by simp
  map_mul' g h := by simp [smul_smul, mul_comm]

def twψ (ρ : NRep F n A) (v : G.Vl) (i : Fin n) : G.Gv v →* Aˣ :=
  (ρ.ψ (K.e v) i).comp (K.lam v).toMulEquiv.toMonoidHom * (K.κA A).comp (G.φ v)

def IsPhi (B : W.UniversalOrdinaryDeterminantRing r) (S : Space O F n) (Φ : B.B →ₐ[O] S.B) : Prop :=
  B.u.Maps ((S.B.val.comp Φ : B.B →ₐ[O] S.R) : B.B →+* S.R) (W.GDet.ofRep (K.tw S.ρ)) (K.twψ S.ρ)

end TwLink

end Link

end UpperTheory.V.Pr

end


[AFE_SRC_NREP_DEFS] Actual NRep, ordered product, IsBar and IsDiag
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/LeAdicClosure.lean#L1-L269
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.FrobeniusSmoothVectors.ContinuousCoboundaries2

noncomputable section

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg UpperTheory.V.U3

namespace UpperTheory.V.Fr

def w (n : ℕ) (i : Fin n) : ℤ := if n = 3 then 1 - (i : ℕ) else (n : ℤ) - 1 - 2 * (i : ℕ)

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] (F : Frame l k) (n : ℕ)

def ordProd {H K : Type} [CommRing K] {n : ℕ} (r : H → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) (ψ : Fin n → H → K) (g : Fin n → H) :
    Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K := (List.ofFn fun i => r (g i) - ψ i (g i) • (1 : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K)).prod

theorem ordProd_map {H K K' : Type} [CommRing K] [CommRing K'] {n : ℕ} (f : K →+* K')
    (r : H → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K) (ψ : Fin n → H → K) (g : Fin n → H) :
    ordProd (fun s => f.mapMatrix (r s)) (fun i s => f (ψ i s)) g = f.mapMatrix (ordProd r ψ g) := by
  simp only [ordProd, map_list_prod, List.map_ofFn]
  congr 1
  refine congrArg List.ofFn (funext fun i => ?_)
  simp only [Function.comp_apply, map_sub]
  congr 1
  ext a b
  by_cases h : a = b <;> simp [h, Matrix.one_apply]

@[ext] structure NRep (K : Type) [CommRing K] where
  r : F.G0 →* Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K
  ψ : ∀ v, Fin n → F.Gv v →* Kˣ
  pol : ∀ g, r (F.star g) = Jmat K n * (r g)ᵀ * Jmat K n
  diag : ∀ i j, i ≠ j → (r F.g0 - r (F.star F.g0)) i j = 0
  ord : ∀ v g, ordProd (fun s => r (F.loc0 v s)) (fun i s => (ψ v i s : K)) g = 0

namespace NRep

variable {F n} {K K' : Type} [CommRing K] [CommRing K']

def map (f : K →+* K') (ρ : NRep F n K) : NRep F n K' where
  r := (f.mapMatrix : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) K →+* _).toMonoidHom.comp ρ.r
  ψ v i := (Units.map f.toMonoidHom).comp (ρ.ψ v i)
  pol g := by
    show f.mapMatrix (ρ.r (F.star g)) = Jmat K' n * (f.mapMatrix (ρ.r g))ᵀ * Jmat K' n
    rw [ρ.pol, map_mul, map_mul]
    simp only [RingHom.mapMatrix_apply, Jmat_map, Matrix.transpose_map]
  diag i j h := by
    show f (ρ.r F.g0 i j) - f (ρ.r (F.star F.g0) i j) = 0
    rw [← map_sub]
    exact (congrArg f (ρ.diag i j h)).trans (map_zero f)
  ord v g := (ordProd_map f _ _ g).trans (by rw [ρ.ord, map_zero])

@[simp] theorem map_r (f : K →+* K') (ρ : NRep F n K) (g : F.G0) (i j : Fin n) :
    (ρ.map f).r g i j = f (ρ.r g i j) := rfl

@[simp] theorem map_ψ (f : K →+* K') (ρ : NRep F n K) (v : F.V) (i : Fin n) (g : F.Gv v) :
    ((ρ.map f).ψ v i g : K') = f (ρ.ψ v i g) := rfl

theorem map_map {K'' : Type} [CommRing K''] (f : K →+* K') (f' : K' →+* K'') (ρ : NRep F n K) :
    (ρ.map f).map f' = ρ.map (f'.comp f) := rfl

def restrict {O : Type} [CommRing O] [Algebra O K] (ρ : NRep F n K) (A : Subalgebra O K)
    (h1 : ∀ g i j, ρ.r g i j ∈ A) (h2 : ∀ v i g, (ρ.ψ v i g : K) ∈ A) : NRep F n A where
  r :=
    { toFun := fun g => Matrix.of fun i j => ⟨ρ.r g i j, h1 g i j⟩
      map_one' := by
        ext i j
        by_cases h : i = j <;> simp [h, Matrix.one_apply]
      map_mul' := fun g h => by
        ext i j
        simp [Matrix.mul_apply] }
  ψ v i :=
    { toFun := fun g => ⟨⟨ρ.ψ v i g, h2 v i g⟩, ⟨ρ.ψ v i g⁻¹, h2 v i g⁻¹⟩, Subtype.ext (by simp), Subtype.ext (by simp)⟩
      map_one' := by ext; simp
      map_mul' := fun g h => by ext; simp }
  pol g := by
    refine Matrix.map_injective (f := (A.val : A →+* K)) Subtype.val_injective ?_
    show _ = (A.val : A →+* K).mapMatrix _
    rw [map_mul, map_mul]
    simp only [RingHom.mapMatrix_apply, Jmat_map]
    exact ρ.pol g
  diag i j h := Subtype.ext (ρ.diag i j h)
  ord v g := by
    refine Matrix.map_injective (f := (A.val : A →+* K)) Subtype.val_injective ?_
    show (A.val : A →+* K).mapMatrix _ = (A.val : A →+* K).mapMatrix _
    rw [← ordProd_map, map_zero]
    exact ρ.ord v g

def Cont (I : Ideal K) (ρ : NRep F n K) : Prop :=
  (∀ i j, AdicCont I fun g => ρ.r g i j) ∧ ∀ v i, AdicCont I fun g => (ρ.ψ v i g : K)

def IsBar (f : k →+* K) (ρ : NRep F n K) : Prop :=
  (∀ g i, ρ.r g i i = f ((F.Ψ g ^ w n i : kˣ) : k)) ∧ ∀ v i g, (ρ.ψ v i g : K) = f ((F.Ψ (F.loc v g) ^ w n i : kˣ) : k)

def IsDiag (ρ : NRep F n K) : Prop := ∀ g i j, i ≠ j → ρ.r g i j = 0

def triP (ρ : NRep F n K) : Ideal K := Ideal.span {x | ∃ g i j, j < i ∧ x = ρ.r g i j}

def triM (ρ : NRep F n K) : Ideal K := Ideal.span {x | ∃ g i j, i < j ∧ x = ρ.r g i j}

def ee (ρ : NRep F n K) : Ideal K := Ideal.span {x | ∃ g i j, Odd (w n i - w n j) ∧ x = ρ.r g i j}

def OffTri (ρ : NRep F n K) (p : Ideal K) : Prop := ¬ ρ.triP ≤ p ∧ ¬ ρ.triM ≤ p

def TI (ρ : NRep F n K) (p : Ideal K) : Prop := ρ.OffTri p ∧ (n = 3 → ¬ ρ.ee ≤ p)

def TE (ρ : NRep F n K) (p : Ideal K) : Prop := ρ.OffTri p ∧ n = 3 ∧ ρ.ee ≤ p

def Coin (ρ : NRep F n K) (p : Ideal K) (v : F.V) (i j : Fin n) : Prop :=
  ∀ g ∈ F.Nv v, (ρ.ψ v i g : K) - ρ.ψ v j g ∈ p

def mon (ρ : NRep F n K) (j : ℤ) : Set K :=
  {x | ∃ L : List (F.G0 × Fin n × Fin n), (L.map fun s => w n s.2.1 - w n s.2.2).sum = j ∧
    x = (L.map fun s => ρ.r s.1 s.2.1 s.2.2).prod}

end NRep

def Irr {H K : Type} [Field K] {ι : Type} [Fintype ι] (r : H → Matrix ι ι K) : Prop :=
  ∀ U : Submodule K (ι → K), (∀ g, ∀ u ∈ U, r g *ᵥ u ∈ U) → U = ⊥ ∨ U = ⊤

def adicClosure {O R : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing R] [IsLocalRing R] [Algebra O R] (S : Subalgebra O R) :
    Subalgebra O R where
  carrier := {x | ∀ N : ℕ, ∃ y ∈ S, x - y ∈ maximalIdeal R ^ N}
  mul_mem' {a b} ha hb N := by
    obtain ⟨x, hx, h1⟩ := ha N
    obtain ⟨y, hy, h2⟩ := hb N
    refine ⟨x * y, mul_mem hx hy, ?_⟩
    have : a * b - x * y = (a - x) * b + x * (b - y) := by ring
    rw [this]
    exact add_mem (Ideal.mul_mem_right _ _ h1) (Ideal.mul_mem_left _ _ h2)
  add_mem' {a b} ha hb N := by
    obtain ⟨x, hx, h1⟩ := ha N
    obtain ⟨y, hy, h2⟩ := hb N
    refine ⟨x + y, add_mem hx hy, ?_⟩
    rw [add_sub_add_comm]
    exact add_mem h1 h2
  algebraMap_mem' c N := ⟨_, S.algebraMap_mem c, by simp⟩

theorem le_adicClosure {O R : Type} [CommRing O] [CommRing R] [IsLocalRing R] [Algebra O R] (S : Subalgebra O R) :
    S ≤ adicClosure S := fun x hx _ => ⟨x, hx, by simp⟩

class IsCoef (l : outParam ℕ) (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] : Prop where
  complete : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O
  fin : Finite (ResidueField O)
  char : CharP (ResidueField O) l

attribute [instance] IsCoef.complete IsCoef.fin IsCoef.char

section Space

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] (O : Type) [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] (F : Frame l (ResidueField O)) (n : ℕ)

variable {F n} in

def IsLift {R : Type} [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (ρ : NRep F n R) : Prop :=
  ρ.Cont (maximalIdeal R) ∧ (ρ.map (res O R : R →+* ResidueField O)).IsBar (RingHom.id _) ∧
    (ρ.map (res O R : R →+* ResidueField O)).IsDiag

structure Space : Type 1 where
  R : Type
  [cr : CommRing R]
  [al : Algebra O R]
  [co : IsCO O R]
  ρ : NRep F n R
  lift : IsLift O ρ
  univ : ∀ (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O C] [IsCO O C] (ρ' : NRep F n C), IsLift O ρ' →
    ∃! f : R →ₐ[O] C, ρ.map (f : R →+* C) = ρ'

attribute [instance] Space.cr Space.al Space.co

namespace Space

variable {O F n} (S : Space O F n)

def B : Subalgebra O S.R :=
  adicClosure (Algebra.adjoin O (S.ρ.mon 0 ∪ {x | ∃ v i g, x = (S.ρ.ψ v i g : S.R)}))

def A : Subalgebra O S.R := Algebra.adjoin O ((S.B : Set S.R) ∪ {x | ∃ g i j, x = S.ρ.r g i j})

theorem B_le_A : S.B ≤ S.A := fun _ hx => Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inl hx)

theorem ψ_mem (v : F.V) (i : Fin n) (g : F.Gv v) : (S.ρ.ψ v i g : S.R) ∈ S.B :=
  le_adicClosure _ (Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inr ⟨v, i, g, rfl⟩))

theorem mon_mem {x : S.R} (hx : x ∈ S.ρ.mon 0) : x ∈ S.B := le_adicClosure _ (Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inl hx))

theorem r_mem (g : F.G0) (i j : Fin n) : S.ρ.r g i j ∈ S.A := Algebra.subset_adjoin (Or.inr ⟨g, i, j, rfl⟩)

def inc : S.B →ₐ[O] S.A := Subalgebra.inclusion S.B_le_A

instance : Algebra S.B S.A := (S.inc : S.B →+* S.A).toAlgebra

instance : IsScalarTower O S.B S.A := IsScalarTower.of_algebraMap_eq fun _ => rfl

def u : NRep F n S.A := S.ρ.restrict S.A S.r_mem fun v i g => S.B_le_A (S.ψ_mem v i g)

def ψB (v : F.V) (i : Fin n) (g : F.Gv v) : S.B := ⟨S.ρ.ψ v i g, S.ψ_mem v i g⟩

def aB (g : F.G0) (i : Fin n) : S.B := ⟨S.ρ.r g i i, S.mon_mem ⟨[(g, i, i)], by simp, by simp⟩⟩

def trB (g : F.G0) : S.B := ∑ i, S.aB g i

def mB : Ideal S.B := (maximalIdeal S.R).comap (S.B.val : S.B →+* S.R)

def π (p : Ideal S.A) : Ideal S.B := p.comap (S.inc : S.B →+* S.A)

def gr (j : ℤ) : Submodule S.B S.A := Submodule.span S.B (S.u.mon j)

def IsHom (I : Ideal S.A) : Prop := I = Ideal.span {x | x ∈ I ∧ ∃ j, x ∈ S.gr j}

def lamId : Ideal S.B :=
  (maximalIdeal O).map (algebraMap O S.B) ⊔
    Ideal.span {x | ∃ v i g, g ∈ F.Iv v ∧ F.Ψ (F.loc v g) = 1 ∧ x = S.ψB v i g - 1}

def FinLam (I : Ideal S.B) : Prop := S.mB ≤ (I ⊔ S.lamId).radical

def BB : Set (PrimeSpectrum S.A) := {p | S.mB ≤ S.π p.asIdeal ∧ ¬ S.u.triP ≤ p.asIdeal}

structure Loc (x : Ideal S.A) : Type 1 where
  O' : Type
  [cO : CommRing O']
  [dO : IsDomain O']
  [vO : IsDiscreteValuationRing O']
  [coef : IsCoef l O']
  [aO : Algebra O O']
  [fO : Module.Finite O O']
  unr : (maximalIdeal O).map (algebraMap O O') = maximalIdeal O'
  R : Type
  [cr : CommRing R]
  [al' : Algebra O' R]
  [co : IsCO O' R]
  [al : Algebra O R]
  [tw : IsScalarTower O O' R]
  ι : S.A →ₐ[O] R
  lies : (maximalIdeal R).comap (ι : S.A →+* R) = x
  univ : ∀ (C : Type) [CommRing C] [Algebra O' C] [IsCO O' C] [Algebra O C] [IsScalarTower O O' C]
    (f : S.A →ₐ[O] C), (∀ a, res O' C (f a) = res O' R (ι a)) → ∃! g : R →ₐ[O'] C, ∀ a, g (ι a) = f a

attribute [instance] Loc.cO Loc.dO Loc.vO Loc.coef Loc.aO Loc.fO Loc.cr Loc.al' Loc.co Loc.al Loc.tw

namespace Loc

variable {S} {x : Ideal S.A} (L : S.Loc x)

def ρ : NRep F n L.R := S.u.map (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R)

def im (P : Ideal L.R) : Ideal S.A := P.comap (L.ι : S.A →+* L.R)

def π (P : Ideal L.R) : Ideal S.B := S.π (L.im P)

end Loc

end Space

end Space

structure IsThickMod {F : Frame l k} {Q : Type} [CommRing Q] (ρ : NRep F 3 Q) (r : F.G0 →* Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) Q) :
    Prop where
  even : ∀ g i j, ¬ Odd (w 3 i - w 3 j) → r g i j = ρ.r g i j
  sub : ∀ g, r g 1 0 = 0 ∧ r g 1 2 = 0
  ord : ∀ v g, ordProd (fun s => r (F.loc0 v s)) (fun i s => (ρ.ψ v i s : Q)) g = 0
  ord_star : ∀ v g, ordProd (fun s => r (F.star (F.loc0 v s))) (fun i s => (ρ.ψ v i.rev s : Q)) g = 0
  nonsplit : ∀ (K : Type) [Field K] (f : Q →+* K), Function.Injective f →
    ¬ ∃ y : Fin 3 → K, y 1 = 1 ∧ ∀ g, (r g).map f *ᵥ y = f (r g 1 1) • y

def Thick {F : Frame l k} {R : Type} [CommRing R] [IsLocalRing R] (ρ : NRep F 3 R) (P : Ideal R) : Prop :=
  ρ.TE P ∧ ∃ r : F.G0 →* Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) (R ⧸ P),
    (∀ i j, AdicCont ((maximalIdeal R).map (Ideal.Quotient.mk P)) fun g => r g i j) ∧
      IsThickMod (ρ.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk P)) r

end UpperTheory.V.Fr


[AFE_SRC_FRAME_DEFS] Frame topologies, localization and Ok normalization
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/FrobeniusSmoothVectors/ContinuousCoboundaries2.lean#L1-L216
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CommutativeAlgebra.FramedTwoCoboundaries
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.UnitaryThreeReflectionRight

noncomputable section

open IsLocalRing Matrix UpperTheory.V.Alg

namespace UpperTheory.V.Fr

section Cone

variable {k G W V : Type} [CommRing k] [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [AddCommGroup W] [Module k W]
  {Gv : V → Type} [∀ v, Group (Gv v)] [∀ v, TopologicalSpace (Gv v)]
  (loc : ∀ v, Gv v →* G) (ρ : Representation k G W) (b : V → Submodule k W)

def coneH0 : Submodule k W where
  carrier := {X | (∀ g, ρ g X = X) ∧ ∀ v, X ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨a1, a2⟩ ⟨b1, b2⟩
    exact ⟨fun g => by rw [map_add, a1, b1], fun v => add_mem (a2 v) (b2 v)⟩
  zero_mem' := ⟨fun g => map_zero _, fun v => zero_mem _⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨a1, a2⟩
    exact ⟨fun g => by rw [map_smul, a1], fun v => Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a2 v)⟩

def Z1C : Submodule k ((G → W) × (V → W)) where
  carrier := {x | IsLocallyConstant x.1 ∧ (∀ s t, x.1 (s * t) = x.1 s + ρ s (x.1 t)) ∧
    ∀ v s, x.1 (loc v s) - (ρ (loc v s) (x.2 v) - x.2 v) ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨a1, a2, a3⟩ ⟨b1, b2, b3⟩
    refine ⟨a1.add b1, fun s t => ?_, fun v s => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, a2, b2, map_add]; abel
    · convert add_mem (a3 v s) (b3 v s) using 1
      simp only [Prod.fst_add, Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply, map_add]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨IsLocallyConstant.const 0, by simp, by simp⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨a1, a2, a3⟩
    refine ⟨a1.comp (c • ·), fun s t => by simp [a2], fun v s => ?_⟩
    convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a3 v s) using 1
    simp [smul_sub]

def continuousCoboundaries1 : Submodule k ((G → W) × (V → W)) where
  carrier := {x | ∃ X : W, (∀ s, x.1 s = ρ s X - X) ∧ ∀ v, x.2 v - X ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨X, a1, a2⟩ ⟨Y, b1, b2⟩
    refine ⟨X + Y, fun s => ?_, fun v => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, a1, b1, map_add]; abel
    · convert add_mem (a2 v) (b2 v) using 1
      simp only [Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨0, by simp, by simp⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨X, a1, a2⟩
    refine ⟨c • X, fun s => by simp [a1, smul_sub], fun v => ?_⟩
    convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a2 v) using 1
    simp [smul_sub]

abbrev H1C : Type := Z1C loc ρ b ⧸ (continuousCoboundaries1 ρ b).comap (Z1C loc ρ b).subtype

def continuousCocycles2 : Submodule k ((G × G → W) × (∀ v, Gv v → W)) where
  carrier := {x | IsLocallyConstant x.1 ∧ (∀ v, IsLocallyConstant (x.2 v)) ∧
    (∀ s t u, ρ s (x.1 (t, u)) + x.1 (s, t * u) = x.1 (s * t, u) + x.1 (s, t)) ∧
    ∀ v s t, x.1 (loc v s, loc v t) - dOne (ρ.comp (loc v)) (x.2 v) (s, t) ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨a1, a2, a3, a4⟩ ⟨b1, b2, b3, b4⟩
    refine ⟨a1.add b1, fun v => (a2 v).add (b2 v), fun s t u => ?_, fun v s t => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, map_add]
      rw [add_add_add_comm, a3, b3, add_add_add_comm]
    · convert add_mem (a4 v s t) (b4 v s t) using 1
      simp only [Prod.fst_add, Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply, dOne, map_add]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨IsLocallyConstant.const 0, fun _ => IsLocallyConstant.const 0, by simp, by simp [dOne]⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨a1, a2, a3, a4⟩
    refine ⟨a1.comp (c • ·), fun v => (a2 v).comp (c • ·), fun s t u => ?_, fun v s t => ?_⟩
    · simp only [Prod.smul_fst, Pi.smul_apply, map_smul, ← smul_add, a3]
    · convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a4 v s t) using 1
      simp [dOne, smul_sub]

def continuousCoboundaries2 : Submodule k ((G × G → W) × (∀ v, Gv v → W)) where
  carrier := {x | ∃ (β : G → W) (Y : V → W), IsLocallyConstant β ∧ x.1 = dOne ρ β ∧
    ∀ v s, x.2 v s - (β (loc v s) - (ρ (loc v s) (Y v) - Y v)) ∈ b v}
  add_mem' := by
    rintro x y ⟨β, Y, a1, a2, a3⟩ ⟨β', Y', b1, b2, b3⟩
    refine ⟨β + β', Y + Y', a1.add b1, ?_, fun v s => ?_⟩
    · funext p
      simp only [Prod.fst_add, Pi.add_apply, a2, b2, dOne, map_add]; abel
    · convert add_mem (a3 v s) (b3 v s) using 1
      simp only [Prod.snd_add, Pi.add_apply, map_add]; abel
  zero_mem' := ⟨0, 0, IsLocallyConstant.const 0, by funext p; simp [dOne], by simp⟩
  smul_mem' := by
    rintro c x ⟨β, Y, a1, a2, a3⟩
    refine ⟨c • β, c • Y, a1.comp (c • ·), ?_, fun v s => ?_⟩
    · funext p
      simp [a2, dOne, smul_sub]
    · convert Submodule.smul_mem _ c (a3 v s) using 1
      simp [smul_sub]

abbrev coneH2 : Type := continuousCocycles2 loc ρ b ⧸ (continuousCoboundaries2 loc ρ b).comap (continuousCocycles2 loc ρ b).subtype

end Cone

def chRep {k G : Type} [CommRing k] [Group G] (χ : G →* kˣ) : Representation k G k where
  toFun g := (χ g : k) • LinearMap.id
  map_one' := by ext; simp
  map_mul' g h := by ext; simp [mul_comm]

@[simp] theorem chRep_apply {k G : Type} [CommRing k] [Group G] (χ : G →* kˣ) (g : G) (x : k) :
    chRep χ g x = χ g * x := rfl

structure Frame (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] (k : Type) [Field k] : Type 1 where
  Γ : Type
  [grp : Group Γ]
  [top : TopologicalSpace Γ]
  [tg : IsTopologicalGroup Γ]
  [cpt : CompactSpace Γ]
  [t2 : T2Space Γ]
  [td : TotallyDisconnectedSpace Γ]
  del : Γ →* ℤˣ
  c : Γ
  V : Type
  [fV : Fintype V]
  Gv : V → Type
  [gv : ∀ v, Group (Gv v)]
  [tv : ∀ v, TopologicalSpace (Gv v)]
  [tgv : ∀ v, IsTopologicalGroup (Gv v)]
  [cv : ∀ v, CompactSpace (Gv v)]
  [t2v : ∀ v, T2Space (Gv v)]
  [tdv : ∀ v, TotallyDisconnectedSpace (Gv v)]
  loc0 : ∀ v, Gv v →* del.ker
  Iv : ∀ v, Subgroup (Gv v)
  dv : V → ℕ
  Inf : Type
  [fI : Fintype Inf]
  cw : Inf → Γ
  eps : Γ →* ℤ_[l]ˣ
  Ψ : Γ →* kˣ
  g0 : del.ker

attribute [instance] Frame.grp Frame.top Frame.tg Frame.cpt Frame.t2 Frame.td Frame.fV Frame.gv Frame.tv Frame.tgv
  Frame.cv Frame.t2v Frame.tdv Frame.fI

namespace Frame

variable {l : ℕ} [Fact l.Prime] {k : Type} [Field k] (F : Frame l k)

abbrev G0 : Type := F.del.ker

def loc (v : F.V) : F.Gv v →* F.Γ := F.del.ker.subtype.comp (F.loc0 v)

def conj : F.G0 →* F.G0 := (MulAut.conjNormal F.c : MulAut F.G0).toMonoidHom

def star (g : F.G0) : F.G0 := F.conj g⁻¹

def loc2 (p : F.V × Bool) : F.Gv p.1 →* F.G0 := if p.2 then F.conj.comp (F.loc0 p.1) else F.loc0 p.1

def d : ℕ := ∑ v, F.dv v

def om (K : Type) [CommRing K] [CharP K l] : F.Γ →* Kˣ :=
  (Units.map ((ZMod.castHom (dvd_refl l) K).comp PadicInt.toZMod).toMonoidHom).comp F.eps

def delK (K : Type) [CommRing K] : F.Γ →* Kˣ := (Units.map (Int.castRingHom K).toMonoidHom).comp F.del

def Nv (v : F.V) : Subgroup (F.Gv v) := F.Iv v ⊓ (F.eps.comp (F.loc v)).ker

def ext {k' : Type} [Field k'] (f : k →+* k') : Frame l k' :=
  { F with Ψ := (Units.map f.toMonoidHom).comp F.Ψ }

def EC : Prop :=
  ∀ (K W : Type) [Field K] [Finite K] [CharP K l] [AddCommGroup W] [Module K W] [Module.Finite K W]
    (ρ : Representation K F.Γ W) (b : F.V → Submodule K W), IsOpen (ρ.ker : Set F.Γ) →
    (∀ v s, ∀ X ∈ b v, ρ (F.loc v s) X ∈ b v) →
    (∀ f : W →ₗ[K] K, (∀ g X, f (ρ g X) = F.om K g * f X) → f = 0) →
    Module.Finite K (coneH2 F.loc ρ b) ∧
    Module.finrank K (coneH2 F.loc ρ b) + Module.finrank K (coneH0 ρ b) + ∑ v, F.dv v * Module.finrank K (b v) ≤
      Module.finrank K (H1C F.loc ρ b) + ∑ w, Module.finrank K (LinearMap.ker (ρ (F.cw w) - 1))

structure Ok [CharP k l] (C₀ : ℕ) : Prop where
  l_odd : l ≠ 2
  fin : Finite k
  del_open : IsOpen (F.del.ker : Set F.Γ)
  c_sq : F.c * F.c = 1
  del_c : F.del F.c = -1
  loc_cont : ∀ v, Continuous (F.loc0 v)
  eps_cont : Continuous F.eps
  Ψ_open : IsOpen (F.Ψ.ker : Set F.Γ)
  Ψ_c : F.Ψ F.c = 1
  baseSubgroup_spec : F.Ψ F.g0 ^ 2 ≠ 1
  phi : PhiCond F.Γ l
  card_inf : Fintype.card F.Inf = F.d
  cw_sq : ∀ w, F.cw w * F.cw w = 1
  del_cw : ∀ w, F.del (F.cw w) = -1
  f2a : ∃ g : F.G0, F.om k g ≠ 1
  f2b : ∀ j : ℤ, j = 1 ∨ j = -1 → ∃ g : F.G0, F.om k g * F.Ψ g ^ j ≠ 1
  f2c : ∀ j : ℤ, j = 2 ∨ j = -2 → ∃ g, F.om k g * F.Ψ g ^ j * F.delK k g ≠ 1
  f3 : ∀ v, ∃ g, F.Ψ (F.loc v g) ^ 2 ≠ 1
  f4 : Nontrivial (H1C F.loc (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹ * F.delK k)) fun _ => ⊥)
  f4' : Nontrivial (H1C F.loc (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹ ^ 2 * F.delK k)) fun _ => ⊥)
  f5 : ¬ ∃ j : ℤ, ∀ g : F.G0, F.om k g = F.Ψ g ^ j
  Ψ_N : ∀ v, ∀ g ∈ F.Nv v, F.Ψ (F.loc v g) = 1

  gen : ∀ v, ∃ (u : Fin (F.dv v) → F.Gv v) (a₀ : Fin (F.dv v)), (∀ a, u a ∈ F.Iv v ∧ F.Ψ (F.loc v (u a)) = 1) ∧
    (∀ a, a ≠ a₀ → u a ∈ F.Nv v) ∧
    ∀ (A : Type) [CommGroup A] [Finite A], IsPGroup l A → ∀ χ : F.Gv v →* A, IsOpen (χ.ker : Set (F.Gv v)) →
      (∀ a, χ (u a) = 1) → ∀ g ∈ F.Iv v, χ g = 1

  cl : ∃ h : ℕ, h ≠ 0 ∧ ∀ (A : Type) [CommGroup A] [Finite A], IsPGroup l A → ∀ χ : F.G0 →* A,
    IsOpen (χ.ker : Set F.G0) → (∀ p : F.V × Bool, ∀ g ∈ F.Iv p.1, χ (F.loc2 p g) = 1) → ∀ g, χ g ^ h = 1
  ec : F.EC
  b1p : Module.rank k (H1C F.loc (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹ ^ 2 * F.delK k)) fun _ => ⊥) ≤ C₀
  b1 : Module.rank k (H1C F.loc2 (chRep (F.Ψ⁻¹.comp F.del.ker.subtype)) fun _ => ⊥) ≤ C₀
  h1 : Module.rank k (H1C F.loc (chRep (1 : F.Γ →* kˣ)) fun _ => ⊤) ≤ 1 + C₀

def Big (C₀ : ℕ) : Prop := 3 * C₀ + 5 + 2 < F.d ∧ ∀ v, 5 < F.dv v

end Frame

end UpperTheory.V.Fr


[AFE_SRC_GG] Semidirect group, J, weights, reference representation and Normalised
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/UnitaryThreeReflectionRight.lean#L1-L305
import Mathlib

namespace UpperTheory.V.U3

open Matrix

section Group

variable {n : Type} [Fintype n] [DecidableEq n] {R S : Type*} [CommRing R] [CommRing S]

def trInv (g : GL n R) : GL n R where
  val := ((g⁻¹ : GL n R) : Matrix n n R)ᵀ
  inv := (g : Matrix n n R)ᵀ
  val_inv := by rw [← Matrix.transpose_mul]; simp
  inv_val := by rw [← Matrix.transpose_mul]; simp

@[simp] theorem trInv_val (g : GL n R) :
    (trInv g : Matrix n n R) = ((g⁻¹ : GL n R) : Matrix n n R)ᵀ := rfl

@[simp] theorem trInv_inv_val (g : GL n R) :
    (((trInv g)⁻¹ : GL n R) : Matrix n n R) = (g : Matrix n n R)ᵀ := rfl

theorem trInv_trInv (g : GL n R) : trInv (trInv g) = g := by
  apply Units.ext
  rw [trInv_val, trInv_inv_val, Matrix.transpose_transpose]

theorem trInv_mul (g h : GL n R) : trInv (g * h) = trInv g * trInv h := by
  apply Units.ext
  simp only [trInv_val, Units.val_mul, _root_.mul_inv_rev, Matrix.transpose_mul]

theorem map_trInv (f : R →+* S) (g : GL n R) :
    GeneralLinearGroup.map f (trInv g) = trInv (GeneralLinearGroup.map f g) := by
  apply Units.ext
  simp [GeneralLinearGroup.map, Matrix.transpose_map]

end Group

section Twisted

variable {m : ℕ} {R S : Type*} [CommRing R] [CommRing S]

variable (R) in

def Jmat (m : ℕ) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R := fun i j => if j = i.rev then 1 else 0

theorem Jmat_mul_Jmat (m : ℕ) : Jmat R m * Jmat R m = 1 := by
  ext i j
  simp only [Jmat, Matrix.mul_apply, Matrix.one_apply, mul_ite, mul_one, mul_zero]
  rw [Finset.sum_eq_single i.rev]
  · simp [eq_comm, Fin.rev_eq_iff]
  · intro k _ hk
    simp [hk]
  · simp

theorem Jmat_transpose (m : ℕ) : (Jmat R m)ᵀ = Jmat R m := by
  ext i j
  simp only [Jmat, Matrix.transpose_apply]
  congr 1
  exact propext ⟨fun h => by simp [h], fun h => by simp [h]⟩

theorem Jmat_map (f : R →+* S) (m : ℕ) : (Jmat R m).map f = Jmat S m := by
  ext i j
  simp only [Jmat, Matrix.map_apply]
  split_ifs <;> simp

variable (R) in

def Jgl (m : ℕ) : GL (Fin m) R := ⟨Jmat R m, Jmat R m, Jmat_mul_Jmat m, Jmat_mul_Jmat m⟩

theorem Jgl_mul_Jgl : Jgl R m * Jgl R m = 1 := Units.ext (Jmat_mul_Jmat m)

theorem trInv_Jgl : trInv (Jgl R m) = Jgl R m := Units.ext (Jmat_transpose m)

def theta (g : GL (Fin m) R) : GL (Fin m) R := Jgl R m * trInv g * Jgl R m

theorem theta_val (g : GL (Fin m) R) : (theta g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) =
    Jmat R m * ((g⁻¹ : GL (Fin m) R) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R)ᵀ * Jmat R m := rfl

theorem theta_inv_val (g : GL (Fin m) R) :
    (((theta g)⁻¹ : GL (Fin m) R) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) =
      Jmat R m * (g : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R)ᵀ * Jmat R m := by
  rw [Matrix.mul_assoc]; rfl

theorem theta_theta (g : GL (Fin m) R) : theta (theta g) = g := by
  have hJ : ∀ x : GL (Fin m) R, Jgl R m * (Jgl R m * x) = x := fun x => by
    rw [← mul_assoc, Jgl_mul_Jgl, one_mul]
  simp only [theta, trInv_mul, trInv_Jgl, trInv_trInv, mul_assoc, Jgl_mul_Jgl, mul_one, hJ]

theorem theta_mul (g h : GL (Fin m) R) : theta (g * h) = theta g * theta h := by
  have hJ : ∀ x : GL (Fin m) R, Jgl R m * (Jgl R m * x) = x := fun x => by
    rw [← mul_assoc, Jgl_mul_Jgl, one_mul]
  simp only [theta, trInv_mul, mul_assoc, hJ]

variable (m R) in

def thetaAut : MulAut (GL (Fin m) R) where
  toFun := theta
  invFun := theta
  left_inv := theta_theta
  right_inv := theta_theta
  map_mul' := theta_mul

variable (m R) in

def sgnAct : ℤˣ →* MulAut (GL (Fin m) R) where
  toFun s := if s = 1 then 1 else thetaAut m R
  map_one' := if_pos rfl
  map_mul' s t := by
    rcases Int.units_eq_one_or s with rfl | rfl <;> rcases Int.units_eq_one_or t with rfl | rfl
    · simp
    · simp
    · simp
    · have h : ((-1 : ℤˣ) = 1) = False := by decide
      simp only [neg_mul_neg, mul_one, if_true, h, if_false]
      ext1 g
      exact (theta_theta g).symm

@[simp] theorem sgnAct_one (g : GL (Fin m) R) : sgnAct m R 1 g = g := by simp

@[simp] theorem sgnAct_neg_one (g : GL (Fin m) R) : sgnAct m R (-1) g = theta g := by
  have h : ((-1 : ℤˣ) = 1) = False := by decide
  show (if (-1 : ℤˣ) = 1 then 1 else thetaAut m R) g = theta g
  simp only [h, if_false]
  rfl

variable (m R) in

abbrev GG : Type _ := GL (Fin m) R ⋊[sgnAct m R] ℤˣ

def GG.mat (x : GG m R) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R := (x.left : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R)

abbrev GG.ofGL : GL (Fin m) R →* GG m R := SemidirectProduct.inl

abbrev GG.nu : GG m R →* ℤˣ := SemidirectProduct.rightHom

theorem map_Jgl (f : R →+* S) : GeneralLinearGroup.map f (Jgl R m) = Jgl S m :=
  Units.ext (Jmat_map f m)

theorem map_theta (f : R →+* S) (g : GL (Fin m) R) :
    GeneralLinearGroup.map f (theta g) = theta (GeneralLinearGroup.map f g) := by
  simp only [theta, map_mul, map_trInv, map_Jgl]

def GG.map (f : R →+* S) : GG m R →* GG m S :=
  SemidirectProduct.map (GeneralLinearGroup.map f) (MonoidHom.id ℤˣ) (by
    intro s
    refine MonoidHom.ext fun g => ?_
    rcases Int.units_eq_one_or s with rfl | rfl
    · simp
    · simpa using map_theta f g)

@[simp] theorem GG.map_mat (f : R →+* S) (x : GG m R) : (GG.map f x).mat = x.mat.map f := rfl

@[simp] theorem GG.map_right (f : R →+* S) (x : GG m R) : (GG.map f x).right = x.right := rfl

def thetaLie (X : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R :=
  -(Jmat R m * Xᵀ * Jmat R m)

def GG.adFun (x : GG m R) (X : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R :=
  x.mat * (if x.right = 1 then X else thetaLie X) *
    ((x.left⁻¹ : GL (Fin m) R) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R)

private theorem jmat_mul_jmat_mul (Y : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) :
    Jmat R m * (Jmat R m * Y) = Y := by
  rw [← Matrix.mul_assoc, Jmat_mul_Jmat, Matrix.one_mul]

private theorem thetaLie_thetaLie (X : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) :
    thetaLie (thetaLie X) = X := by
  simp only [thetaLie, Matrix.transpose_neg, Matrix.transpose_mul, Jmat_transpose,
    Matrix.transpose_transpose, Matrix.mul_neg, Matrix.neg_mul, neg_neg, Matrix.mul_assoc,
    jmat_mul_jmat_mul, Jmat_mul_Jmat, Matrix.mul_one]

private theorem theta_conj_thetaLie_eq_thetaLie_conj (b : GL (Fin m) R) (X : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) :
    (theta b : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) * (thetaLie X *
        (((theta b)⁻¹ : GL (Fin m) R) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R)) =
      thetaLie ((b : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) * (X * ((b⁻¹ : GL (Fin m) R) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R))) := by
  simp only [theta_val, theta_inv_val, thetaLie, Matrix.transpose_mul, Jmat_transpose,
    Matrix.mul_neg, Matrix.neg_mul, Matrix.mul_assoc, jmat_mul_jmat_mul]

theorem GG.adFun_mul (x y : GG m R) (X : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) :
    (x * y).adFun X = x.adFun (y.adFun X) := by
  obtain ⟨a, s⟩ := x
  obtain ⟨b, t⟩ := y
  have hn : ((-1 : ℤˣ) = 1) = False := by decide
  rcases Int.units_eq_one_or s with rfl | rfl <;> rcases Int.units_eq_one_or t with rfl | rfl <;>
    simp only [GG.adFun, GG.mat, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, SemidirectProduct.mul_right,
      sgnAct_one, sgnAct_neg_one, hn, if_true, if_false, mul_one, one_mul, neg_mul_neg,
      Units.val_mul, _root_.mul_inv_rev, Matrix.mul_assoc]
  · rw [← theta_conj_thetaLie_eq_thetaLie_conj]
    simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc]
  · rw [← theta_conj_thetaLie_eq_thetaLie_conj, thetaLie_thetaLie]
    simp only [Matrix.mul_assoc]

variable (m R) in

def GG.ad : Representation R (GG m R) (Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) where
  toFun x :=
    { toFun := x.adFun
      map_add' := fun X Y => by
        unfold GG.adFun thetaLie
        split_ifs <;> simp [Matrix.mul_add, Matrix.add_mul, add_comm]
      map_smul' := fun a X => by
        unfold GG.adFun thetaLie
        split_ifs <;> simp }
  map_one' := by
    ext1 X
    simp [GG.adFun, GG.mat]
  map_mul' x y := by
    ext1 X
    exact GG.adFun_mul x y X

@[simp] theorem GG.ad_apply (x : GG m R) (X : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) :
    GG.ad m R x X = x.adFun X := rfl

variable {G : Type*} [Group G]

def conjBy (h : GL (Fin m) R) (r : G →* GG m R) : G →* GG m R :=
  (MulAut.conj (GG.ofGL h)).toMonoidHom.comp r

def glPart (η : G →* ℤˣ) (r : G →* GG m R) (hr : ∀ g, (r g).right = η g) :
    η.ker →* GL (Fin m) R where
  toFun g := (r g).left
  map_one' := by simp
  map_mul' g h := by
    have hg : (r g).right = 1 := (hr g).trans g.2
    simp [hg]

def adRep (r : G →* GG m R) : Representation R G (Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) := (GG.ad m R).comp r

end Twisted

section Three

variable {R : Type*} [CommRing R]

def wt1 (i : Fin 3) : ℤ := 1 - (i : ℕ)

def wt (i j : Fin 3) : ℤ := wt1 i - wt1 j

def torGL (t : Rˣ) : GL (Fin 3) R where
  val := Matrix.diagonal fun i => ((t ^ wt1 i : Rˣ) : R)
  inv := Matrix.diagonal fun i => (((t ^ wt1 i)⁻¹ : Rˣ) : R)
  val_inv := by rw [Matrix.diagonal_mul_diagonal]; simp only [Units.mul_inv]; exact Matrix.diagonal_one
  inv_val := by rw [Matrix.diagonal_mul_diagonal]; simp only [Units.inv_mul]; exact Matrix.diagonal_one

variable (R) in

def tor : Rˣ →* GL (Fin 3) R where
  toFun := torGL
  map_one' := by
    apply Units.ext
    simp [torGL]
  map_mul' s t := by
    apply Units.ext
    simp [torGL, mul_zpow, Matrix.diagonal_mul_diagonal]

theorem tor_val (t : Rˣ) : (tor R t : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) =
    Matrix.diagonal fun i => ((t ^ wt1 i : Rˣ) : R) := rfl

theorem theta_tor (t : Rˣ) : theta (tor R t) = tor R t := by
  apply Units.ext
  rw [theta_val, ← map_inv, tor_val, tor_val]
  ext i j
  fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;>
    simp [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, Jmat, wt1, Matrix.diagonal, Fin.rev]

variable (R) in

def gw (j : ℤ) : Submodule R (Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) where
  carrier := {X | ∀ a b, wt a b ≠ j → X a b = 0}
  add_mem' := fun hX hY a b h => by simp [hX a b h, hY a b h]
  zero_mem' := fun _ _ _ => rfl
  smul_mem' := fun c X hX a b h => by simp [hX a b h]

variable (R) in

def unitaryThreeTorusLieAlgebra : Submodule R (Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) :=
  Submodule.span R {Matrix.diagonal fun i => ((wt1 i : ℤ) : R)}

variable {G : Type*} [Group G]

def gstar (c g : G) : G := c * g⁻¹ * c

def zmat (c g₁ : G) (r : G →* GG 3 R) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R :=
  (r g₁).mat - (r (gstar c g₁)).mat

structure Normalised (c g₁ : G) (r : G →* GG 3 R) : Prop where
  atc : (r c).mat = 1
  z12 : zmat c g₁ r 0 1 = 0
  z21 : zmat c g₁ r 1 0 = 0

def r0 (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* Rˣ) : G →* GG 3 R where
  toFun g := ⟨tor R (Ψ g), η g⟩
  map_one' := by ext1 <;> simp
  map_mul' g h := by
    ext1
    · rcases Int.units_eq_one_or (η g) with hg | hg <;> simp [hg, theta_tor]
    · simp

@[simp] theorem unitaryThreeReflection_right (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* Rˣ) (g : G) : (r0 η Ψ g).right = η g := rfl

@[simp] theorem unitaryThreeReflection_left (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* Rˣ) (g : G) : (r0 η Ψ g).left = tor R (Ψ g) := rfl

end Three

end UpperTheory.V.U3


[AFE_SRC_MARKS] All five original Marks fields
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/BaseRepGL3NormalisedOfPsiEqOne.lean#L1-L93
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.UnitaryThreeReflectionRight

namespace UpperTheory.V.U3

open Matrix

section Shape

variable {G : Type*} [Group G] {R : Type*} [CommRing R]

def HasMult {m : ℕ} (η : G →* ℤˣ) (r : G →* GG m R) : Prop := ∀ g, (r g).right = η g

def zeta (c g₁ : G) (r : G →* GG 3 R) : R := zmat c g₁ r 0 0

def MatIrr {m : ℕ} (H : Subgroup G) (a : G → Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) : Prop :=
  ∀ W : Submodule R (Fin m → R), (∀ g ∈ H, ∀ v ∈ W, a g *ᵥ v ∈ W) → W = ⊥ ∨ W = ⊤

def mats {m : ℕ} (r : G →* GG m R) (g : G) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R := (r g).mat

def TypeI (η : G →* ℤˣ) (r : G →* GG 3 R) : Prop := MatIrr η.ker (mats r)

def Upper (η : G →* ℤˣ) (r : G →* GG 3 R) : Prop := ∀ g ∈ η.ker, ∀ a b, wt a b < 0 → (r g).mat a b = 0

def Lower (η : G →* ℤˣ) (r : G →* GG 3 R) : Prop := ∀ g ∈ η.ker, ∀ a b, 0 < wt a b → (r g).mat a b = 0

def Block (η : G →* ℤˣ) (r : G →* GG 3 R) : Prop := ∀ g ∈ η.ker, ∀ a b, Odd (wt a b) → (r g).mat a b = 0

def corner (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) R) : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) R := X.submatrix ![0, 2] ![0, 2]

def TypeII (η : G →* ℤˣ) (r : G →* GG 3 R) : Prop :=
  Block η r ∧ MatIrr η.ker fun g => corner (r g).mat

def Wfun (r : G →* GG 3 R) (g : G) : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) R :=
  if (r g).right = 1 then corner (r g).mat else corner (r g).mat * Matrix.diagonal ![-1, 1]

def MatIso {m : ℕ} (H : Subgroup G) (a a' : G → Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) : Prop :=
  ∃ M : GL (Fin m) R, ∀ g ∈ H, (M : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) R) * a g = a' g * M

end Shape

section Deform

variable {G : Type*} [Group G] {C : Type*} [CommRing C] {m : ℕ}

def IsCoc (r : G →* GG m C) (γ : G → Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) C) : Prop :=
  ∀ g h, γ (g * h) = γ g + adRep r g (γ h)

def EntIn (I : Ideal C) (X : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) C) : Prop := ∀ a b, X a b ∈ I

def IsDeform (r : G →* GG m C) (γ : G → Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) C) (r' : G →* GG m C) : Prop :=
  ∀ g, (r' g).right = (r g).right ∧ (r' g).mat = (1 + γ g) * (r g).mat

def zvar (c g₁ : G) (r : G →* GG 3 C) (γ : G → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C :=
  γ g₁ * (r g₁).mat - γ (gstar c g₁) * (r (gstar c g₁)).mat

structure NormCoc (c g₁ : G) (r : G →* GG 3 C) (γ : G → Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C) : Prop where
  atc : γ c = 0
  z12 : zvar c g₁ r γ 0 1 = 0
  z21 : zvar c g₁ r γ 1 0 = 0

def cob (r : G →* GG m C) (X : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) C) (g : G) : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) C :=
  X - adRep r g X

def CongOne (I : Ideal C) (h : GL (Fin m) C) : Prop := EntIn I ((h : Matrix (Fin m) (Fin m) C) - 1)

end Deform

structure Marks {G : Type*} [Group G] {k : Type*} [CommRing k] (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* kˣ) (c g₁ : G) : Prop where
  csq : c * c = 1
  ηc : η c = -1
  Ψc : Ψ c = 1
  ηg : η g₁ = 1
  Ψg : IsUnit (((Ψ g₁ : kˣ) : k) ^ 2 - 1)

theorem hasSimilitudeFactor_diagonalRepr {G : Type*} [Group G] {R : Type*} [CommRing R] (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* Rˣ) :
    HasMult η (r0 η Ψ) := fun _ => rfl

theorem baseRepGL3_normalised_of_psi_eq_one {G : Type*} [Group G] {R : Type*} [CommRing R] (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* Rˣ)
    {c : G} (g₁ : G) (hc : Ψ c = 1) : Normalised c g₁ (r0 η Ψ) := by
  refine ⟨?_, ?_, ?_⟩
  · show Matrix.diagonal (fun i => ((Ψ c ^ wt1 i : Rˣ) : R)) = 1
    rw [hc]
    simp
  · show Matrix.diagonal _ 0 1 - Matrix.diagonal _ 0 1 = (0 : R)
    simp
  · show Matrix.diagonal _ 1 0 - Matrix.diagonal _ 1 0 = (0 : R)
    simp

example : Marks (G := ℤˣ × ℚˣ) (k := ℚ) (MonoidHom.fst _ _) (MonoidHom.snd _ _) (-1, 1)
    (1, Units.mk0 2 two_ne_zero) :=
  ⟨by simp, rfl, rfl, rfl, by norm_num⟩

end UpperTheory.V.U3


[AFE_SRC_LOCAL] Exact Red, CondT, TriIn and adic continuity
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeDeformations/AdjointDiagonalRepPreservesWeightPiece.lean#L1-L223
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.BaseRepGL3NormalisedOfPsiEqOne
import ReciprocityTheory.GaloisValuations.ContinuousConstSMulQuotient

noncomputable section

open Matrix IsLocalRing ReciprocityTheory.Gal UpperTheory.V.U3

namespace UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia

section Local

variable {O R : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] [CommRing R] [IsLocalRing R] [Algebra O R]
  {D : Type} [Group D]

def psi (Ψ : D →* Oˣ) (a : Fin 3) (g : D) : O := ((Ψ g ^ wt1 a : Oˣ) : O)

abbrev InHat (h : GL (Fin 3) R) : Prop := CongOne (maximalIdeal R) h

structure Red (η : D →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : D →* Oˣ) (r : D →* GG 3 R) : Prop where
  mult : HasMult η r
  red : ∀ g, EntIn (maximalIdeal R) ((r g).mat - ((r0 η Ψ g).mat).map (algebraMap O R))

def kerBar (η : D →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : D →* Oˣ) (I : Subgroup D) : Subgroup D :=
  I ⊓ η.ker ⊓ ((Units.map (residue O).toMonoidHom).comp Ψ).ker

def CondU (η : D →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : D →* Oˣ) (I : Subgroup D) (r : D →* GG 3 R) : Prop :=
  ∀ s ∈ kerBar η Ψ I, r s = 1

structure TriIn (η : D →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : D →* Oˣ) (I : Subgroup D) {A : Type} [CommRing A] [Algebra O A]
    (r : D →* GG 3 A) (h : GL (Fin 3) A) : Prop where
  tri : Upper η (conjBy h⁻¹ r)
  ine : ∀ s ∈ I, η s = 1 → ∀ a, (conjBy h⁻¹ r s).mat a a = algebraMap O A (psi Ψ a s)

structure DiagIn (η : D →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : D →* Oˣ) (I : Subgroup D) {A : Type} [CommRing A] [Algebra O A]
    (r : D →* GG 3 A) (h : GL (Fin 3) A) : Prop extends TriIn η Ψ I r h where
  low : Lower η (conjBy h⁻¹ r)

def CondT (η : D →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : D →* Oˣ) (I : Subgroup D) (r : D →* GG 3 R) : Prop :=
  ∃ h : GL (Fin 3) R, InHat h ∧ TriIn η Ψ I r h

def CondD (η : D →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : D →* Oˣ) (I : Subgroup D) (r : D →* GG 3 R) : Prop :=
  ∃ h : GL (Fin 3) R, InHat h ∧ DiagIn η Ψ I r h

def AdicCont [TopologicalSpace D] {A : Type} [CommRing A] (J : Ideal A) (r : D →* GG 3 A) : Prop :=
  ∀ n : ℕ, IsLocallyConstant fun g => (r g).mat.map (Ideal.Quotient.mk (J ^ n))

def GrMat {A : Type} [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] (𝒜 : ℤ → Submodule O A) (X : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) A) : Prop :=
  ∀ a b, X a b ∈ 𝒜 (wt a b)

end Local

section Residual

variable {k : Type} [CommRing k] {D : Type} [Group D]

variable (k) in

def Gb (j : ℤ) : Type := gw k j

instance (j : ℤ) : AddCommGroup (Gb k j) := inferInstanceAs (AddCommGroup (gw k j))
instance (j : ℤ) : Module k (Gb k j) := inferInstanceAs (Module k (gw k j))
instance (j : ℤ) : TopologicalSpace (Gb k j) := ⊥
instance (j : ℤ) : DiscreteTopology (Gb k j) := ⟨rfl⟩
instance (j : ℤ) : ContinuousConstSMul k (Gb k j) := ⟨fun _ => continuous_of_discreteTopology⟩

def Gb.val {j : ℤ} (X : Gb k j) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) k := Subtype.val (p := fun X => X ∈ gw k j) X

theorem adjoint_diagonal_rep_preserves_weight_piece (η : D →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : D →* kˣ) (j : ℤ) (g : D) :
    gw k j ≤ (gw k j).comap (adRep (r0 η Ψ) g) := by
  intro X hX a b hab
  have hJ : ∀ i j : Fin 3, (Jmat k 3 * Xᵀ * Jmat k 3) i j = X j.rev i.rev := by
    intro i j
    fin_cases i <;> fin_cases j <;> simp [Matrix.mul_apply, Fin.sum_univ_three, Jmat, Fin.rev]
  have hw : wt b.rev a.rev = wt a b := by
    fin_cases a <;> fin_cases b <;> simp [wt, wt1, Fin.rev]
  change ((r0 η Ψ g).mat * (if (r0 η Ψ g).right = 1 then X else thetaLie X) * (((r0 η Ψ g).left⁻¹ : GL (Fin 3) k) : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) k)) a b = 0
  rw [GG.mat, unitaryThreeReflection_left, ← map_inv, tor_val, tor_val]
  simp only [Matrix.mul_diagonal, Matrix.diagonal_mul]
  split_ifs
  · rw [hX a b hab]
    simp
  · unfold thetaLie
    rw [Matrix.neg_apply, hJ, hX b.rev a.rev (by rw [hw]; exact hab)]
    simp

def gRep (η : D →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : D →* kˣ) (j : ℤ) : Representation k D (Gb k j) :=
  (adRep (r0 η Ψ)).subrepresentation (gw k j) (adjoint_diagonal_rep_preserves_weight_piece η Ψ j)

theorem gRep_val (η : D →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : D →* kˣ) (j : ℤ) (g : D) (X : Gb k j) :
    (gRep η Ψ j g X).val = adRep (r0 η Ψ) g X.val := rfl

end Residual

structure Frame (O : Type) [CommRing O] (G Γ : Type) [Group G] [Group Γ] (ι : Type) where
  π : G →* Γ
  η : Γ →* ℤˣ
  Ψ : Γ →* Oˣ
  c : Γ
  D : ι → Subgroup G
  I : ι → Subgroup G
  vl : ι
  Sb : Set ι

namespace Frame

variable {O : Type} [CommRing O] [IsLocalRing O] {G Γ : Type} [Group G] [Group Γ] {ι : Type} (F : Frame O G Γ ι)

def loc (v : ι) : F.D v →* Γ := F.π.comp (F.D v).subtype

abbrev ηv (v : ι) : F.D v →* ℤˣ := F.η.comp (F.loc v)

abbrev Ψv (v : ι) : F.D v →* Oˣ := F.Ψ.comp (F.loc v)

abbrev Iv (v : ι) : Subgroup (F.D v) := (F.I v).subgroupOf (F.D v)

abbrev Nv (v : ι) : Subgroup (F.D v) := kerBar (F.ηv v) (F.Ψv v) (F.Iv v)

def Tb : Set ι := insert F.vl F.Sb

def Ψbar : Γ →* (ResidueField O)ˣ := (Units.map (residue O).toMonoidHom).comp F.Ψ

def rz (A : Type) [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] : Γ →* GG 3 A := (GG.map (algebraMap O A)).comp (r0 F.η F.Ψ)

section Cond

variable {R : Type} [CommRing R] [IsLocalRing R] [Algebra O R]

def LocAt (v : ι) (r : F.D v →* GG 3 R) : Prop :=
  (v = F.vl → CondT (F.ηv v) (F.Ψv v) (F.Iv v) r) ∧ (v ∈ F.Sb → CondD (F.ηv v) (F.Ψv v) (F.Iv v) r) ∧
    (v ∉ F.Tb → CondU (F.ηv v) (F.Ψv v) (F.Iv v) r)

structure Cond (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) : Prop extends Red F.η F.Ψ r where
  loc : ∀ v, F.LocAt v (r.comp (F.loc v))

structure CondN (g₁ : Γ) (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) : Prop extends F.Cond r where
  norm : Normalised F.c g₁ r

structure IsPt [TopologicalSpace Γ] (g₁ : Γ) (r : Γ →* GG 3 R) : Prop extends F.CondN g₁ r where
  cont : AdicCont (maximalIdeal R) r

end Cond

def Marked (g₁ : Γ) : Prop := F.η g₁ = 1 ∧ IsUnit ((F.Ψ g₁ : O) ^ 2 - 1)

variable [TopologicalSpace G] [IsTopologicalGroup G] [TopologicalSpace Γ]

structure Ok : Prop where
  two : IsUnit (2 : O)
  π_cont : Continuous F.π
  η_cont : IsLocallyConstant F.η
  Ψ_cont : ∀ n : ℕ, IsLocallyConstant fun g => Ideal.Quotient.mk (maximalIdeal O ^ n) (F.Ψ g : O)
  csq : F.c * F.c = 1
  ηc : F.η F.c = -1
  Ψc : F.Ψ F.c = 1
  vl_notMem : F.vl ∉ F.Sb
  D_closed : ∀ v, IsClosed (F.D v : Set G)
  I_closed : ∀ v, IsClosed (F.I v : Set G)
  I_le : ∀ v, F.I v ≤ F.D v
  I_normal : ∀ v, (F.Iv v).Normal

  frob : ∀ v, ∃ φ : F.D v, (F.Iv v ⊔ Subgroup.zpowers φ).topologicalClosure = ⊤ ∧
    ∀ n : ℕ, 0 < n → ∃ d : F.D v →* Multiplicative (ZMod n), IsLocallyConstant d ∧ (∀ s ∈ F.Iv v, d s = 1) ∧
      d φ = Multiplicative.ofAdd 1
  η_I : ∀ v ∈ F.Tb, ∀ s ∈ F.Iv v, F.ηv v s = 1
  x : ∀ v ∈ F.Tb, ∃ x : F.D v, F.ηv v x = 1 ∧ IsUnit ((F.Ψv v x : O) ^ 2 - 1)
  teich : ∀ v ∉ F.Tb, ∀ s ∈ F.Nv v, F.Ψv v s = 1

abbrev gbar (j : ℤ) : Representation (ResidueField O) Γ (Gb (ResidueField O) j) := gRep F.η F.Ψbar j

abbrev gloc (j : ℤ) (v : ι) : Representation (ResidueField O) (F.D v) (Gb (ResidueField O) j) :=
  (F.gbar j).comp (F.loc v)

def ZL (j : ℤ) (v : ι) : Submodule (ResidueField O) (F.D v → Gb (ResidueField O) j) where
  carrier := {y | y ∈ Z1 (F.gloc j v) ∧ (v ≠ F.vl → ∀ s ∈ F.Nv v, y s = 0) ∧
    (v = F.vl → j < 0 → y ∈ B1 (F.gloc j v)) ∧ (v = F.vl → j = 0 → ∀ s ∈ F.Iv v, y s = 0)}
  add_mem' := fun {y y'} h h' => ⟨add_mem h.1 h'.1, fun e s hs => by simp [h.2.1 e s hs, h'.2.1 e s hs],
    fun e e' => add_mem (h.2.2.1 e e') (h'.2.2.1 e e'), fun e e' s hs => by simp [h.2.2.2 e e' s hs, h'.2.2.2 e e' s hs]⟩
  zero_mem' := ⟨zero_mem _, fun _ _ _ => rfl, fun _ _ => zero_mem _, fun _ _ _ _ => rfl⟩
  smul_mem' := fun a y h => ⟨Submodule.smul_mem _ a h.1, fun e s hs => by simp [h.2.1 e s hs],
    fun e e' => Submodule.smul_mem _ a (h.2.2.1 e e'), fun e e' s hs => by simp [h.2.2.2 e e' s hs]⟩

def res1 (j : ℤ) (v : ι) : (Γ → Gb (ResidueField O) j) →ₗ[ResidueField O] (F.D v → Gb (ResidueField O) j) :=
  LinearMap.funLeft _ _ (F.loc v)

def res2 (j : ℤ) (v : ι) :
    (Γ × Γ → Gb (ResidueField O) j) →ₗ[ResidueField O] (F.D v × F.D v → Gb (ResidueField O) j) :=
  LinearMap.funLeft _ _ (Prod.map (F.loc v) (F.loc v))

def selmerCocycles (j : ℤ) : Submodule (ResidueField O) (Γ → Gb (ResidueField O) j) :=
  Z1 (F.gbar j) ⊓ ⨅ v, (F.ZL j v).comap (F.res1 j v)

abbrev selmerCohomologyQuotient (j : ℤ) : Type := F.selmerCocycles j ⧸ (B1 (F.gbar j)).comap (F.selmerCocycles j).subtype

def framedTwoCocycles (j : ℤ) : Submodule (ResidueField O)
    ((Γ × Γ → Gb (ResidueField O) j) × ∀ v, F.D v → Gb (ResidueField O) j) :=
  (Z2 (F.gbar j)).comap (LinearMap.fst _ _ _) ⊓
    ⨅ v, ((contMaps _ _ (F.D v)).comap ((LinearMap.proj v).comp (LinearMap.snd _ _ _)) ⊓
      LinearMap.eqLocus ((d1 (F.gloc j v)).comp ((LinearMap.proj v).comp (LinearMap.snd _ _ _)))
        ((F.res2 j v).comp (LinearMap.fst _ _ _)))

def framedTwoCoboundaries (j : ℤ) : Submodule (ResidueField O)
    ((Γ × Γ → Gb (ResidueField O) j) × ∀ v, F.D v → Gb (ResidueField O) j) :=
  (contMaps _ _ Γ).map ((d1 (F.gbar j)).prod (LinearMap.pi fun v => F.res1 j v)) ⊔
    (Submodule.pi Set.univ fun v => F.ZL j v).map (LinearMap.inr _ _ _)

abbrev Ob (j : ℤ) : Type := F.framedTwoCocycles j ⧸ (F.framedTwoCoboundaries j).comap (F.framedTwoCocycles j).subtype

def defect (j : ℤ) : ℤ := if j = -1 then -1 else if j = 1 ∨ j = 2 then 1 else 0

def Count : Prop := ∀ j : ℤ, Module.Finite (ResidueField O) (F.selmerCohomologyQuotient j) ∧ Module.Finite (ResidueField O) (F.Ob j) ∧
  (Module.finrank (ResidueField O) (F.selmerCohomologyQuotient j) : ℤ) - Module.finrank (ResidueField O) (F.Ob j) = defect j

structure LocVan : Prop where
  h2 : ∀ v ∈ F.Tb, ∀ j ≠ 0, Subsingleton (H2 (F.gloc j v))
  h1 : ∀ v ∈ F.Sb, ∀ j ≠ 0, Subsingleton (H1 (F.gloc j v))

def Xpp : Prop := Subsingleton (F.selmerCohomologyQuotient (-2))

end Frame

end UpperTheory.V.UnitaryThreeInertia

end


[AFE_SRC_STAR] Literal polarization matrix identity
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/UnitaryThreeGroups/MatGstar.lean#L1-L22
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.BaseRepGL3NormalisedOfPsiEqOne

open Matrix UpperTheory.V.U3

theorem UpperTheory.V.U3.mat_gstar :
    ∀ {G R : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] {m : ℕ} (r : G →* GG m R) (c g : G), (r c).mat = 1 → (r c).right = -1 → (r g).right = 1 →
    (r (gstar c g)).mat = Jmat R m * (r g).matᵀ * Jmat R m ∧ (r (gstar c g)).right = 1 := by
  intro G R _ _ m r c g h1 h2 h3
  have hc : r c = SemidirectProduct.mk 1 (-1) := by
    apply SemidirectProduct.ext
    · exact Units.ext (by simpa [GG.mat] using h1)
    · exact h2
  have hg : r g = SemidirectProduct.mk (r g).left 1 := SemidirectProduct.ext rfl h3
  have hs : r (gstar c g) = r c * (r g)⁻¹ * r c := by
    simp only [gstar, map_mul, map_inv]
  rw [hs, hc, hg]
  simp only [GG.mat, SemidirectProduct.mul_left, SemidirectProduct.mul_right, SemidirectProduct.inv_left,
    SemidirectProduct.inv_right, inv_one, map_one, mul_one, one_mul, MulAut.one_apply, map_inv,
    sgnAct_neg_one, theta_inv_val]
  refine ⟨?_, ?_⟩
  · simp [map_one, theta_inv_val]
  · norm_num


[AFE_SRC_CD] Exact IsCD coefficient class
https://github.com/CocoSgt/riemann-hypothesis-lean/blob/7cbf7a0c32fca1badf05931879a37c3f8a2b03b4/src/Development/AutomorphicArithmetic/AutomorphicVarieties/CurveDVRSpecialization/IsUnitOrIsUnitOfAddOne.lean#L1-L129
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.UnitaryThreeGroups.BaseRepGL3NormalisedOfPsiEqOne
import Development.AutomorphicArithmetic.AutomorphicVarieties.CommutativeAlgebra.ResMapBijective

namespace UpperTheory.V.Pr

open Matrix Polynomial IsLocalRing UpperTheory.V.U3 UpperTheory.V.Alg

abbrev gm {G R : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] {n : ℕ} (r : G →* GL (Fin n) R) (g : G) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) R := r g

noncomputable def chPoly {G R ι : Type} [Group G] [CommRing R] [Fintype ι] (χ : ι → G →* Rˣ) (g : G) : R[X] :=
  ∏ i, (X - C ((χ i g : Rˣ) : R))

def CharPerm : Prop :=
  ∀ (G k : Type) [Group G] [Field k] (n : ℕ) (χ χ' : Fin n → G →* kˣ), (∀ g, chPoly χ g = chPoly χ' g) →
    ∃ σ : Equiv.Perm (Fin n), ∀ i, χ' i = χ (σ i)

def NotIrrOfChar : Prop :=
  ∀ (G κ : Type) [Group G] [Field κ] (n : ℕ), 2 ≤ n → ∀ (r : G →* GL (Fin n) κ) (χ : Fin n → G →* κˣ),
    (∀ g, (gm r g).charpoly = chPoly χ g) → ¬ MatIrr ⊤ (gm r)

def CliffPrime : Prop :=
  ∀ (G κ : Type) [Group G] [Field κ] [IsAlgClosed κ] (n : ℕ), n.Prime → ∀ (r : G →* GL (Fin n) κ), MatIrr ⊤ (gm r) →
    ∀ (U : Subgroup G) [U.Normal], MatIrr U (gm r) ∨ (∀ u ∈ U, ∃ a : κ, gm r u = a • (1 : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) κ)) ∨
      ∃ (b : Module.Basis (Fin n) κ (Fin n → κ)) (la : Fin n → U →* κˣ) (π : G →* Equiv.Perm (Fin n)),
        Function.Injective la ∧ (∀ i (u : U), gm r u *ᵥ b i = ((la i u : κˣ) : κ) • b i) ∧
        (∀ g i, ∃ a : κ, gm r g *ᵥ b i = a • b (π g i)) ∧ ∀ i j, ∃ g, π g i = j

def RedTo {G κ k B : Type} [Group G] [Field κ] [Field k] [CommRing B] {n : ℕ} (r : G →* GL (Fin n) κ)
    (j : B →+* κ) (φ : B →+* k) (χ : Fin n → G →* kˣ) : Prop :=
  ∀ g, ∃ p : B[X], p.map j = (gm r g).charpoly ∧ p.map φ = chPoly χ g

def IrrNormal : Prop :=
  ∀ (G κ k B : Type) [Group G] [Field κ] [IsAlgClosed κ] [Field k] [CommRing B] (n : ℕ), n.Prime →
    ∀ (r : G →* GL (Fin n) κ), MatIrr ⊤ (gm r) → ∀ (j : B →+* κ) (φ : B →+* k) (χ : Fin n → G →* kˣ),
    Function.Injective j → RedTo r j φ χ → ∀ (D : Type) [Group D] (ι : D →* G) (ψ : Fin n → D →* κˣ),
    (∀ g, (gm r (ι g)).charpoly = chPoly ψ g) → ∀ (U : Subgroup G) [U.Normal],
    (∀ a b, a ≠ b → ∃ g, ι g ∈ U ∧ ψ a g ≠ ψ b g) → (∃ a b g, χ a (ι g) ≠ χ b (ι g)) → MatIrr U (gm r)

def LinClosed {G κ : Type} [TopologicalSpace G] [Field κ] {n : ℕ} (a : G → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) κ) : Prop :=
  ∀ f : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) κ →ₗ[κ] κ, IsClosed {g | f (a g) = 0}

def LinClosedOfAdic : Prop :=
  ∀ (G A κ : Type) [TopologicalSpace G] [CommRing A] [IsDomain A] [IsNoetherianRing A] [IsLocalRing A] [Field κ]
    (j : A →+* κ), Function.Injective j → ∀ (n : ℕ) (a : G → Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) A),
    (∀ s t, AdicCont (maximalIdeal A) fun g => a g s t) → LinClosed fun g => (a g).map j

def IrrProcyclic : Prop :=
  ∀ (G κ : Type) [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [IsTopologicalGroup G] [CompactSpace G] [Field κ] [IsAlgClosed κ]
    (n : ℕ), n.Prime → ∀ (r : G →* GL (Fin n) κ), LinClosed (gm r) →
    (∀ U : Subgroup G, IsOpen (U : Set G) → MatIrr U (gm r)) → ∀ (Z : Subgroup G) [Z.Normal] (g : G),
    (Z ⊔ Subgroup.zpowers g).topologicalClosure = ⊤ → MatIrr Z (gm r)

def StrongIrr {G κ : Type} [Group G] [Field κ] {n : ℕ} (r : G →* GL (Fin n) κ) : Prop :=
  ∀ (W : Submodule κ (Fin n → κ)) (S : Subgroup G), S.FiniteIndex → (∀ g, g ∈ S ↔ ∀ v ∈ W, gm r g *ᵥ v ∈ W) →
    W = ⊥ ∨ W = ⊤

def StrongIrrOfOpen : Prop :=
  ∀ (G κ : Type) [Group G] [TopologicalSpace G] [IsTopologicalGroup G] [Field κ] (n : ℕ) (r : G →* GL (Fin n) κ),
    LinClosed (gm r) → (∀ U : Subgroup G, IsOpen (U : Set G) → MatIrr U (gm r)) → StrongIrr r

def FreeRatios {κ ι : Type} [Field κ] (α : ι → κˣ) : Prop := ∀ i j, i ≠ j → ∀ m : ℕ, 0 < m → α i ^ m ≠ α j ^ m

def CommonFree : Prop :=
  ∀ (H A ι : Type) [Group H] [CommGroup A] [Finite ι] (χ : ι → H →* A) (N : ℕ), 0 < N →
    (∀ i h (m : ℕ), 0 < m → χ i h ^ m = 1 → χ i h ^ N = 1) → (∀ i, ∃ h, χ i h ^ N ≠ 1) →
    ∃ h, ∀ i (m : ℕ), 0 < m → χ i h ^ m ≠ 1

def ExpSums : Prop :=
  ∀ (κ ι σ : Type) [Field κ] [Fintype ι] [Finite σ] (α : σ → ι → κˣ) (c : σ → ι → κ), (∀ s, FreeRatios (α s)) →
    (∀ s, ∃ i, c s i ≠ 0) → ∃ m : ℕ, ∀ s, ∑ i, c s i * ((α s i : κˣ) : κ) ^ m ≠ 0

def Full {κ : Type} [Field κ] {n : ℕ} (b : GL (Fin n) κ) : Prop :=
  ∀ i j, (b : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) κ) i j ≠ 0 ∧ ((b⁻¹ : GL (Fin n) κ) : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) κ) i j ≠ 0

def FullEx : Prop :=
  ∀ (G κ : Type) [Group G] [Field κ] (n : ℕ) (r : G →* GL (Fin n) κ), StrongIrr r →
    ∀ (u : G) (α : Fin n → κˣ), FreeRatios α → gm r u = diagonal (fun i => ((α i : κˣ) : κ)) → ∃ b, Full (r b)

def SepOfMinors : Prop :=
  ∀ (κ : Type) [Field κ] (n : ℕ), n ≤ 3 → ∀ (y : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) κ) (α : Fin n → κˣ), FreeRatios α →
    (∀ S : Finset (Fin n), (y.submatrix (Subtype.val : S → Fin n) Subtype.val).det ≠ 0) →
    ∃ m : ℕ, (y * diagonal (fun i => ((α i : κˣ) : κ)) ^ m).charpoly.Separable

def RegCoset : Prop :=
  ∀ (G κ : Type) [Group G] [Field κ] (n : ℕ), n ≤ 3 → ∀ (r : G →* GL (Fin n) κ), StrongIrr r →
    ∀ (u : G) (α : Fin n → κˣ), FreeRatios α → (gm r u).charpoly = ∏ i, (X - C ((α i : κˣ) : κ)) →
    ∀ y : GL (Fin n) κ, ∃ h, ((y : Matrix (Fin n) (Fin n) κ) * gm r h).charpoly.Separable

def RegCosetE : Prop :=
  ∀ (G κ : Type) [Group G] [Field κ] (σ : G →* GL (Fin 2) κ) (ψ₀ : G →* κˣ), StrongIrr σ →
    ∀ (u : G) (α : Fin 2 → κˣ), FreeRatios ![α 0, α 1, ψ₀ u] → (gm σ u).charpoly = ∏ i, (X - C ((α i : κˣ) : κ)) →
    ∀ (y : GL (Fin 2) κ) (z : κˣ), ∃ h,
      (((y : Matrix (Fin 2) (Fin 2) κ) * gm σ h).charpoly * (X - C ((z * ψ₀ h : κˣ) : κ))).Separable

def ConstOfAlg : Prop :=
  ∀ (k A : Type) [Field k] [CommRing A] [IsDomain A] [IsLocalRing A] [Algebra k A] (x : A) (c : k),
    IsAlgebraic k x → x - algebraMap k A c ∈ maximalIdeal A → x = algebraMap k A c

def ConstOfChar : Prop :=
  ∀ (G k A : Type) [Group G] [Field k] [Finite k] [CommRing A] [IsDomain A] [IsLocalRing A] [Algebra k A],
    (2 : k) ≠ 0 → ∀ (η : G →* ℤˣ) (Ψ : G →* kˣ) (c g₁ : G), Marks η Ψ c g₁ → ∀ r : G →* GG 3 A, HasMult η r →
    (∀ g, EntIn (maximalIdeal A) ((r g).mat - ((r0 η Ψ g).mat).map (algebraMap k A))) → Normalised c g₁ r →
    ∀ U : Subgroup G, U.FiniteIndex →
    (∀ g ∈ U, η g = 1 → (r g).mat.charpoly = (((r0 η Ψ g).mat).map (algebraMap k A)).charpoly) →
    (Upper η r ∨ Lower η r) ∧ ∀ g a, (r g).mat a a = algebraMap k A ((r0 η Ψ g).mat a a)

class IsCD (O A : Type) [CommRing O] [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] : Prop extends IsDomain A, IsDiscreteValuationRing A where
  complete : IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal A) A
  isLocalHom : IsLocalHom (algebraMap O A)
  fin : Finite (ResidueField A)

attribute [instance] IsCD.complete IsCD.isLocalHom IsCD.fin

def FinCrit : Prop :=
  ∀ (O R : Type) [CommRing O] [IsDomain O] [IsDiscreteValuationRing O] [IsAdicComplete (maximalIdeal O) O]
    [Finite (ResidueField O)] [CommRing R] [Algebra O R] [IsCO O R] (W : WPres O ℤ R),
    (∀ i, W.z i ∈ maximalIdeal R) →
    (∀ (A : Type) [CommRing A] [Algebra O A] [IsCD O A] (f : R →ₐ[O] A), IsLocalHom f →
      (∀ a ∈ maximalIdeal O, algebraMap O A a = 0) →
      (∀ i, W.wt i = 0 → f (W.z i) = 0) ∧
        ((∀ i, W.wt i < 0 → f (W.z i) = 0) ∨ ∀ i, 0 < W.wt i → f (W.z i) = 0)) →
    Module.Finite O (W.part 0)

example (l : ℕ) [Fact l.Prime] : IsCD ℤ_[l] ℤ_[l] where
  complete := inferInstance
  isLocalHom := ⟨fun _ h => h⟩
  fin := Finite.of_equiv _ (PadicInt.residueField (p := l)).symm.toEquiv

end UpperTheory.V.Pr
